===== .changeset/r28-finding-outcomes.md =====
---
"@olympus-ai/core": minor
"@olympus-ai/vault": minor
"@olympus-ai/integrity": minor
"@olympus-ai/adapters": minor
"@olympus-ai/api": minor
"@olympus-ai/compiler": minor
"olympus-ai": minor
---

Finding outcomes, derived by the runtime (R28). The reviewer and the security seat now end with located findings (`category`, `path`, `line`), read strictly and recorded beside the seat's result; an unlocated finding is refused with its cause, an unreadable result is recorded as unreadable, and neither ever changes the task's status. A critic's finding names one of the draft's files. Both coverage adapters report each changed file's executed and unexecuted changed lines, and the evidence records them beside `coverageDelta`. A finding's outcome is a write-once runtime record: `resolved` by a covered merged change or by a changed and locked draft, or `dismissed` by a person with a cause and no text (`GET /findings`, `POST /findings/:id/dismiss`, `olympus findings`, `olympus dismiss-finding`); a finding with no record is open. R2 reports resolved, dismissed, and open per seat kind and per reviewer family, and says a code finding's resolved rate is unmeasured until a run records line coverage, which needs M3's coverage channel.

===== examples/roles/reviewer.md =====
diff --git a/examples/roles/reviewer.md b/examples/roles/reviewer.md
index 89c1bff..e7d428d 100644
--- a/examples/roles/reviewer.md
+++ b/examples/roles/reviewer.md
@@ -26,8 +26,27 @@ the code and the evidence.
 
 ## What to say
 
-Your final message is your review. It is recorded beside the evidence for the
-person who approves the change. For each problem, name the file and what is
-wrong with it. If you found nothing wrong, say so and say what you checked. If
-you could not review something, say what and why. Do not soften a finding and
-do not invent one.
+Your final message is your result, and nothing else: one JSON object,
+
+```
+{"findings": [{"category": "...", "path": "...", "line": 1, "note": "..."}]}
+```
+
+One finding per problem. `category` is the question it answers:
+
+- `spec-mismatch`: the change does not do what the spec asks.
+- `beyond-spec`: the change does something the spec does not ask.
+- `untested-behavior`: the acceptance tests do not exercise the behavior the
+  spec describes, or the evidence does not show them failing before the
+  change and passing after it.
+- `check-bypassed`: a check passes without the behavior being there.
+- `uncovered-breakage`: the change breaks something no check covers.
+
+`path` is a file in the working directory, relative to it, and `line` a line
+in that file. `note` says what is wrong there; it is read by the person who
+approves the change, never by the runtime. If you found nothing wrong, return
+`{"findings": []}`. A finding that names no file and line the working
+directory holds is not recorded, and a message that is not this object is
+recorded as unreadable, never as nothing found. Your result decides nothing:
+the change is neither passed nor failed by it. Do not soften a finding and do
+not invent one.

 ## What to say
 
-Your final message is your review. It is recorded beside the evidence for the
-person who approves the change; it does not decide anything. Every finding

-not soften a finding and do not invent one.
+Your final message is your result, and nothing else: one JSON object,
+
+```
+{"findings": [{"category": "...", "path": "...", "line": 1, "note": "..."}]}
+```
+
+One finding per problem. `category` is the question it answers:
+
+- `unchecked-input`: input reaches a query, a command, a path, a template, or
+  a deserializer without being checked.
+- `exposed-secret`: a secret is written into the code, a log, an error, or a
+  response.
+- `unenforced-authorization`: an authorization rule is not enforced, or is
+  enforced on one path and not another.
+- `weakened-check`: a check is weakened, skipped, or suppressed so that it
+  passes.
+- `reachable-before-authentication`: an entry point, route, or code path is
+  reachable before authentication.
+- `unnamed-surface`: an administrative, debug, or legacy surface the spec does
+  not name.
+- `unsafe-default`: a default configuration turns a control off.
+- `weakening-combination`: settings that each look safe weaken a control
+  together.
+
+`path` is a file in the working directory, relative to it, and `line` a line
+in that file. `note` says what is wrong there and what an attacker could do
+with it; it is read by a person, never by the runtime. If you found nothing,
+return `{"findings": []}`. A finding that names no file and line the working
+directory holds is not recorded, and a message that is not this object is
+recorded as unreadable, never as nothing found. Your result decides nothing.
+Do not soften a finding and do not invent one.

 /** A report for a large repository runs to tens of megabytes; this bounds what one may cost to read. */
 export const REPORT_CAP = 256 * 1024 * 1024;
@@ -112,70 +113,80 @@ function executableLines(text: string, fileName: string): Set<number> {
 export class IstanbulCoverageAdapter implements CoverageAdapter {
   readonly #options: IstanbulCoverageOptions;
 
   constructor(options: IstanbulCoverageOptions) {
     this.#options = options;
   }
 
   /** Every file entry in the report under `sourceRoot`, keyed by its path relative to it. */
   async #readReport(): Promise<Map<string, LineHits>> {
     const { report, sourceRoot } = this.#options;
     if (!(await exists(report))) refuse('missing-report', `no coverage report at ${report}`);
     let value: unknown;
     try {
       value = JSON.parse(await readRegularFile(report, REPORT_CAP));
     } catch (error) {
       if (error instanceof SyntaxError) refuse('malformed-report', `${report} is not JSON: ${error.message}`);
       throw error;
     }
     if (!isRecord(value)) refuse('malformed-report', `${report} is not a JSON object keyed by file`);
     // Both sides are normalised the same way, and by separator rather than by host: a report
     // written on Windows names its files with backslashes wherever it is read, and `toPosix`
     // converts the separator of the host doing the reading, which on Linux is already `/`.
     const prefix = `${sourceRoot.replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/\/+$/, '')}/`;
     const files = new Map<string, LineHits>();
     const keys = Object.keys(value);
     for (const key of keys) {
       const normal = key.replaceAll('\\', '/');
       if (normal.startsWith(prefix)) files.set(normal.slice(prefix.length), lineHits(key, value[key]));
     }
     if (keys.length > 0 && files.size === 0) {
       refuse('malformed-report', `${report} names ${String(keys.length)} files and none under ${sourceRoot}, so it is not a report on this tree`);
     }
     return files;
   }
 
   async changedLineCoverage(base: string, head: string): Promise<number> {
+    return coverageFraction(await this.changedLineReport(base, head));
+  }
+
+  async changedLineReport(base: string, head: string): Promise<ChangedLineCoverage[]> {
     const report = await this.#readReport();
     const tests = new Set((await this.#options.tests.enumerateSuites(head)).map((file) => toPosix(file)));
     const headPosix = toPosix(head).replace(/\/+$/, '');
     const isSource = (path: string): boolean =>
       SOURCE.test(path) && !DECLARATION.test(path) && !isConfigFile(path) && !tests.has(`${headPosix}/${path}`);
 
     const changes = await diffTrees(base, head, isSource, {
       skipDirectory: (name) => isDependencyTree(name) || VCS_DIRECTORIES.has(name),
     });
-    let total = 0;
-    let covered = 0;
+    const out: ChangedLineCoverage[] = [];
     for (const { path, change } of changes) {
       if (change === 'removed') continue;
       const after = await readRegularFile(join(head, path), SOURCE_FILE_CAP);
       const before = change === 'added' ? '' : await readRegularFile(join(base, path), SOURCE_FILE_CAP);
       const changed = changedLines(change === 'added' ? [] : splitLines(before), splitLines(after));
       const hits = report.get(path);
       // The report may add obligations and never remove one. Taking the denominator from the
       // report alone lets the file being judged decide what counts: an entry with an empty
       // statement map, or one an `istanbul ignore` comment thinned out, would leave nothing to
       // cover and read as covered in full. The repository's own exclusions are not honoured
       // (D-P8-09), and an ignore comment is one of them.
       const executable = executableLines(after, path);
       for (const line of hits?.keys() ?? []) executable.add(line);
-      for (const line of changed) {
-        if (!executable.has(line)) continue;
-        total++;
-        if ((hits?.get(line) ?? 0) > 0) covered++;
-      }
+      const lines = [...changed].filter((line) => executable.has(line)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
+      if (lines.length > 0) out.push({ path, executed: lines.filter((line) => (hits?.get(line) ?? 0) > 0), unexecuted: lines.filter((line) => (hits?.get(line) ?? 0) === 0) });
     }
-    // No executable line changed, so no changed line is uncovered.
-    return total === 0 ? 1 : covered / total;
+    return out.sort((a, b) => (a.path < b.path ? -1 : a.path > b.path ? 1 : 0));
+  }
+}
+
+/** The changed-line fraction from the per-line report: executed over executable, and 1 where no executable line changed, so no changed line is uncovered. */
+export function coverageFraction(files: readonly ChangedLineCoverage[]): number {
+  let total = 0;
+  let covered = 0;
+  for (const f of files) {
+    total += f.executed.length + f.unexecuted.length;
+    covered += f.executed.length;
   }
+  return total === 0 ? 1 : covered / total;
 }

   readonly #options: CoveragePyOptions;
 
   constructor(options: CoveragePyOptions) {
     this.#options = options;
   }
 
   async #readReport(): Promise<Map<string, FileLines>> {
     const { report, sourceRoot } = this.#options;
     if (!(await exists(report))) refuse('missing-report', `no coverage report at ${report}`);
     let value: unknown;
     try {
       value = JSON.parse(await readRegularFile(report, REPORT_CAP));
     } catch (error) {
       if (error instanceof SyntaxError) refuse('malformed-report', `${report} is not JSON: ${error.message}`);
       throw error;
     }
     if (!isRecord(value) || !isRecord(value.files)) refuse('malformed-report', `${report} is not a coverage.py JSON report: it has no files object`);
     const prefix = `${sourceRoot.replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/\/+$/u, '')}/`;
     const out = new Map<string, FileLines>();
     const keys = Object.keys(value.files);
     for (const key of keys) {
       const entry = value.files[key];
       if (!isRecord(entry)) refuse('malformed-report', `the entry for ${key} is not an object`);
       const executed = lines(key, 'executed_lines', entry.executed_lines);
       const missing = lines(key, 'missing_lines', entry.missing_lines);
       const normal = key.replaceAll('\\', '/');
       // coverage.py names a file under the directory it ran in relative to it, and any other absolutely.
       const path = normal.startsWith(prefix) ? normal.slice(prefix.length) : normal.startsWith('/') ? null : normal.replace(/^\.\//u, '');
       if (path !== null) out.set(path, { executed: new Set(executed), measured: new Set([...executed, ...missing]) });
     }
     if (keys.length > 0 && out.size === 0) refuse('malformed-report', `${report} names ${String(keys.length)} files and none under ${sourceRoot}, so it is not a report on this tree`);
     return out;
   }

+    return coverageFraction(await this.changedLineReport(base, head));
+  }
+

===== packages/api/setup/roles/reviewer.md =====
diff --git a/packages/api/setup/roles/reviewer.md b/packages/api/setup/roles/reviewer.md
index 89c1bff..e7d428d 100644
--- a/packages/api/setup/roles/reviewer.md
+++ b/packages/api/setup/roles/reviewer.md
@@ -26,8 +26,27 @@ the code and the evidence.
 
 ## What to say
 
-Your final message is your review. It is recorded beside the evidence for the
-person who approves the change. For each problem, name the file and what is
-wrong with it. If you found nothing wrong, say so and say what you checked. If
-you could not review something, say what and why. Do not soften a finding and
-do not invent one.
+Your final message is your result, and nothing else: one JSON object,
+
+```
+{"findings": [{"category": "...", "path": "...", "line": 1, "note": "..."}]}
+```
+
+One finding per problem. `category` is the question it answers:
+
+- `spec-mismatch`: the change does not do what the spec asks.
+- `beyond-spec`: the change does something the spec does not ask.
+- `untested-behavior`: the acceptance tests do not exercise the behavior the
+  spec describes, or the evidence does not show them failing before the
+  change and passing after it.
+- `check-bypassed`: a check passes without the behavior being there.
+- `uncovered-breakage`: the change breaks something no check covers.
+
+`path` is a file in the working directory, relative to it, and `line` a line
+in that file. `note` says what is wrong there; it is read by the person who
+approves the change, never by the runtime. If you found nothing wrong, return
+`{"findings": []}`. A finding that names no file and line the working
+directory holds is not recorded, and a message that is not this object is
+recorded as unreadable, never as nothing found. Your result decides nothing:
+the change is neither passed nor failed by it. Do not soften a finding and do
+not invent one.

 ## What to say

+```
+
+One finding per problem. `category` is the question it answers:
+
+- `unchecked-input`: input reaches a query, a command, a path, a template, or
+  a deserializer without being checked.
+- `exposed-secret`: a secret is written into the code, a log, an error, or a
+  response.

+  enforced on one path and not another.

+  passes.

+  reachable before authentication.
+- `unnamed-surface`: an administrative, debug, or legacy surface the spec does
+  not name.
+- `unsafe-default`: a default configuration turns a control off.
+- `weakening-combination`: settings that each look safe weaken a control
+  together.
+
+`path` is a file in the working directory, relative to it, and `line` a line
+in that file. `note` says what is wrong there and what an attacker could do
+with it; it is read by a person, never by the runtime. If you found nothing,
+return `{"findings": []}`. A finding that names no file and line the working
+directory holds is not recorded, and a message that is not this object is
+recorded as unreadable, never as nothing found. Your result decides nothing.
+Do not soften a finding and do not invent one.

 async function retryParts(ctx: LineContext, station: DraftStation, previous: DraftAttempt | null): Promise<RetryPart[]> {
   const back = previous?.sentBack ?? null;
   if (previous === null || back === null) return [];
   switch (back.by) {
     case 'runtime': {
       if (station !== 'test-design') return [{ kind: 'runtime-problems', problems: back.problems }];
       const own = back.problems.filter((p) => !BASE_PROBLEMS.has(p.code));
       return [
         ...(own.length > 0 ? [{ kind: 'runtime-problems' as const, problems: own }] : []),

       return back.reason === null ? [] : [{ kind: 'rejection-reason', reason: back.reason }];

 /**
  * The approved draft written into the artifact root and the base, and
  * locked against its hash (R19). The lock record comes first, with the base
  * digest the draft makes, and is committed before anything is written; then
  * the files, then the Vault's lock of each path not already locked. Every
  * step can be taken again by a resume, and the lock is held to the approved
  * hash: bytes changed in the artifact root after the draft was read are a
  * tamper, never what is locked (I3).
  */
 export async function lockApproved(ctx: LineContext, station: DraftStation, approval: DraftLockRecord['approval']): Promise<StationRefusal | undefined> {

   const record = await readRecord<DraftRecord>(ctx, current.draft);
   if (record.problems.length > 0 || record.files.length === 0) throw new Error(`line: ${station}'s draft ${record.sha256} carries problems and cannot be locked; approval refuses it`);
   const base = basePath(workspaces, ctx.run.id);
   let lock: DraftLockRecord;
   if (drafts.locked === null) {
     // The digest the base will have, computed on a copy, so the base itself is changed only once the lock is recorded.
     const scratch = scratchPath(workspaces, ctx.run.id, `lock.${station}`);
     await copyTree(base, scratch);
     await writeFiles(scratch, record.files.map((f) => ({ path: f.path, bytes: bytesOf(f) })));
     const digest = await treeDigest(scratch);
     await rm(scratch, { recursive: true, force: true });
     lock = {
       runId: ctx.run.id,
       station,
       draft: { sha256: record.sha256, attempt: record.attempt },
       files: record.files.map(({ path, sha256: hash }) => ({ path, sha256: hash })),
       approval,
       baseTreeSha256: digest,
       collectedBy: 'runtime',
     };
     const ref = await vault.recordDraftLock(lock);
     await commit(ctx, { drafts: { [station]: { ...draftsOf(ctx.state, station), locked: ref } } });
   } else {
     lock = await readRecord<DraftLockRecord>(ctx, drafts.locked);
   }
 
   const held = new Set((await vault.readLocks(ctx.run.id))?.entries.map((e) => e.path) ?? []);
   const unlocked = record.files.filter((f) => !held.has(f.path));
   // A path in the artifact root that holds other bytes than the draft is not overwritten: what is there is not ours to replace.
   const clobbered: Array<{ path: string; expected: string; actual: string }> = [];
   for (const file of unlocked) {
     const there = await fileAt(ctx.run.repo, file.path);
     if (there !== undefined && there.sha256 !== file.sha256) clobbered.push({ path: file.path, expected: file.sha256, actual: there.sha256 });
   }
   if (clobbered.length > 0) return recordTamper(ctx, station, null, clobbered, { phase: 'draft-lock' }, null);
   await writeFiles(ctx.run.repo, unlocked.map((f) => ({ path: f.path, bytes: bytesOf(f) })));
   await writeFiles(base, record.files.map((f) => ({ path: f.path, bytes: bytesOf(f) })));
   if (unlocked.length > 0) await vault.lock(ctx.run.id, unlocked.map((f) => f.path), station);
   const manifest = await vault.readLocks(ctx.run.id);
   const tampered = record.files.flatMap((file) => {
     const entry = manifest?.entries.find((e) => e.path === file.path);
     return entry?.sha256 === file.sha256 ? [] : [{ path: file.path, expected: file.sha256, actual: entry?.sha256 ?? 'missing' }];
   });
   if (tampered.length > 0) return recordTamper(ctx, station, null, tampered, { phase: 'draft-lock' }, null);
   const digest = await treeDigest(base);
   if (digest !== lock.baseTreeSha256) throw new Error(`line: the base is ${digest} after ${station}'s draft was written, not the ${lock.baseTreeSha256} its lock recorded`);
 
+  // The critic findings the lock resolves, once it holds: derived from the draft records alone, and derived
+  // again by a resume, where each outcome already written is refused and nothing more is written (R28).
+  const lockRef = draftsOf(ctx.state, station).locked;
+  if (lockRef !== null) await resolveCriticFindings(vault, ctx.state, station, lockRef, lock, () => new Date().toISOString());
+
   applyLock(ctx, station, record);
   ctx.baseTreeSha256 = lock.baseTreeSha256;
   return undefined;
 }
 
 /** The line's artifacts, checks, and spec parts once `record` is locked at `station`, parsed from the bytes it holds. */

===== packages/api/src/findings.ts =====
/**
 * Findings and what became of them (R28, D-A-FO-01, D-R28-01).
 *
 * A review seat's finding is read from its final message strictly, as a
 * nightly reviewer's is: located in the tree it reviewed, or refused with its
 * cause. Its note stays in the claim and enters no record and no prompt (I7).
 * The task's status never reads a finding (I2, D-P4-07).
 */

import { lstat, readFile, realpath } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { join, relative, sep } from 'node:path';
import { CHAR_CLASSES, REVIEW_CATEGORIES, TREE_CATEGORIES, type DraftStation, type FieldDefect, type ReviewCategory, type ReviewSeatKind, type RunId, type RunState, type TaskId, type TreeCategory, type VaultRef } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import type { CheckResult } from '@olympus-ai/integrity';
import { criticFindingsOf, findingKeyOf, isNoRunState } from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import type { CriticRecord, DismissalCause, DraftLockRecord, DraftRecord, EvidenceBundle, FindingOutcome, FindingOutcomeRecord, FindingRef, IntegrationMerged, ReviewFindingsRecord, SeatFinding, Vault } from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import { isAncestor, pathHunks, type HunkRange } from './history.js';
import { readIntegration } from './line.js';
import { readReviewResult } from './nightly-review.js';

/** The byte caps a finding's fields are held to, as the nightly tree finding's schema holds them. */
const PATH_CAP = 1024;
const LINE_CAP = 10;
const CATEGORY_CAP = 40;

const encoder = new TextEncoder();

/** The categories each seat may name: one per question its role asks. */
export function seatCategories(seat: ReviewSeatKind): readonly string[] {
  return seat === 'security' ? TREE_CATEGORIES : REVIEW_CATEGORIES;
}

/** The number of lines a file holds, as an editor would number them. */
export function lineCount(text: string): number {
  if (text.length === 0) return 0;
  const lines = text.split('\n').length;
  return text.endsWith('\n') ? lines - 1 : lines;
}

/**
 * One element's fields checked as `checkFields` checks a schema's: no field
 * but `category`, `path`, `line`, and an optional string `note`; each a
 * string under its cap and in its class. A line may be a whole number.
 */
function seatFields(element: unknown, categories: readonly string[]): { category: string; path: string; line: number } | { defects: FieldDefect[] } {
  const keys = ['category', 'path', 'line'];
  if (typeof element !== 'object' || element === null || Array.isArray(element)) return { defects: keys.map((field) => ({ field, problem: 'missing' })) };
  const { note, ...fields } = element as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (note !== undefined && typeof note !== 'string') return { defects: [{ field: 'note', problem: 'not-a-string' }] };
  const defects: FieldDefect[] = [];
  for (const field of Object.keys(fields)) if (!keys.includes(field)) defects.push({ field, problem: 'unknown' });
  const line = typeof fields.line === 'number' && Number.isSafeInteger(fields.line) ? String(fields.line) : fields.line;
  const values: Record<string, unknown> = { category: fields.category, path: fields.path, line };
  const checks: Record<string, { cap: number; ok: (v: string) => boolean }> = {
    category: { cap: CATEGORY_CAP, ok: (v) => categories.includes(v) },
    path: { cap: PATH_CAP, ok: (v) => CHAR_CLASSES['tree-path'].test(v) },
    line: { cap: LINE_CAP, ok: (v) => CHAR_CLASSES.decimal.test(v) },
  };
  for (const field of keys) {
    const value = values[field];
    const check = checks[field];
    if (check === undefined) continue;
    if (!Object.hasOwn(fields, field)) defects.push({ field, problem: 'missing' });
    else if (typeof value !== 'string') defects.push({ field, problem: 'not-a-string' });
    else if (encoder.encode(value).byteLength > check.cap) defects.push({ field, problem: 'over-cap' });
    else if (!check.ok(value)) defects.push({ field, problem: 'out-of-class' });
  }
  if (defects.length > 0) return { defects };
  return { category: values.category as string, path: values.path as string, line: Number(values.line) };
}

/**
 * The file a finding names, read from `tree` only if it is a regular file
 * inside it: a link, a directory, or a path that resolves outside is not a
 * file the tree holds. Null for any of those, or for no file.
 */
async function heldText(tree: string, path: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const full = join(tree, path);
  try {
    const info = await lstat(full);
    if (!info.isFile()) return null;
    const inside = relative(await realpath(tree), await realpath(full));
    if (inside === '' || inside.startsWith(`..${sep}`) || inside === '..') return null;
    return await readFile(full, 'utf8');
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * A review seat's findings, read strictly from its final message (R28). An
 * unreadable message is recorded as unreadable, never as no findings (I5). A
 * finding whose fields break their rules is `unrecordable`, naming only the
 * field and the rule; one naming no file `tree` holds, or a line that file
 * does not have, is `unlocated`. Neither is ever stored as a finding.
 */
export async function readSeatFindings(seat: ReviewSeatKind, narrative: string, tree: string): Promise<{ readable: boolean; findings: SeatFinding[] }> {
  const read = readReviewResult(narrative);
  if (!read.readable) return { readable: false, findings: [] };
  const categories = seatCategories(seat);
  const findings: SeatFinding[] = [];
  for (const element of read.findings) {
    const fields = seatFields(element, categories);
    if ('defects' in fields) {
      findings.push({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'unrecordable', defects: fields.defects });
      continue;
    }
    const text = await heldText(tree, fields.path);
    if (text === null || fields.line > lineCount(text)) {
      findings.push({ kind: 'refused', cause: 'unlocated', defects: [] });
      continue;
    }
    findings.push({ kind: 'located', category: fields.category as ReviewCategory | TreeCategory, path: fields.path, line: fields.line });
  }
  return { readable: true, findings };
}

/**
 * A finding's id as a person names it on the wire and in the CLI (R28):
 * `review:<run>:<hash>:<index>`, `critic:<run>:<hash>:<index>`, or
 * `nightly:<hash>:<index>`, the hash its source record's.
 */
export function findingId(f: FindingRef): string {
  const s = f.source;
  return s.kind === 'nightly' ? `nightly:${s.record}:${String(f.index)}` : `${s.kind}:${s.record.runId}:${s.record.hash}:${String(f.index)}`;
}

const SHA = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/u;
const RUN = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]*$/u;
const INDEX = /^(?:0|[1-9][0-9]{0,8})$/u;

/** The finding an id names, or null for anything that is not one. */
export function parseFindingId(id: string): FindingRef | null {
  const [kind = '', a = '', b = '', c = '', ...rest] = id.split(':');
  if (rest.length > 0) return null;
  if (kind === 'nightly' && c === '' && SHA.test(a) && INDEX.test(b) && id === `nightly:${a}:${b}`) return { source: { kind: 'nightly', record: a }, index: Number(b) };
  if ((kind === 'review' || kind === 'critic') && RUN.test(a) && SHA.test(b) && INDEX.test(c)) {
    const record: VaultRef = { runId: a as RunId, kind: kind === 'review' ? 'review-findings' : 'critic-result', hash: b };
    return { source: { kind, record }, index: Number(c) };
  }
  return null;
}

/** What a raised finding cites, and so what could resolve it (D-R28-01). */
export type Citation =
  /** A file and line of code at `commit`; null where the code never landed, a run that did not merge. */
  | { readonly kind: 'code'; readonly path: string; readonly line: number; readonly commit: string | null }
  /** A file of the draft a critic read, at the station it was drafted at. */
  | { readonly kind: 'draft'; readonly station: DraftStation; readonly path: string }
  /** A record of the day a nightly reviewer read: nothing resolves it. */
  | { readonly kind: 'record' };

/**
 * A located finding, as R28 measures it (D-A-FO-01). A refused finding is
 * never one: it named nothing the runtime could find.
 */
export interface RaisedFinding {
  readonly ref: FindingRef;
  readonly id: string;
  /** `review`, `security`, `critic`, or `nightly:<job>`. */
  readonly seat: string;
  /** The seat's model family, as the runtime recorded it (I6). */
  readonly family: string;
  /** The run it was raised in; null for a nightly one. */
  readonly runId: RunId | null;
  /** The UTC date a nightly firing covers; null for a run's. */
  readonly date: string | null;
  readonly cites: Citation;
}

function decodeJson(bytes: Uint8Array): unknown {
  return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
}

/**
 * Every located finding the store holds (R28): each review seat's, from the
 * findings record of its committed result only, so a seat rerun after a stop
 * is counted once; each critic's, from the attempts run state holds; and each
 * nightly reviewer's. The store names the set, never a caller.
 */
export async function raisedFindings(vault: Vault): Promise<RaisedFinding[]> {
  const out: RaisedFinding[] = [];
  for (const runId of await vault.listRuns()) {
    let state: RunState;
    try {
      state = await vault.readRunState(runId);
    } catch (error) {
      if (isNoRunState(error)) continue;
      throw error;
    }
    const merged = (await readIntegration(vault, runId)).find((r) => r.kind === 'merged');
    for (const ref of await vault.readReviewFindings(runId)) {
      const record = decodeJson(await vault.read(ref)) as ReviewFindingsRecord;
      if (!Object.hasOwn(state.results, record.taskId) || state.results[record.taskId]?.hash !== record.result.hash) continue;
      record.findings.forEach((f, index) => {
        if (f.kind !== 'located') return;
        const finding: FindingRef = { source: { kind: 'review', record: ref }, index };
        out.push({ ref: finding, id: findingId(finding), seat: record.seat, family: record.reviewer.family, runId, date: null, cites: { kind: 'code', path: f.path, line: f.line, commit: merged?.commit ?? null } });
      });
    }
    for (const [station, drafts] of Object.entries(state.drafts) as Array<[DraftStation, NonNullable<RunState['drafts'][DraftStation]>]>) {
      for (const attempt of drafts.attempts) {
        if (attempt.critic === null || attempt.critic === 'not-seated') continue;
        const ref = attempt.critic;
        const critic = decodeJson(await vault.read(ref)) as CriticRecord;
        criticFindingsOf(critic.findings).forEach((f, index) => {
          if (f.kind !== 'located') return;
          const finding: FindingRef = { source: { kind: 'critic', record: ref }, index };
          out.push({ ref: finding, id: findingId(finding), seat: 'critic', family: critic.critic.family, runId, date: null, cites: { kind: 'draft', station, path: f.path } });
        });
      }
    }
  }
  for (const entry of await vault.readNightly()) {
    const { review } = entry.record;
    if (review?.seat == null) continue;
    const family = review.seat.reviewer.model.family;
    const commit = review.subject?.kind === 'tree' ? review.subject.commit : null;
    review.findings.forEach((f, index) => {
      if (f.kind === 'refused') return;
      const finding: FindingRef = { source: { kind: 'nightly', record: entry.sha256 }, index };
      const cites: Citation = f.kind === 'tree' ? { kind: 'code', path: f.path, line: f.line, commit } : { kind: 'record' };
      out.push({ ref: finding, id: findingId(finding), seat: `nightly:${entry.record.template}`, family, runId: null, date: entry.record.date, cites });
    });
  }
  return out;
}

/** Every outcome, by its finding's key. A finding with none is open (D-A-FO-01). */
export async function outcomesByFinding(vault: Vault): Promise<Map<string, FindingOutcomeRecord>> {
  return new Map((await vault.readFindingOutcomes()).map((e) => [findingKeyOf(e.record.finding), e.record]));
}

/**
 * Resolves the critic findings of a station's drafts once its draft is locked
 * (D-A-FO-01): a located finding whose file's hash differs between the draft
 * the critic read and the locked draft. At `test-design` the locked draft
 * must have a base record, which shows its checks ran over the base. No
 * check covers a spec file, so a `spec` finding is never resolved. Safe to
 * call again: an outcome already written is refused, and nothing is written.
 */
export async function resolveCriticFindings(vault: Vault, state: RunState, station: DraftStation, lockRef: VaultRef, lock: DraftLockRecord, at: () => string): Promise<number> {
  if (station === 'spec') return 0;
  if ((await vault.readBaseRun(state.runId, lock.draft.sha256)) === null) return 0;
  const locked = new Map(lock.files.map((f) => [f.path, f.sha256]));
  let resolved = 0;
  for (const attempt of state.drafts[station]?.attempts ?? []) {
    if (attempt.critic === null || attempt.critic === 'not-seated' || attempt.draft === null) continue;
    const critic = decodeJson(await vault.read(attempt.critic)) as CriticRecord;
    const draft = decodeJson(await vault.read(attempt.draft)) as DraftRecord;
    const raisedAt = new Map(draft.files.map((f) => [f.path, f.sha256]));
    const findings = criticFindingsOf(critic.findings);
    for (const [index, f] of findings.entries()) {
      if (f.kind !== 'located') continue;
      const raised = raisedAt.get(f.path);
      const now = locked.get(f.path);
      // A file the locked draft no longer holds did not change into anything a check covers: it stays open.
      if (raised === undefined || now === undefined || raised === now) continue;
      const written = await vault.recordFindingOutcome({
        finding: { source: { kind: 'critic', record: attempt.critic }, index },
        outcome: { kind: 'resolved', resolution: { kind: 'draft-changed', lock: lockRef, raised, locked: now } },
        at: at(),
        collectedBy: 'runtime',
      });
      if (written !== null) resolved += 1;
    }
  }
  return resolved;
}

/**
 * A line mapped through a zero-context diff (D-R28-01): null where a hunk
 * rewrote it, otherwise shifted by every hunk wholly before it. A pure
 * insertion after line n is before every line past n.
 */
export function mapLine(hunks: readonly HunkRange[], line: number): number | null {
  let delta = 0;
  for (const h of hunks) {
    if (h.oldLines > 0 && line >= h.oldStart && line < h.oldStart + h.oldLines) return null;
    const before = h.oldLines > 0 ? h.oldStart + h.oldLines - 1 < line : h.oldStart < line;
    if (before) delta += h.newLines - h.oldLines;
  }
  return line + delta;
}

/** The hunk whose base side holds `line`, if any: a pure insertion holds none. */
export function holdingHunk(hunks: readonly HunkRange[], line: number): HunkRange | undefined {
  return hunks.find((h) => h.oldLines > 0 && line >= h.oldStart && line < h.oldStart + h.oldLines);
}

/**
 * A run's latest accepted evidence bundle and its ref: the last bundle of a
 * passed task, whose head tree is the tree `integrate` pushed, so its line
 * numbers are the run's change's head side (D-R28-01). Null for none.
 */
async function latestAccepted(vault: Vault, state: RunState): Promise<{ bundle: EvidenceBundle; ref: VaultRef } | null> {
  const last = new Map<TaskId, { bundle: EvidenceBundle; ref: VaultRef; at: number }>();
  for (const [at, ref] of state.evidenceRefs.entries()) {
    const bundle = decodeJson(await vault.read(ref)) as EvidenceBundle;
    last.set(bundle.taskId, { bundle, ref, at });
  }
  let latest: { bundle: EvidenceBundle; ref: VaultRef; at: number } | null = null;
  for (const [taskId, entry] of last) {
    if (Object.hasOwn(state.tasks, taskId) && state.tasks[taskId] === 'passed' && (latest === null || entry.at > latest.at)) latest = entry;
  }
  return latest;
}

/** Whether a check's result passed, judged as the gate judges one (A-P8-01). */
function passed(result: CheckResult): boolean {
  return result.expectation === null ? result.exitCode === 0 : result.expectation.held;
}

/** What one resolving run did to the open code findings. */
export interface CodeResolutions {
  readonly resolved: number;
  /** Findings whose history could not be read, by id: each stays open, and the walk names them (I5). */
  readonly unreadable: readonly string[];
}

/**
 * Resolves the open code findings a merged run fixed (D-A-FO-01): each whose
 * cited line, mapped from its commit to the run's base, a hunk of the run's
 * change rewrote, where the run's pinned coverage check passed with a changed
 * line of that hunk executed. A run with no line coverage, a check that did
 * not pass, a line the history rewrote, or a file either end lacks resolves
 * nothing, and each such finding stays open (I5). Today no run carries line
 * coverage: a pinned coverage check is refused at `verify` until M3 gives its
 * report a channel (D-P7-05, D-R28-01).
 */
export async function resolveCodeFindings(vault: Vault, checkout: string, runId: RunId, at: () => string): Promise<CodeResolutions> {
  const merged = (await readIntegration(vault, runId)).find((r): r is IntegrationMerged => r.kind === 'merged');
  if (merged === undefined) return { resolved: 0, unreadable: [] };
  let state: RunState;
  try {
    state = await vault.readRunState(runId);
  } catch (error) {
    // A merged run whose state the store does not hold has no evidence to resolve anything by; the plan's
    // reader refuses it at close (D-A-OB-14). Anything else is a fault, never nothing resolved (I5).
    if (isNoRunState(error)) return { resolved: 0, unreadable: [] };
    throw error;
  }
  const accepted = await latestAccepted(vault, state);
  // A bundle stored before R28 names no lines: read as none.
  const lines = accepted?.bundle.tamper.changedLines ?? null;
  if (accepted === null || lines === null) return { resolved: 0, unreadable: [] };
  const check = accepted.bundle.checks.find((c) => c.checkId === lines.check);
  if (check === undefined || !passed(check)) return { resolved: 0, unreadable: [] };

  const outcomes = await outcomesByFinding(vault);
  const unreadable: string[] = [];
  let resolved = 0;
  for (const f of await raisedFindings(vault)) {
    if (f.cites.kind !== 'code' || f.cites.commit === null || f.runId === runId || outcomes.has(findingKeyOf(f.ref))) continue;
    const { path, line: cited, commit } = f.cites;
    try {
      // Raised before this run's base, on its history; a finding of later code is not this run's to resolve.
      if (!(await isAncestor(checkout, commit, merged.baseCommit))) continue;
      const toBase = await pathHunks(checkout, commit, merged.baseCommit, path);
      const line = toBase === null ? null : mapLine(toBase, cited);
      if (line === null) continue;
      const change = await pathHunks(checkout, merged.baseCommit, merged.commit, path);
      const hunk = change === null ? undefined : holdingHunk(change, line);
      if (hunk === undefined) continue;
      const end = hunk.newStart + hunk.newLines;
      const executed = (lines.files.find((x) => x.path === path)?.executed ?? []).filter((n) => n >= hunk.newStart && n < end);
      if (executed.length === 0) continue;
      const written = await vault.recordFindingOutcome({
        finding: f.ref,
        outcome: { kind: 'resolved', resolution: { kind: 'covered-change', runId, mergeCommit: merged.mergeCommit, path, line, hunk, executed, evidence: accepted.ref } },
        at: at(),
        collectedBy: 'runtime',
      });
      if (written !== null) resolved += 1;
    } catch {
      unreadable.push(f.id);
    }
  }
  return { resolved, unreadable };
}

/** A raised finding with what became of it, as the API and the CLI show it (R28). `open` is the absence of an outcome. */
export interface FindingSummary {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly seat: string;
  readonly family: string;
  readonly runId: RunId | null;
  readonly date: string | null;
  readonly cites: Citation;
  readonly outcome: { readonly kind: 'open' } | FindingOutcome;
}

/** Every located finding and its outcome, open where it has none, sorted by id. */
export async function listFindings(vault: Vault): Promise<FindingSummary[]> {
  const outcomes = await outcomesByFinding(vault);
  return (await raisedFindings(vault))
    .map((f): FindingSummary => ({ id: f.id, seat: f.seat, family: f.family, runId: f.runId, date: f.date, cites: f.cites, outcome: outcomes.get(findingKeyOf(f.ref))?.outcome ?? { kind: 'open' } }))
    .sort((a, b) => (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
}

/**
 * A person's dismissal of a finding, with a cause and no text (A-R16-05,
 * D-A-FO-01). Only a located finding can be dismissed: a refused one was
 * never measured. A finding that already has an outcome keeps it.
 */
export async function dismissFinding(
  vault: Vault, id: string, cause: DismissalCause, at: string,
): Promise<{ readonly kind: 'dismissed'; readonly finding: FindingSummary } | { readonly kind: 'unknown' } | { readonly kind: 'decided'; readonly outcome: FindingOutcome }> {
  const ref = parseFindingId(id);
  if (ref === null) return { kind: 'unknown' };
  const finding = (await raisedFindings(vault)).find((f) => f.id === id);
  if (finding === undefined) return { kind: 'unknown' };
  const written = await vault.recordFindingOutcome({ finding: finding.ref, outcome: { kind: 'dismissed', cause }, at, collectedBy: 'runtime' });
  if (written === null) {
    const outcome = (await outcomesByFinding(vault)).get(findingKeyOf(finding.ref))?.outcome;
    if (outcome === undefined) throw new Error(`findings: ${id} was refused a second outcome, and none is stored`);
    return { kind: 'decided', outcome };
  }
  return { kind: 'dismissed', finding: { id, seat: finding.seat, family: finding.family, runId: finding.runId, date: finding.date, cites: finding.cites, outcome: { kind: 'dismissed', cause } } };
}

===== packages/api/src/history.ts =====

@@ -111,16 +111,62 @@ async function emptyTree(checkout: string): Promise<string> {
 /**
  * The patch from `a` to `b`, with no external diff or text conversion; `a` null is the empty tree.
  * `--text` diffs every file as text, so a `.gitattributes` marking files `-diff` or `binary` cannot empty
  * the hunks a partial reversal is matched on (gemini-3).
  */
 async function patch(checkout: string, a: string | null, b: string, context: 'default' | 'none'): Promise<string> {
   const left = a ?? (await emptyTree(checkout));
   return git(
     checkout, 'diff-tree', '-p', '-r', '--text', '--no-ext-diff', '--no-textconv', '--no-color', '--no-renames', '--full-index',
     ...(context === 'none' ? ['-U0'] : []), requireOid(left, 'tree'), requireOid(b, 'tree'),
   );
 }
 
+/** One hunk's ranges at zero context, as `@@ -a,b +c,d @@` gives them; a count left out is one (R28). */
+export interface HunkRange {
+  readonly oldStart: number;
+  readonly oldLines: number;
+  readonly newStart: number;
+  readonly newLines: number;
+}
+
+const HUNK_HEADER = /^@@ -(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? \+(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? @@/u;
+
+/** Every hunk header's ranges in a patch, in order. */
+export function parseHunkRanges(text: string): HunkRange[] {
+  const out: HunkRange[] = [];
+  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
+    const m = HUNK_HEADER.exec(line);
+    if (m === null) continue;
+    out.push({ oldStart: Number(m[1]), oldLines: m[2] === undefined ? 1 : Number(m[2]), newStart: Number(m[3]), newLines: m[4] === undefined ? 1 : Number(m[4]) });
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+
+/** Whether `commit` holds a file at `path`. Only a missing path is no; a read that failed is never an answer (I5). */
+async function holdsPath(checkout: string, commit: string, path: string): Promise<boolean> {
+  try {
+    await run('git', gitArgs(checkout, 'cat-file', '-e', `${requireOid(commit, 'commit')}:${path}`));
+    return true;
+  } catch (error) {
+    if (typeof error === 'object' && error !== null && 'code' in error && error.code === 128) return false;
+    throw error;
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * One path's hunks from `from` to `to`, at zero context with renames off
+ * (R28): a renamed file is a file gone and another added. Null where either
+ * commit holds no file at `path`, which nothing maps a line through.
+ */
+export async function pathHunks(checkout: string, from: string, to: string, path: string): Promise<HunkRange[] | null> {
+  if (!(await holdsPath(checkout, from, path)) || !(await holdsPath(checkout, to, path))) return null;
+  const text = await git(
+    checkout, 'diff', '--text', '--no-ext-diff', '--no-textconv', '--no-color', '--no-renames', '-U0',
+    requireOid(from, 'commit'), requireOid(to, 'commit'), '--', `:(literal)${path}`,
+  );
+  return parseHunkRanges(text);
+}
+
 /** The `patch-id --stable` of a patch, or null for an empty one. */
 async function patchId(checkout: string, text: string): Promise<string | null> {
   if (text.trim() === '') return null;

 import { imageFor, type BuiltGraph } from '../graph.js';

@@ -336,434 +337,449 @@ function refusalStatus(outcome: Exclude<RunOutcome, { ok: true }>): number {
 export function createApiServer(options: ApiServerOptions): ApiServer {
   if (typeof options.token !== 'string' || options.token.length < MIN_TOKEN_LENGTH) {
     throw new Error(`api server: the local token must be at least ${String(MIN_TOKEN_LENGTH)} characters`);
   }
   if (typeof options.principal !== 'string' || options.principal.trim() === '') throw new Error('api server: principal must name who the token is');
   const expected = digest(`Bearer ${options.token}`);
   const { components, principal } = options;
   const runs = new Map<RunId, Tracked>();
   const open = new Set<ServerResponse>();
   // The nightly jobs are given the sandbox, the scanners, and the Vault, and no builder or integrator (I2). The two
   // reviewer jobs seat the reviewer driver, under the roles runs are admitted with (R13b, D-R13b-03, D-R13b-07).
   const jobs = new NightlyJobs({
     vault: components.vault, sandbox: components.sandbox, workspaces: components.workspaces,
     limits: components.profile === null ? null : components.profile.limits, scanners: components.scanners,
     target: options.nightlyTarget ?? null,
     seat: {
       driver: components.reviewer, roles: options.rolesDirectory,
       image: components.profile === null ? 'none' : imageFor(components.profile, components.reviewer),
     },
   });
   const intake = new Intake({ vault: components.vault, secrets: options.secrets ?? (() => undefined), jobs });
   let scheduler: Scheduler | null = null;
 
   const tracked = (runId: RunId): Tracked => {
     let entry = runs.get(runId);
     if (entry === undefined) {
       entry = { drive: null, cancel: null, lastOutcome: null, streams: new Set() };
       runs.set(runId, entry);
     }
     return entry;
   };
 
   const publish = (entry: Tracked, event: RunEvent): void => {
     for (const stream of entry.streams) stream(event);
   };
 
   /** Drives a run in the background; the returned promise never rejects. */
   const drive = (runId: RunId, start: (hooks: DriveHooks) => Promise<RunOutcome>): void => {
     const entry = tracked(runId);
     entry.cancel = null;
     const hooks: DriveHooks = {
       cancelRequested: () => entry.cancel,
       onCommit: (state) => { publish(entry, { event: 'state', data: state }); },
       onAttemptFailed: (task, failure) => {
         publish(entry, { event: 'attempt-failed', data: { task, failure } });
         options.log?.(attemptLine(runId, task, failure));
       },
       onGateFailed: (task, failure) => {
         publish(entry, { event: 'gate-failed', data: { task, failure } });
       },
     };
     entry.drive = start(hooks)
       .then((outcome): DriveEnd => ({ kind: 'outcome', outcome }), (error: unknown): DriveEnd => ({ kind: 'error', message: describe(error) }))
       .then((end) => (end.kind === 'error' ? halt(runId, end) : end))
       .then(async (end) => {
         entry.lastOutcome = end;
         entry.drive = null;
         entry.cancel = null;
         const standing = await runStanding(components.vault, runId).then((s) => s.standing, () => undefined);
         if (standing !== undefined) publish(entry, { event: 'standing', data: standing });
         publish(entry, { event: 'end', data: end });
       });
   };
 
   /**
    * A drive that ended in an error, rather than a refusal the line committed,
    * left the run's state as it was mid-step, which status would read as open
    * and a cancel would relabel. The halt is committed so the stop is the
    * Vault's record, and survives this process (A-P9-02). A run already
    * cancelled keeps that record instead; if the halt cannot be committed, the
    * drive's end says so.
    */
   const halt = async (runId: RunId, end: DriveEnd & { kind: 'error' }): Promise<DriveEnd> => {
     try {
       const state = await components.vault.readRunState(runId);
       if (state.cancelled !== null || state.halted !== null) return end;
       await components.vault.commitRunState({ ...state, halted: { at: new Date().toISOString(), message: end.message } }, state.version);
       return end;
     } catch (error) {
       return { kind: 'error', message: `${end.message}; the halt could not be recorded: ${describe(error)}` };
     }
   };
 
   /** The per-run report, read from the Vault alone (D-I1b-07). */
   const report = async (runId: RunId): Promise<RunReport> => {
     try {
       await components.vault.readRunState(runId);
     } catch (error) {
       throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no run ${runId}: ${describe(error)}`);
     }
     return runReport(components.vault, runId);
   };
 
   /**
    * The run's records as OTLP/JSON, for the client to send where its operator
    * chooses: the service itself never makes an outbound request (D-R12-02).
    */
   const telemetry = async (runId: RunId): Promise<TelemetryExport> => {
     try {
       await components.vault.readRunState(runId);
     } catch (error) {
       // A state that cannot be read is a fault, never absence (I5). A run refused at admission has no
       // state and is still exported; only a run with no record at all is absent.
       if (!isNoRunState(error)) throw error;
       if ((await components.vault.readDecisions(runId)).length === 0) throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no run ${runId}: ${describe(error)}`);
     }
     const result = await telemetryOf(components.vault, runId);
     if (!result.ok) throw new HttpError(409, 'not-finished', `run ${runId} has not finished, so its records may still change and it is not exported`);
     return result.export;
   };
 
   const view = async (runId: RunId): Promise<RunView> => {
     // Taken before the read, not after: a drive that ends while the state is read has committed
     // past what was read, and a view saying `driving: false` beside that state would present a
     // stale standing as settled. Read first, a drive running at the start is still reported.
     const entry = runs.get(runId);
     const driving = entry?.drive != null;
     const lastOutcome = entry?.lastOutcome ?? null;
     let read: { state: RunState; standing: RunView['standing'] };
     try {
       read = await runStanding(components.vault, runId);
     } catch (error) {
       throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no run ${runId}: ${describe(error)}`);
     }
     return { runId, state: read.state, standing: read.standing, driving, lastOutcome };
   };
 
   const create = async (body: CreateRunBody): Promise<CreatedRun> => {
     const policy = await loadPolicyFile(options.policyFile);
     if (!policy.ok) throw new HttpError(422, 'refused', `the server's policy file is refused: ${policy.message}`, policy);
     const runId = randomUUID() as RunId;
     const { intent, ...request } = body;
     const admission = await admitRun({
       runId,
       ...request,
       // Only this route, authenticated as the principal, supplies an intent (R19, I7), a bug fix's as its parts (A-R16-02).
       intent: intent === undefined ? null : typeof intent === 'string' ? { text: intent, from: 'principal' } : { ...intent, from: 'principal', template: 'bug-fix' },
       rubrics: options.rubricsDirectory ?? null,
       policy: policy.policy,
       roles: options.rolesDirectory,
       components,
       // The host's scanner, so a run above its base commit's ceiling is refused (R40); none, and the record says no scan ran.
       readiness: options.readiness ?? null,
     });
     if (!admission.ok) throw new HttpError(refusalStatus(admission), 'refused', `run refused: ${admission.reason}`, admission);
     const { admitted } = admission;
     drive(runId, (hooks) => admitted.drive(hooks));
     return { runId, worstCase: admitted.worstCase, state: admitted.state };
   };
 
   const approve = async (runId: RunId, body: ApproveBody): Promise<RunView> => {
     const entry = tracked(runId);
     if (entry.drive !== null) throw new HttpError(409, 'conflict', `run ${runId} is being driven; it is not waiting for an approval`);
     await view(runId);
     const outcome = await approveStation({ runId, key: body.key, draft: body.draft ?? null, approvedBy: principal, vault: components.vault });
     if (!outcome.ok) throw new HttpError(outcome.reason === 'invalid-request' ? 400 : 409, 'refused', outcome.message, outcome);
     drive(runId, (hooks) => resumeRun({ runId, components }, hooks));
     return view(runId);
   };
 
   const reject = async (runId: RunId, body: RejectBody): Promise<RunView> => {
     const entry = tracked(runId);
     if (entry.drive !== null) throw new HttpError(409, 'conflict', `run ${runId} is being driven; it is not waiting for a decision on a draft`);
     await view(runId);
     const outcome = await rejectDraft({ runId, key: body.key, draft: body.draft, notes: body.notes ?? null, reason: body.reason ?? null, rejectedBy: principal, vault: components.vault });
     if (!outcome.ok) throw new HttpError(outcome.reason === 'invalid-request' ? 400 : 409, 'refused', outcome.message, outcome);
     // A draft sent back is drafted again; a parked run is driven only to record where it stopped.
     drive(runId, (hooks) => resumeRun({ runId, components }, hooks));
     return view(runId);
   };
 
   const cancel = async (runId: RunId): Promise<RunView> => {
     await view(runId);
     const entry = tracked(runId);
     const inFlight = entry.drive;
     if (inFlight !== null) {
       entry.cancel = { by: principal, at: new Date().toISOString() };
       await inFlight;
       const after = await view(runId);
       if (after.state.cancelled === null) {
         throw new HttpError(409, 'refused', `run ${runId} finished before the cancellation was taken; there is nothing to cancel`, after.standing);
       }
       return after;
     }
     const outcome = await cancelRun({ runId, by: principal, vault: components.vault });
     if (!outcome.ok) throw new HttpError(outcome.reason === 'finished' ? 409 : 400, 'refused', outcome.message, outcome);
     const now = await view(runId);
     publish(entry, { event: 'state', data: now.state });
     publish(entry, { event: 'standing', data: now.standing });
     return now;
   };
 
   const events = async (runId: RunId, res: ServerResponse): Promise<void> => {
     const now = await view(runId);
     const entry = tracked(runId);
     res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8', 'cache-control': 'no-store', connection: 'keep-alive' });
     open.add(res);
     const write = (event: RunEvent): void => {
       res.write(`event: ${event.event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(event.data)}\n\n`);
     };
     const finish = (): void => {
       entry.streams.delete(stream);
       open.delete(res);
       res.end();
     };
     const stream = (event: RunEvent): void => {
       write(event);
       if (event.event === 'end') finish();
     };
     write({ event: 'state', data: now.state });
     write({ event: 'standing', data: now.standing });
     if (entry.drive === null) {
       write({ event: 'end', data: entry.lastOutcome });
       open.delete(res);
       res.end();
       return;
     }
     entry.streams.add(stream);
     res.on('close', () => {
       entry.streams.delete(stream);
       open.delete(res);
     });
   };
 
   /**
    * `POST /events/<subscription>`, the one route outside the local token: a
    * delivery authenticates by its signature (D-A-TF-06). The subscription is
    * checked before the body is read, and nothing a refusal answers with is
    * the body's text.
    */
   const deliver = async (req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse, id: string): Promise<void> => {
     const policy = await loadPolicyFile(options.policyFile);
     // A policy that does not load grants no subscription; nothing is read or recorded against it (I5).
     if (!policy.ok) throw new HttpError(503, 'refused', 'the server has no policy that loads, so no subscription is granted');
     const subscription = intake.subscription(policy.policy, id);
     let outcome: DeliveryOutcome;
     if (subscription === null) {
       outcome = await intake.count(UNSUBSCRIBED_BUCKET, 'not-subscribed');
       discard(req, MAX_DELIVERY_BYTES);
     } else {
       const body = await readCapped(req, MAX_DELIVERY_BYTES);
       if (body === 'oversize') {
         outcome = await intake.count(subscription.id, 'oversize');
         discard(req, MAX_DELIVERY_BYTES);
       } else {
         const signature = req.headers['x-hub-signature-256'];
         outcome = await intake.deliver(policy.policy, subscription, typeof signature === 'string' ? signature : undefined, body);
       }
     }
     send(res, deliveryStatus(outcome), outcome satisfies DeliveryAnswer);
   };
 
   const listIntake = async (): Promise<CandidateSummary[]> => {
     const entries = await components.vault.readTriggerDecisions();
     return entries.flatMap((entry) => (entry.candidate === null ? [] : [summary({ ...entry, candidate: entry.candidate })]));
   };
 
   const readIntake = async (id: string): Promise<CandidateView> => {
     const entry = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(id) ? await components.vault.readCandidate(id as CandidateId) : null;
     if (entry?.candidate == null) throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no intake candidate ${id}`);
     const c = entry.candidate;
     return { ...summary({ ...entry, candidate: c }), body: c.body, signedAt: c.signedAt, decisionSha256: entry.sha256 };
   };
 
   /** A person's dismissal: once, of a candidate neither admitted nor expired, with a cause and no text (A-R16-05). */
   const dismissIntake = async (id: string, body: DismissBody): Promise<CandidateSummary> => {
     const entry = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(id) ? await components.vault.readCandidate(id as CandidateId) : null;
     if (entry?.candidate == null) throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no intake candidate ${id}`);
     if (entry.expiredAt !== null) throw new HttpError(409, 'conflict', `candidate ${id} expired at ${entry.expiredAt}; an expired candidate is already recorded as not worked`);
     if (!(await components.vault.dismissCandidate(entry.candidate.id, body.cause, new Date().toISOString()))) {
       throw new HttpError(409, 'conflict', `candidate ${id} was admitted or dismissed before`);
     }
     const after = await components.vault.readCandidate(entry.candidate.id);
     if (after?.candidate == null) throw new Error(`server: candidate ${id} is gone after its dismissal`);
     return summary({ ...after, candidate: after.candidate });
   };
 
   /**
    * `GET /readiness`: the scan's report for one revision, as a stream. A bad
    * revision is refused before the stream opens; then `started` names the
    * commit, a comment every 15 s keeps a client waiting through a scan that
    * outlasts its header timeout, and one `report` or `failed` event ends it.
    * Read-only: nothing is written, and admission does not read the ceiling.
    */
   const readiness = async (revision: string, res: ServerResponse): Promise<void> => {
     const scanner = options.readiness ?? null;
     if (scanner === null) throw new HttpError(409, 'refused', 'this server scans no repository; readiness is served by factory-host over its FACTORY_REPOSITORY');
     let commit: string;
     try {
       commit = await scanner.resolve(revision);
     } catch (error) {
       throw new HttpError(400, 'bad-request', error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
     }
     res.writeHead(200, { 'content-type': 'text/event-stream; charset=utf-8', 'cache-control': 'no-store', connection: 'keep-alive' });
     open.add(res);
     res.write(`event: started\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ revision, commit })}\n\n`);
     const beat = setInterval(() => { res.write(': scanning\n\n'); }, 15_000);
     try {
       const report = await scanner.scan(commit);
       res.write(`event: report\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(report)}\n\n`);
     } catch (error) {
       res.write(`event: failed\ndata: ${JSON.stringify({ message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) })}\n\n`);
     } finally {
       clearInterval(beat);
       open.delete(res);
       res.end();
     }
   };
 
   const route = async (req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse): Promise<void> => {
     const url = new URL(req.url ?? '/', 'http://server.invalid');
     const parts = url.pathname.split('/').filter((p) => p !== '');
     const method = req.method ?? 'GET';
     if (parts[0] === 'events') {
       if (parts.length !== 2 || method !== 'POST') throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no route ${url.pathname}`);
       await deliver(req, res, decodeURIComponent(parts[1] ?? ''));
       return;
     }
     const presented = digest(req.headers.authorization ?? '');
     if (!timingSafeEqual(presented, expected)) throw new HttpError(401, 'unauthorized', 'a valid local token is required');
     if (parts[0] === 'intake' && parts.length === 3 && parts[2] === 'dismiss') {
       if (method !== 'POST') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 200, await dismissIntake(decodeURIComponent(parts[1] ?? ''), dismissBody(await readJson(req))));
       return;
     }
     if (parts[0] === 'intake' && parts.length <= 2) {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 200, parts.length === 1 ? await listIntake() : await readIntake(decodeURIComponent(parts[1] ?? '')));
       return;
     }
+    // A finding's outcome (R28): listed for a person to read, and dismissed by one, with a cause and no text.
+    if (parts[0] === 'findings' && parts.length === 1) {
+      if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
+      send(res, 200, await listFindings(components.vault));
+      return;
+    }
+    if (parts[0] === 'findings' && parts.length === 3 && parts[2] === 'dismiss') {
+      if (method !== 'POST') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
+      const id = decodeURIComponent(parts[1] ?? '');
+      const done = await dismissFinding(components.vault, id, dismissBody(await readJson(req)).cause, new Date().toISOString());
+      if (done.kind === 'unknown') throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no located finding ${id}`);
+      if (done.kind === 'decided') throw new HttpError(409, 'conflict', `finding ${id} is already ${done.outcome.kind}; a finding has one outcome`);
+      send(res, 200, done.finding);
+      return;
+    }
     if (parts[0] === 'nightly' && parts.length === 1) {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 200, await components.vault.readNightly());
       return;
     }
     if (parts[0] === 'outcomes' && parts.length === 1) {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 200, await outcomes(components.vault, outcomeWindow(url.searchParams)));
       return;
     }
     if (parts[0] === 'readiness' && parts.length === 1) {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       await readiness(url.searchParams.get('revision') ?? 'HEAD', res);
       return;
     }
     if (parts[0] !== 'runs' || parts.length > 3) throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no route ${url.pathname}`);
     if (parts.length === 1) {
       if (method !== 'POST') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 201, await create(createBody(await readJson(req))));
       return;
     }
     const runId = decodeURIComponent(parts[1] ?? '') as RunId;
     const action = parts[2];
     if (action === undefined) {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 200, await view(runId));
       return;
     }
     if (action === 'report') {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 200, await report(runId));
       return;
     }
     if (action === 'telemetry') {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       send(res, 200, await telemetry(runId));
       return;
     }
     if (action === 'events') {
       if (method !== 'GET') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       await events(runId, res);
       return;
     }
     if (action === 'approve' || action === 'cancel' || action === 'reject') {
       if (method !== 'POST') throw new HttpError(405, 'method-not-allowed', `${method} ${url.pathname}`);
       if (action === 'approve') send(res, 200, await approve(runId, approveBody(await readJson(req))));
       else if (action === 'reject') send(res, 200, await reject(runId, rejectBody(await readJson(req))));
       else send(res, 200, await cancel(runId));
       return;
     }
     throw new HttpError(404, 'not-found', `no route ${url.pathname}`);
   };
 
   const server: Server = createServer((req, res) => {
     route(req, res).catch((error: unknown) => {
       const http = error instanceof HttpError ? error : new HttpError(500, 'internal', describe(error));
       if (res.headersSent) {
         res.end();
         return;
       }
       sendError(res, http);
     });
   });
 
   return {
     listen: async (port, host) => {
       // The schedule is the service's: what a stop missed is recorded before the first request is taken (D-A-TF-08).
       scheduler ??= await startSchedules(async () => {
         const loaded = await loadPolicyFile(options.policyFile);
         return loaded.ok ? loaded.policy : null;
       }, components.vault, intake);
       return new Promise((resolveUrl, reject) => {
         server.once('error', reject);
         server.listen(port, host, () => {
           server.off('error', reject);
           const address = server.address();
           if (address === null || typeof address === 'string') {
             reject(new Error('api server: listening on something other than a TCP port'));
             return;
           }
           const shown = address.family === 'IPv6' ? `[${address.address}]` : address.address;
           resolveUrl(`http://${shown}:${String(address.port)}`);
         });
       });
     },
     close: async () => {
       scheduler?.stop();
       scheduler = null;
       for (const res of open) res.end();
       open.clear();
       await new Promise<void>((done) => {
         server.close(() => { done(); });
         server.closeAllConnections();
       });
       await Promise.all([...runs.values()].flatMap((entry) => (entry.drive === null ? [] : [entry.drive])));
     },
   };
 }
 
 /**
  * An ISO 8601 date-time with `Z` or an offset. Without a zone `Date.parse`
  * reads a time in the server's own, so one request would name a different
  * window on every host.
  */

 /**

 async function review(ctx: LineContext, task: Task): Promise<StationRefusal | undefined> {
   const { reviewer, vault } = ctx.components;
   const kind = isSecuritySeat(task) ? 'security' : 'review';
   const contract = kind === 'security' ? securitySeatContract(STATION_CONTRACTS.review) : STATION_CONTRACTS.review;

   }

   // The seat the runtime resolved, at the weaker of the two grades: what the reviewer reports can lower the
   // grade and never widen it, and never becomes the recorded identity, which stays the runtime's (I2, I6; P6 codex-1, gemini-1; R9 codex-1).
   const seat = { ...planned.seat, independence: weakerGrade(planned.seat.independence, seated.seat.independence) };
   const ref = await vault.recordTaskResult(ctx.run.id, ran.result);

 /**

+/**
+ * R2's figures over settled runs, without the findings (R28): a finding's
+ * outcome is written after its run settles, by a person or a later merge, so
+ * its rates move with no run changing and are no drift. They are R2's to
+ * report, never this snapshot's to compare.
+ */
+function settledFigures(runs: readonly RunRecords[]): Omit<ReturnType<typeof deriveOutcomes>, 'findings'> {
+  const { findings: _findings, ...figures } = deriveOutcomes(runs);
+  return figures;
