 /** Every leaf of a JSON value, by its dotted path, as JSON: two snapshots compare figure by figure. */
 export function flattenFigures(value: unknown, prefix = '', out: Record<string, string> = {}): Record<string, string> {
   if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
@@ -140,337 +151,337 @@ function refusedWith(refusals: readonly NightlyRefusal[]): NightlyRecord['outcom
 export class NightlyJobs {
   readonly #options: NightlyOptions;
   readonly #now: () => number;
 
   constructor(options: NightlyOptions) {
     this.#options = options;
     this.#now = options.now ?? Date.now;
   }
 
   /** Runs the schedule's job for `date` and writes its record; returns the record and its hash. */
   async run(policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule & { readonly template: JobTemplateId }, date: string, intake: Intake): Promise<{ record: NightlyRecord; sha256: string }> {
     const startedAt = new Date(this.#now()).toISOString();
     let part: Part;
     try {
       const { template } = schedule;
       if (template === 'nightly-integrity') part = await this.#integrity(schedule, date);
       else if (template === 'nightly-security') part = await this.#security(policy, schedule, date, intake);
       else if (template === 'observe-history') {
         const walked = await walkHistory(this.#options.vault, this.#options.target, policy, this.#now);
         part = { findings: [], refusals: walked.refusals, integrity: null, security: null, observation: walked.observation };
       }
       else if (isReviewJobTemplate(template)) {
         const { vault, sandbox, workspaces, limits, target } = this.#options;
         const reviewed = await reviewJob(template, {
           vault, sandbox, root: workspaces.root, user: workspaces.user, limits, target, seat: this.#options.seat ?? null, now: this.#now,
         }, policy, schedule, date, intake);
         part = { findings: [], refusals: reviewed.refusals, integrity: null, security: null, review: reviewed.review };
       } else throw new Error(`no job is named ${String(template)}`);
     } catch (error) {
       // A failure no part caught still writes the record, refused, so the firing is never left without one (codex-5).
       part = { findings: [], refusals: [{ part: 'job', cause: 'job-failed', detail: describe(error) }], integrity: null, security: null };
     }
     const record: NightlyRecord = {
       schedule: schedule.id, template: schedule.template, date, startedAt, endedAt: new Date(this.#now()).toISOString(),
       outcome: refusedWith(part.refusals), findings: part.findings,
       integrity: part.integrity, security: part.security, review: part.review ?? null, observation: part.observation ?? null, collectedBy: 'runtime',
     };
     return { record, sha256: await this.#options.vault.recordNightly(record) };
   }
 
   /** A delivery's signal, recorded in place of a candidate (R8a, D-R8a-11); the observation's hash is returned. */
   signal(kind: 'post-merge' | 'ci-failure', fields: Readonly<Record<string, string>>, signedAt: number): Promise<string> {
     return recordSignal(this.#options.vault, this.#now, kind, fields, signedAt);
   }
 
   async #integrity(schedule: TriggerSchedule, date: string): Promise<Part> {
     const { vault } = this.#options;
     const findings: NightlyFinding[] = [];
     const refusals: NightlyRefusal[] = [];
     let prior: NightlyEntry | undefined;
     try {
       prior = [...(await vault.readNightly())]
         .filter((e) => e.record.schedule === schedule.id && e.record.integrity !== null && e.record.date < date)
         .at(-1);
     } catch (error) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: `the nightly records could not be read: ${describe(error)}` });
     }
     // Each earlier firing names the record its job wrote. A baseline no firing names was not written by this job (codex-4, gemini-2).
     const named = new Map<string, string>();
     for (const entry of await vault.readTriggerDecisions()) {
       const { source, key, outcome } = entry.decision;
       if (source === `schedule:${schedule.id}` && outcome.kind === 'job') named.set(key, outcome.nightly);
     }
     if (prior !== undefined && named.get(prior.record.date) !== prior.sha256) {
       findings.push(drift(null, `nightly/${prior.sha256}.json`, 'changed-since-snapshot', named.get(prior.record.date) ?? 'unnamed', prior.sha256));
       refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'baseline-unrecorded', detail: `the record for ${prior.record.date} is not the one that night's firing names, so nothing is compared against it` });
       prior = undefined;
     }
 
     // Re-hash: the store names every object, and each content-addressed one is checked against its name.
     const hashes: Record<string, string> = {};
     let objects = 0;
     let storeRead = true;
     try {
       for await (const object of vault.listObjects()) {
         objects += 1;
         const observed = sha256(object.bytes);
         hashes[object.path] = observed;
         if (object.named === 'content') {
           const expected = /([0-9a-f]{64})\.json$/u.exec(object.path)?.[1] ?? '';
           if (observed !== expected) findings.push(drift(object.runId, object.path, 'hash-mismatch', expected, observed));
         }
       }
     } catch (error) {
       storeRead = false;
       refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });
     }
 
     // Every record a firing names is still held.
     if (storeRead) {
       for (const [day, hash] of named) {
         if (hashes[`nightly/${hash}.json`] === undefined) findings.push(drift(null, `nightly/${hash}.json`, 'missing', hash, `missing (named by the firing of ${day})`));
       }
     }
 
     // Against the last night: a record that changed or went missing since it was hashed.
     const priorCoverage = prior?.record.integrity ?? null;
     if (storeRead && prior !== undefined && priorCoverage !== null) {
       for (const [path, expected] of Object.entries(priorCoverage.hashes)) {
         if (stillOpen(path, prior.record.startedAt)) continue;
         const observed = hashes[path];
         const runId = /^runs\/([^/]+)\//u.exec(path)?.[1] as RunId | undefined;
         if (observed === undefined) findings.push(drift(runId ?? null, path, 'missing', expected, 'missing'));
         else if (observed !== expected) findings.push(drift(runId ?? null, path, 'changed-since-snapshot', expected, observed));
       }
     }
 
     // Refs, locks, and rates, run by run.
     const records = new Map<RunId, RunRecords>();
     let ratesRead = true;
     for (const runId of await vault.listRuns()) {
       let state: RunState | null = null;
       try {
         state = await vault.readRunState(runId);
       } catch (error) {
         if (!isNoRunState(error)) {
           refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: `run ${runId}: ${describe(error)}` });
           continue;
         }
       }
       if (storeRead && state !== null) {
         for (const ref of refsOf(state)) {
           const path = pathOf(ref);
           const observed = hashes[path] ?? (ref.kind === 'base-run' ? hashes[`runs/${runId}/base-run.json`] : undefined);
           if (observed === undefined) findings.push(drift(runId, path, 'missing', ref.hash, 'missing'));
           else if (observed !== ref.hash) findings.push(drift(runId, path, 'hash-mismatch', ref.hash, observed));
         }
       }
       let run: RunRecords;
       try {
         run = await readRunRecords(vault, runId);
       } catch (error) {
         ratesRead = false;
         refusals.push({ part: 'rates', cause: 'rates-unreadable', detail: `run ${runId}: ${describe(error)}` });
         continue;
       }
       records.set(runId, run);
       // A lock governs only while its run can still move; a settled run's artifacts may be edited for the next (D-R13a-05).
       if (SETTLED.has(run.ending)) continue;
       try {
         if ((await vault.readLocks(runId)) === null) continue;
         const verdict = await vault.verifyLocks(runId);
         if (!verdict.ok) for (const t of verdict.tampered) findings.push(drift(runId, `runs/${runId}/locks: ${t.path}`, 'lock-unverified', t.expected, t.actual));
       } catch (error) {
         refusals.push({ part: 'locks', cause: 'locks-unreadable', detail: `run ${runId}: ${describe(error)}` });
       }
     }
 
     // Rates: the last snapshot's runs, derived again from the records as they now stand (D-A-NR-03).
     let rates: RateSnapshot | null = null;
     if (ratesRead) {
       const priorRates = priorCoverage?.rates ?? null;
       if (priorRates !== null) {
         const again: RunRecords[] = [];
         for (const runId of priorRates.runs) {
           const run = records.get(runId);
           if (run === undefined) findings.push({ kind: 'rate-drift', rate: `runs.${runId}`, snapshot: '"settled"', observed: 'absent' });
           else {
             if (!settledForRates(run)) findings.push({ kind: 'rate-drift', rate: `runs.${runId}`, snapshot: '"settled"', observed: JSON.stringify(run.ending) });
             again.push(run);
           }
         }
-        const figures = flattenFigures(deriveOutcomes(again));
+        const figures = flattenFigures(settledFigures(again));
         for (const rate of new Set([...Object.keys(priorRates.figures), ...Object.keys(figures)])) {
           const before = priorRates.figures[rate] ?? 'absent';
           const now = figures[rate] ?? 'absent';
           if (before !== now) findings.push({ kind: 'rate-drift', rate, snapshot: before, observed: now });
         }
       }
       const settled = [...records.values()].filter(settledForRates);
-      rates = { runs: settled.map((r) => r.runId).sort(), figures: flattenFigures(deriveOutcomes(settled)) };
+      rates = { runs: settled.map((r) => r.runId).sort(), figures: flattenFigures(settledFigures(settled)) };
     }
 
     // Drift that names a run is also in that run's P14 record (A-R13a-04).
     for (const finding of findings) {
       if (finding.kind !== 'record-drift' || finding.runId === null) continue;
       await vault.recordDecision({
         runId: finding.runId, taskId: null, station: null, decidedAt: new Date(this.#now()).toISOString(), occurrence: randomUUID(),
         decision: { cause: 'record-drift', decidedBy: 'nightly', record: finding.record, problem: finding.problem, expected: finding.expected, observed: finding.observed },
         collectedBy: 'runtime',
       });
     }
 
     const integrity: IntegrityCoverage = {
       objects, hashes, comparedWith: prior === undefined ? 'none' : { record: prior.sha256 }, rates,
     };
     return { findings, refusals, integrity, security: null };
   }
 
   async #security(policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule, date: string, intake: Intake): Promise<Part> {
     const { target } = this.#options;
     const findings: NightlyFinding[] = [];
     const refusals: NightlyRefusal[] = [];
     const nightly = policy.triggers.nightly;
     const egress: Array<SecurityCoverage['egress'][number]> = [];
     const coverage = (commit: { branch: string; commit: string } | null, ran: SecurityCoverage['ran'], audited: SecurityCoverage['audited']): SecurityCoverage => ({ target: commit, ran, audited, egress });
     if (nightly === undefined) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'policy', cause: 'no-policy', detail: 'the policy names no nightly block, so no scanner or advisory host is granted' });
       return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(null, [], null) };
     }
     if (target === null) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'target', cause: 'no-target', detail: 'this host names no default branch to scan' });
       return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(null, [], null) };
     }
     const dir = join(this.#options.workspaces.root, 'nightly', `${schedule.id}-${date}-${randomUUID()}`);
     const tree = join(dir, 'tree');
     const ran: Array<SecurityCoverage['ran'][number]> = [];
     let commit: string;
     let files: readonly string[];
     try {
       commit = await resolveCommit(target.checkout, `refs/heads/${target.branch}`);
       await mkdir(tree, { recursive: true });
       files = (await materialize(target.checkout, commit, tree)).files;
     } catch (error) {
       await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
       refusals.push({ part: 'target', cause: 'target-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });
       return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(null, [], null) };
     }
     const where = { branch: target.branch, commit };
     let audited: SecurityCoverage['audited'] = null;
     try {
       for (const [index, selected] of nightly.scanners.entries()) {
         const outcome = await this.#scan(tree, join(dir, `scan-${String(index)}`), selected);
         if (outcome.egress !== null) egress.push({ part: selected.kind, log: outcome.egress });
         if (!outcome.ok) {
           refusals.push({ part: selected.kind, cause: 'scan-failed', detail: outcome.reason });
           continue;
         }
         ran.push(selected.kind);
         for (const f of outcome.findings) {
           findings.push(await this.#finding(intake, policy, schedule, date, selected.kind, f.rule, f.location.path, f.location.line, f.fingerprint));
         }
       }
       const audit = await this.#audit(tree, files, join(dir, 'audit'), nightly.advisoryHost);
       if (audit.egress !== null) egress.push({ part: 'dependency-audit', log: audit.egress });
       if (!audit.ok) refusals.push({ part: 'dependency-audit', cause: audit.cause, detail: audit.detail });
       else {
         ran.push('dependency-audit');
         audited = { lockfile: audit.auditor.lockfile, stack: audit.auditor.stack };
         for (const f of audit.findings) {
           findings.push(await this.#finding(intake, policy, schedule, date, 'dependency-audit', f.rule, audit.auditor.lockfile, f.line, f.fingerprint));
         }
       }
     } finally {
       await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
     }
     return { findings, refusals, integrity: null, security: coverage(where, ran, audited) };
   }
 
   /** One R17 scanner over the tree, as `verify` runs it: a copy, `deny-all`, the pinned image, the tree the only data mount. */
   async #scan(
     tree: string, scratch: string, selected: NightlyScanner,
   ): Promise<({ ok: true; findings: readonly Finding[] } | { ok: false; reason: string }) & { egress: EgressLog | null }> {
     const scanner = scannerFor(selected.kind, this.#options.scanners);
     const check: SecurityCheckSpec = { id: `nightly-${selected.kind}`, kind: selected.kind, required: true, timeoutMs: NIGHTLY_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS, image: scanner.image, config: selected.config };
     const scanned = await scanTree({
       sandbox: this.#options.sandbox, tree, scratch: { dir: scratch, tree: join(scratch, 'tree'), config: join(scratch, 'config') },
       // Nightly installs nothing into the tree it scans, so nothing in it is the install's and all of it is scanned.
       check, scanner, limits: this.#options.limits, user: this.#options.workspaces.user, installed: [],
     });
     if (!scanned.ok) return { ok: false, reason: scanned.reason, egress: scanned.at === 'exec' ? scanned.egress : null };
     if (scanned.result.findings === null) return { ok: false, reason: `the scanner exited ${String(scanned.result.exitCode)} without a whole report`, egress: scanned.egress };
     return { ok: true, findings: scanned.result.findings, egress: scanned.egress };
   }
 
   /** The lockfile's audit in its own sandbox: the manifest and lockfile alone, read-only, egress to the advisory host alone (D-R13a-06). */
   async #audit(
     tree: string, files: readonly string[], scratch: string, advisoryHost: string,
   ): Promise<({ ok: true; auditor: DependencyAuditor; findings: readonly AuditFinding[] } | { ok: false; cause: NightlyRefusal['cause']; detail: string }) & { egress: EgressLog | null }> {
     const root = new Set(files.filter((f) => !f.includes('/')));
     const auditors = this.#options.auditors ?? AUDITORS;
     const lockfiles = [...auditors.map((a) => a.lockfile), ...UNSUPPORTED_LOCKFILES].filter((name) => root.has(name));
     // Lockfiles of two stacks: auditing whichever is listed first would leave the other unaudited (gemini-3).
     if (lockfiles.length > 1) return { ok: false, cause: 'unsupported-stack', detail: `the default branch holds ${lockfiles.join(' and ')}; which stack it uses is not decided`, egress: null };
     const auditor = auditors.find((a) => root.has(a.lockfile));
     if (auditor === undefined) {
       const other = UNSUPPORTED_LOCKFILES.find((name) => root.has(name));
       return other === undefined
         ? { ok: false, cause: 'no-lockfile', detail: 'the default branch holds no lockfile at its root', egress: null }
         : { ok: false, cause: 'unsupported-stack', detail: `${other} is a lockfile no audit covers`, egress: null };
     }
     await mkdir(scratch, { recursive: true });
     for (const name of auditor.files) if (root.has(name)) await copyFile(join(tree, name), join(scratch, name));
     const lockfileText = await readFile(join(scratch, auditor.lockfile), 'utf8');
     const spec: SandboxSpec = {
       image: auditor.image,
       mounts: { workspace: { source: scratch, target: '/workspace', mode: 'ro' }, others: [] },
       egress: { mode: 'allowlist', allow: [advisoryHost] },
       limits: this.#options.limits === null ? { cpus: 0, memoryMb: 0, pids: 0, wallClockMs: NIGHTLY_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS } : { ...this.#options.limits, wallClockMs: NIGHTLY_STEP_TIMEOUT_MS },
       user: { ...this.#options.workspaces.user },
     };
     let handle: SandboxHandle;
     try {
       handle = await this.#options.sandbox.provision(spec);
     } catch (error) {
       return { ok: false, cause: 'audit-failed', detail: `its sandbox could not be provisioned: ${describe(error)}`, egress: null };
     }
     let outcome: { ok: true; auditor: DependencyAuditor; findings: readonly AuditFinding[] } | { ok: false; cause: NightlyRefusal['cause']; detail: string };
     let egress: EgressLog;
     try {
       const exec = await this.#options.sandbox.exec(handle, [...auditor.invocation], { env: { HOME: '/tmp', COREPACK_ENABLE_DOWNLOAD_PROMPT: '0' } });
       const parsed = auditor.parse(exec.stdout, exec.exitCode, lockfileText);
       if (parsed.ok) outcome = { ok: true, auditor, findings: parsed.findings };
       else {
         outcome = parsed.unreachable
           ? { ok: false, cause: 'advisory-unreachable', detail: `the audit could not reach ${advisoryHost} (exit ${String(exec.exitCode)})` }
           : { ok: false, cause: 'audit-failed', detail: `the audit exited ${String(exec.exitCode)} without a whole report` };
       }
     } catch (error) {
       outcome = { ok: false, cause: 'audit-failed', detail: describe(error) };
     } finally {
       // Read after the audit whatever it did, so the record holds what the proxy decided (D-P14-04).
       ({ egress } = await this.#options.sandbox.destroy(handle));
     }
     // A report counts only where the proxy saw the advisory host reached; a grant is permission, not contact (codex-2).
     const reached = egress.kind === 'proxied' && egress.connections.some((c) => c.verdict !== 'refused' && c.host === advisoryHost);
     if (outcome.ok && !reached) {
       return { ok: false, cause: 'advisory-unreachable', detail: `the audit's proxy recorded no connection to ${advisoryHost}`, egress };
     }
     return { ...outcome, egress };
   }
 
   /** A finding, with the candidate it became or why it became none (D-R13a-07). */
   async #finding(
     intake: Intake, policy: Policy, schedule: TriggerSchedule, date: string,
     scanner: SecurityCoverage['ran'][number], rule: string, path: string, line: number | null, fingerprint: string,
   ): Promise<NightlyFinding> {
     // Checked before any record holds them: an unlocated finding is checked with a stand-in line, which is not kept.
     const checked = checkFields('nightly-finding', { scanner, rule, path, line: String(line ?? 1) });
     if (!checked.ok) return { kind: 'security-unrecordable', scanner, defects: checked.defects };
     if (!FINGERPRINT.test(fingerprint)) return { kind: 'security-unrecordable', scanner, defects: [{ field: 'fingerprint', problem: 'out-of-class' }] };
     const candidate = line === null
       ? { none: 'unlocated' as const }
       : await intake.recordFinding(policy, schedule, `${date}:${scanner}:${fingerprint}`, { scanner, rule, path, line: String(line) });
     return { kind: 'security', scanner, rule, path, line, fingerprint, candidate };
   }
 }

 /** The checkout the walk reads and the branch runs merge into: the target's default branch. */
@@ -232,156 +233,169 @@ const closes = (entries: readonly ObservationEntry[]): boolean => entries.some((
 /**
  * The walk (D-A-OB-02, D-R8a-06 to D-R8a-10). Reversals are recorded before
  * any window is closed, so a revert the walk reaches counts against a window
  * that closes in the same walk (D-R8a-07).
  */
 export async function walkHistory(vault: Vault, target: ObserveTarget | null, policy: Policy, now: () => number): Promise<Walked> {
   const refusals: NightlyRefusal[] = [];
   const coverage = { branch: target?.branch ?? null, from: null as string | null, to: null as string | null, walked: 0, opened: [] as OpenedChange[], closed: [] as string[], signals: 0 };
   const done = (): Walked => ({ refusals, observation: { ...coverage } });
   const windowMs = policy.triggers.observe?.windowMs;
   if (windowMs === undefined) {
     refusals.push({ part: 'policy', cause: 'no-window', detail: 'the policy names no observation window, so no window can close' });
     return done();
   }
   if (target === null) {
     refusals.push({ part: 'target', cause: 'no-target', detail: 'this host names no default branch to walk' });
     return done();
   }
   const { checkout, branch } = target;
   // A window waits out the policy's delivery age too: a signed event older than that is refused as stale, so once it
   // has passed, every failure GitHub could still deliver about the window has arrived or never will (codex-4).
   const graceMs = policy.triggers.maxDeliveryAgeMs ?? 0;
   const stamp = (): string => new Date(now()).toISOString();
 
   // What the store already holds: every merge, every observation, and every earlier walk.
   const merged = await mergedRuns(vault);
   const runObservations = new Map<RunId, readonly ObservationEntry[]>();
   for (const run of merged.values()) runObservations.set(run.runId, await vault.readObservations(run.runId));
   const beside = await vault.readObservations(null);
   const closedBaseline = new Set(beside.filter((e) => e.record.signal.kind === 'window-closed').map((e) => e.record.change));
   const walks = (await vault.readNightly()).filter((e) => e.record.observation !== null);
   const lastTo = [...walks].reverse().find((e) => e.record.observation?.to != null)?.record.observation?.to ?? null;
   const openedBefore = new Map<string, OpenedChange>();
   for (const w of walks) for (const o of w.record.observation?.opened ?? []) if (!closedBaseline.has(o.change)) openedBefore.set(o.change, o);
   // A provider-reported landing time, where a push was recorded for a commit (D-R8a-08).
   const pushed = new Map<string, SourcedTime>();
   for (const e of [...beside, ...[...runObservations.values()].flat()]) if (e.record.signal.kind === 'pushed') pushed.set(e.record.signal.commit, e.record.signal.at);
 
   const openRuns = [...merged.values()].filter((r) => !closes(runObservations.get(r.runId) ?? []));
   let head: string;
   let commits: BranchCommit[];
   // When the head was read: a window closes only if it had closed by then, so no close covers history the walk did not read (codex-2).
   let headAt: number;
   try {
     head = await resolveCommit(checkout, `refs/heads/${branch}`);
     headAt = now();
     let from = lastTo;
     if (from === null) {
       // The first walk starts before the oldest merge still observed, so a revert of it already on the branch is found (D-R8a-10).
       const oldest = [...openRuns].sort((a, b) => a.mergedAt.localeCompare(b.mergedAt))[0];
       from = oldest === undefined ? head : ((await commitOf(checkout, oldest.mergeCommit)).parents[0] ?? null);
     }
     coverage.from = from;
     // The walk's start and every change still watched must be on the branch's own first-parent history. One an
     // ancestor only through a merge's second parent, or dropped by a reset, is history rewritten under the record,
     // never a change left to close clean (codex-1, gemini-2).
     const chain = await firstParentChain(checkout, head);
     if (from !== null && !chain.has(from)) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-rewritten', detail: `${from}, where the walk last stopped, is not on ${branch}'s first-parent history at ${head}` });
       return done();
     }
     const dropped = [...openRuns.map((r) => r.mergeCommit), ...openedBefore.keys()].filter((c) => !chain.has(c));
     if (dropped.length > 0) {
       refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-rewritten', detail: `changes still watched are no longer on ${branch}'s first-parent history at ${head}: ${dropped.join(', ')}` });
       return done();
     }
     commits = from === head ? [] : await firstParents(checkout, from, head);
   } catch (error) {
     refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });
     return done();
   }
 
   try {
     // Changes already open precede the range; one inside it opens when the walk passes it.
     const inRange = new Set(commits.map((c) => c.commit));
     const open: OpenChange[] = [];
     const pending = new Map<string, OpenChange>();
     for (const r of openRuns) {
       const change: OpenChange = { change: r.mergeCommit, runId: r.runId, opened: { at: r.mergedAt, source: 'provider-reported' } };
       if (inRange.has(r.mergeCommit)) pending.set(r.mergeCommit, change);
       else open.push(change);
     }
     for (const o of openedBefore.values()) open.push({ change: o.change, runId: null, opened: { at: o.firstSeen, source: 'runtime' } });
     const closedRuns = new Set([...merged.values()].filter((r) => !openRuns.includes(r)).map((r) => r.mergeCommit));
 
     for (const commit of commits) {
       if (open.length > 0) {
         const diff: CommitDiff = await commitDiff(checkout, commit);
         for (const change of open) {
           change.inverse ??= await changeInverse(checkout, await commitOf(checkout, change.change));
           const found = classifyReversal(change.inverse, diff);
           if (found.kind === 'none') continue;
           const at = pushed.get(commit.commit) ?? { at: commit.committedAt, source: 'committer-claimed' as const };
           const signal: ObservationSignal = found.kind === 'rollback'
             ? { kind: 'rollback', commit: commit.commit, at, committedAt: commit.committedAt, match: found.match }
             : { kind: 'partial-reversal', commit: commit.commit, at, hunks: found.hunks, of: found.of };
           await vault.recordObservation({ runId: change.runId, change: change.change, signal, recordedAt: stamp(), collectedBy: 'runtime' });
           coverage.signals += 1;
         }
       }
       coverage.walked += 1;
       const run = pending.get(commit.commit);
-      if (run !== undefined) open.push(run);
+      if (run !== undefined) {
+        open.push(run);
+        // The code findings this run's merged change resolved, if its evidence shows it covered them (R28). One
+        // whose history cannot be read stays open, and the walk names it (I5).
+        if (run.runId !== null) {
+          try {
+            const { unreadable } = await resolveCodeFindings(vault, checkout, run.runId, stamp);
+            if (unreadable.length > 0) refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-unreadable', detail: `the history of findings ${unreadable.join(', ')} could not be read against run ${run.runId}, so each stays open` });
+          } catch (error) {
+            // The store, not the history: the walk goes on, and every finding stays open until a walk can read it.
+            refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: `the findings run ${run.runId} could resolve could not be read, so each stays open: ${describe(error)}` });
+          }
+        }
+      }
       else if (!merged.has(commit.commit) && !closedRuns.has(commit.commit) && !closedBaseline.has(commit.commit)) {
         // A change no run made: the baseline. Its window opens when the walk first sees it (D-R8a-09).
         const opened: OpenedChange = { change: commit.commit, firstSeen: stamp(), committedAt: commit.committedAt, leadTime: await leadTimeOf(checkout, commit) };
         coverage.opened.push(opened);
         open.push({ change: opened.change, runId: null, opened: { at: opened.firstSeen, source: 'runtime' } });
       }
     }
     coverage.to = head;
 
     // Windows close after every reversal is recorded (D-R8a-07).
     const firstSeen = new Map<string, OpenedChange>([...openedBefore, ...coverage.opened.map((o) => [o.change, o] as const)]);
     for (const change of open) {
       if (headAt < Date.parse(change.opened.at) + windowMs + graceMs) continue;
       if (change.runId !== null) {
         // A run's plan is read and recorded before its window closes; one that cannot be read keeps it open (D-A-OB-14).
         const planned = await observePlan(vault, target.checks ?? null, checkout, head, { ...change, runId: change.runId }, stamp);
         if (typeof planned === 'object') {
           refusals.push(planned);
           continue;
         }
       }
       const committedAt = (await commitOf(checkout, change.change)).committedAt;
       const record: ObservationRecord = {
         runId: change.runId, change: change.change, recordedAt: stamp(), collectedBy: 'runtime',
         signal: { kind: 'window-closed', at: { at: stamp(), source: 'runtime' }, windowMs, graceMs, opened: change.opened, committedAt, leadTime: change.runId === null ? (firstSeen.get(change.change)?.leadTime ?? null) : null },
       };
       const sha = await vault.recordObservation(record);
       coverage.closed.push(change.change);
       if (change.runId !== null) {
         await vault.recordDecision({
           runId: change.runId, taskId: null, station: 'observe', decidedAt: stamp(), occurrence: randomUUID(),
           decision: { cause: 'observation-closed', decidedBy: 'observe', observation: sha, windowMs }, collectedBy: 'runtime',
         });
       }
     }
   } catch (error) {
     // The range is walked again next time; a reversal already recorded is the same signal, counted once (D-R8a-07).
     coverage.to = null;
     refusals.push({ part: 'history', cause: 'history-unreadable', detail: describe(error) });
   }
   try {
     await leaveObserve(vault, merged);
   } catch (error) {
     refusals.push({ part: 'store', cause: 'store-unreadable', detail: `a run whose window closed could not be moved out of observe: ${describe(error)}` });
   }
   return done();
 }

  * watched is moved out, phase `exiting` at `observe` (D-R8a-04). Run after
  * every walk, so a close whose state commit failed is finished by the next.
  */

 /** Reads one run's records. A run with no state is one refused at admission, or one with nothing yet. */
 export async function readRunRecords(vault: Vault, runId: RunId): Promise<RunRecords> {
   const decisions: EnforcementDecision[] = [];
   for (const ref of await vault.readDecisions(runId)) decisions.push(decode(await vault.read(ref)) as EnforcementDecision);
   decisions.sort((a, b) => a.decidedAt.localeCompare(b.decidedAt));
 
   // Integration before state: a merge happens only after its grant is committed, so a state read
   // after a merge was seen holds that grant, and a merge is never paired with an older state.
   const integration: IntegrationRecord[] = await readIntegration(vault, runId);
 
   let state: RunState;
   try {
     state = await vault.readRunState(runId);
   } catch (error) {
     // Only absence is absence; a state that cannot be read is a fault, never a run with no evidence (I5).
     if (!isNoRunState(error)) throw error;
     const refusal = decisions.find((d) => d.decision.cause === 'admission-refused');
     return {
       runId,
       ending: refusal === undefined ? 'unfinished' : 'refused',
       requestedLevel: null,
       stack: null,
       startedAt: refusal?.decidedAt ?? null,
       mergedAt: null,
       mergeCommit: null,
+      lineCoverage: false,
       approvalsSpent: 0,
       tasks: [],
       decisions,
       usage: [],
       cost: null,
       reviewGrade: 'no-review',
       critiques: [],
       observations: [],
     };
   }
 
   const admission = decode(await vault.read(state.admission)) as AdmissionRecord;
   const merged = integration.find((r) => r.kind === 'merged');
   const usage = await readUsage(vault, state);
 
   const bundles: EvidenceBundle[] = [];
   for (const ref of state.evidenceRefs) bundles.push(decode(await vault.read(ref)) as EvidenceBundle);
 
   const tasks = Object.entries(state.tasks).map(([id, status]): TaskRecord => {
     const taskId = id as TaskId;
     const own = bundles.filter((b) => b.taskId === taskId);
     // The line builds again only after a failed verdict, so every bundle before the last failed;
     // the last carries the task's status, the runtime's verdict and nothing else (I2).
     const attempts = own.map((b, i): AttemptRecord => ({
       outcome: i === own.length - 1 && status === 'passed' ? 'passed' : 'failed',
       claimMismatch: b.claimEvidenceDiff.length > 0,
     }));
     const calls = usage.filter((r) => r.taskId === taskId);
     const first = calls.find((r) => r.attempt === 1) ?? calls[0];
     const spent = state.attempts[taskId];
     return {
       taskId,
       station: calls[0]?.station ?? null,
       status,
       iterations: spent === undefined ? 0 : gatedIterations(spent),
       attempts,
       firstModel: first === undefined ? null : modelKey(first.model),
       models: [...new Set(calls.map((r) => modelKey(r.model)))].sort(),
     };
   });
 
   const ending: RunEnding = merged !== undefined
     ? 'merged'
     : state.cancelled !== null
       ? 'cancelled'
       : state.halted !== null
         ? 'halted'
         : state.approvals.some((g) => g.key === approvalKey('integrate', levelOf(admission.run.requestedLevel, state.narrowed)) && g.usedAt !== null)
           // The grant for the last exit is spent in the commit that ends a passed run, as `runStanding` reads it (D-P9-08).
           ? 'passed'
           : Object.values(state.tasks).includes('parked') || Object.values(state.drafts).some((d) => d.parked)
           ? 'parked'
           : 'unfinished';
 
   return {
     runId,
     ending,
     requestedLevel: admission.run.requestedLevel,
     stack: recordedStack(admission),
     startedAt: admission.run.createdAt,
     mergedAt: merged?.kind === 'merged' ? merged.mergedAt : null,
     mergeCommit: merged?.kind === 'merged' ? merged.mergeCommit : null,
     // The drive merges before it spends the integrate grant (D-I1b-01), so on a merged run that grant
     // was consumed by the merge, spent in state or not yet.
     approvalsSpent: state.approvals.filter((g) => g.usedAt !== null || (merged !== undefined && g.key === approvalKey('integrate', levelOf(admission.run.requestedLevel, state.narrowed)))).length,
     tasks,
     decisions,
     usage,
     cost: costTotals(usage).run,
     reviewGrade: reviewGradeOf(state),
     critiques: await critiquesOf(vault, state),
     observations: (await vault.readObservations(runId)).map((e) => e.record),
+    // A bundle stored before R28 names no lines.
+    lineCoverage: bundles.some((b) => (b.tamper.changedLines ?? null) !== null),
   };
 }

 /** Every run the Vault names (A-R2-02), read whole. The store picks the runs, never a caller. */
 export async function outcomes(vault: Vault, window: OutcomeWindow = {}): Promise<Outcomes> {
   const runs: RunRecords[] = [];

     const run = await readRunRecords(vault, runId);

+  // A run's findings are in the window when its run is; a nightly one when the day it covers is (R28).
+  const inRuns = new Set(runs.map((r) => r.runId));
+  const decided = await outcomesByFinding(vault);
+  const findings = (await raisedFindings(vault))
+    .filter((f) => (f.runId !== null ? inRuns.has(f.runId) : f.date !== null && inWindow(`${f.date}T00:00:00.000Z`, window)))

+/**
+ * The rates of one group of findings (R28). Resolved is a success, bounded
+ * below; dismissed and open are failures to act, bounded above.
+ */
+function findingRates(items: readonly FindingInput[], lineCoverage: boolean): FindingRates {
+  const n = items.length;
+  const count = (o: FindingInput['outcome']): number => items.filter((f) => f.outcome === o).length;
+  return {
+    raised: n,
+    resolved: !lineCoverage && items.some((f) => f.code) ? { kind: 'unmeasured', cause: 'no-line-coverage' } : successRate(count('resolved'), n),
+    dismissed: failureRate(count('dismissed'), n),
+    open: failureRate(count('open'), n),
+  };
+}
+
+function groupRates(items: readonly FindingInput[], key: (f: FindingInput) => string, lineCoverage: boolean): Keyed<FindingRates> {
+  const groups = new Map<string, FindingInput[]>();
+  for (const f of items) groups.set(key(f), [...(groups.get(key(f)) ?? []), f]);
+  return Object.fromEntries([...groups.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([k, v]) => [k, findingRates(v, lineCoverage)]));
 }

   const fpModel = new Tally();
   const fpStack = new Tally();

===== packages/api/test/findings.test.ts =====
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdir, mkdtemp, rm, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { RunId } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import {
  deriveOutcomes, findingId, holdingHunk, mapLine, parseFindingId, parseHunkRanges, readSeatFindings, type FindingInput, type HunkRange, type RunRecords,
} from '../src/index.js';
import { pathHunks } from '../src/history.js';

const made: string[] = [];
afterEach(async () => {
  for (const dir of made.splice(0)) await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

async function tree(files: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): Promise<string> {
  const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'r28-tree-'));
  made.push(dir);
  for (const [path, text] of Object.entries(files)) {
    await mkdir(dirname(join(dir, path)), { recursive: true });
    await writeFile(join(dir, path), text);
  }
  return dir;
}

describe('a finding\'s id (R28)', () => {
  it('names its source record and index, and reads back as the same finding', () => {
    const hash = 'a'.repeat(64);
    const review = { source: { kind: 'review' as const, record: { runId: 'run-1' as RunId, kind: 'review-findings' as const, hash } }, index: 2 };
    expect(findingId(review)).toBe(`review:run-1:${hash}:2`);
    expect(parseFindingId(findingId(review))).toEqual(review);
    const nightly = { source: { kind: 'nightly' as const, record: hash }, index: 0 };
    expect(parseFindingId(findingId(nightly))).toEqual(nightly);
  });

  it('is refused for anything else', () => {
    for (const id of ['', 'review:run-1:abc:0', `nightly:${'a'.repeat(64)}:01`, `review:../x:${'a'.repeat(64)}:0`, `critic:r:${'a'.repeat(64)}:0:1`, `other:r:${'a'.repeat(64)}:0`]) {
      expect(parseFindingId(id), id).toBeNull();
    }
  });
});

describe('a line mapped through a zero-context diff (D-R28-01)', () => {
  const h = (oldStart: number, oldLines: number, newStart: number, newLines: number): HunkRange => ({ oldStart, oldLines, newStart, newLines });

  it('shifts by every hunk wholly before it, and is unmappable where a hunk rewrote it', () => {
    // Two lines inserted after line 2, and line 8 replaced by three.
    const hunks = [h(2, 0, 3, 2), h(8, 1, 10, 3)];
    expect(mapLine(hunks, 1)).toBe(1);
    expect(mapLine(hunks, 2)).toBe(2);
    expect(mapLine(hunks, 3)).toBe(5);
    expect(mapLine(hunks, 8)).toBeNull();
    expect(mapLine(hunks, 9)).toBe(13);
    expect(mapLine([], 4)).toBe(4);
  });

  it('finds the hunk whose base side holds a line; a pure insertion holds none', () => {
    const hunks = [h(2, 0, 3, 2), h(8, 2, 10, 1)];
    expect(holdingHunk(hunks, 9)).toEqual(h(8, 2, 10, 1));
    expect(holdingHunk(hunks, 2)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(holdingHunk(hunks, 10)).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it('reads each header, a count left out being one', () => {
    expect(parseHunkRanges('diff --git a/x b/x\n@@ -3 +3,2 @@\n-a\n+b\n+c\n@@ -0,0 +7 @@\n+d\n')).toEqual([h(3, 1, 3, 2), h(0, 0, 7, 1)]);
  });

  it('is read from git, renames off, and a path either commit lacks maps nothing', async () => {
    const repo = await tree({ 'src/a.ts': 'one\ntwo\nthree\n' });
    const git = (...args: string[]): string => execFileSync('git', ['-C', repo, '-c', 'user.name=t', '-c', 'user.email=t@t', ...args], { encoding: 'utf8' }).trim();
    git('init', '-q');
    git('add', '.');
    git('commit', '-qm', 'one');
    const first = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD');
    await writeFile(join(repo, 'src/a.ts'), 'zero\none\ntwo\nTHREE\n');
    git('commit', '-qam', 'two');
    const second = git('rev-parse', 'HEAD');
    const hunks = await pathHunks(repo, first, second, 'src/a.ts');
    expect(hunks).toEqual([h(0, 0, 1, 1), h(3, 1, 4, 1)]);
    expect(mapLine(hunks ?? [], 2)).toBe(3);
    expect(mapLine(hunks ?? [], 3)).toBeNull();
    expect(await pathHunks(repo, first, second, 'src/missing.ts')).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('a review seat\'s findings, read strictly (R28)', () => {
  const result = (findings: unknown): string => JSON.stringify({ findings });

  it('locates a finding in the reviewed tree, and refuses one naming no file or line it holds', async () => {
    const dir = await tree({ 'src/a.ts': 'one\ntwo\n' });
    const read = await readSeatFindings('review', result([
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/a.ts', line: 2, note: 'kept in the claim only' },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/a.ts', line: 3 },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/gone.ts', line: 1 },
      { category: 'check-bypassed', path: 'src', line: 1 },
    ]), dir);
    expect(read).toEqual({
      readable: true,
      findings: [
        { kind: 'located', category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/a.ts', line: 2 },
        { kind: 'refused', cause: 'unlocated', defects: [] },
        { kind: 'refused', cause: 'unlocated', defects: [] },
        { kind: 'refused', cause: 'unlocated', defects: [] },
      ],
    });
    expect(JSON.stringify(read)).not.toContain('kept in the claim only');
  });

  it('refuses fields outside their rules as unrecordable, naming the field and never the value, by each seat\'s own categories', async () => {
    const dir = await tree({ 'a.ts': 'x\n' });
    const read = await readSeatFindings('review', result([
      { category: 'unchecked-input', path: 'a.ts', line: 1 },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: '../a.ts', line: 1 },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: '/etc/passwd', line: 1 },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'a.ts', line: 0 },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'a.ts', line: 1, severity: 'high' },
      'a.ts:1',
    ]), dir);
    expect(read.findings.map((f) => (f.kind === 'refused' ? [f.cause, f.defects.map((d) => `${d.field}:${d.problem}`)] : f.kind))).toEqual([
      ['unrecordable', ['category:out-of-class']],
      ['unrecordable', ['path:out-of-class']],
      ['unrecordable', ['path:out-of-class']],
      ['unrecordable', ['line:out-of-class']],
      ['unrecordable', ['severity:unknown']],
      ['unrecordable', ['category:missing', 'path:missing', 'line:missing']],
    ]);
    const security = await readSeatFindings('security', result([{ category: 'unchecked-input', path: 'a.ts', line: 1 }]), dir);
    expect(security.findings).toEqual([{ kind: 'located', category: 'unchecked-input', path: 'a.ts', line: 1 }]);
  });

  it('records a message it cannot read as unreadable, never as no findings (I5)', async () => {
    const dir = await tree({ 'a.ts': 'x\n' });
    for (const narrative of ['Looks good to me.', '{"findings": "none"}', '{"findings": [], "verdict": "pass"}', '']) {
      expect(await readSeatFindings('review', narrative, dir)).toEqual({ readable: false, findings: [] });
    }
    expect(await readSeatFindings('review', '{"findings": []}', dir)).toEqual({ readable: true, findings: [] });
  });
});

describe('finding rates (R28)', () => {
  const run = (over: Partial<RunRecords>): RunRecords => ({
    runId: 'r' as RunId, ending: 'merged', requestedLevel: 2, stack: 's', startedAt: null, mergedAt: null, mergeCommit: null, approvalsSpent: 0,
    tasks: [], decisions: [], usage: [], cost: null, reviewGrade: 'different-family', critiques: [], observations: [], lineCoverage: false, ...over,
  });
  const f = (seat: string, family: string, code: boolean, outcome: FindingInput['outcome']): FindingInput => ({ seat, family, code, outcome });

  it('are each a proportion over findings raised, per seat kind and per family', () => {
    const { findings } = deriveOutcomes([run({})], {}, null, [f('critic', 'gpt', false, 'resolved'), f('critic', 'gpt', false, 'open'), f('critic', 'claude', false, 'dismissed')]);
    expect(findings.bySeat.critic?.raised).toBe(3);
    expect(findings.bySeat.critic?.resolved).toMatchObject({ kind: 'rate', count: 1, sample: 3, bound: { side: 'lower' } });
    expect(findings.bySeat.critic?.dismissed).toMatchObject({ kind: 'rate', count: 1, sample: 3, bound: { side: 'upper' } });
    expect(findings.bySeat.critic?.open).toMatchObject({ kind: 'rate', count: 1, sample: 3 });
    expect(findings.byFamily.gpt?.raised).toBe(2);
    expect(findings.byFamily.claude?.raised).toBe(1);
  });

  it('leave a code seat\'s resolved rate unmeasured, never 0 of n, where no run recorded line coverage (D-R28-01)', () => {
    const items = [f('review', 'gpt', true, 'open'), f('review', 'gpt', true, 'dismissed')];
    expect(deriveOutcomes([run({})], {}, null, items).findings.bySeat.review?.resolved).toEqual({ kind: 'unmeasured', cause: 'no-line-coverage' });
    expect(deriveOutcomes([run({ lineCoverage: true })], {}, null, items).findings.bySeat.review?.resolved).toMatchObject({ kind: 'rate', count: 0, sample: 2 });
    expect(deriveOutcomes([run({})], {}, null, items).findings.bySeat.review?.dismissed).toMatchObject({ kind: 'rate', count: 1, sample: 2 });
  });

  it('are empty where nothing was raised', () => {
    expect(deriveOutcomes([run({})]).findings).toEqual({ bySeat: {}, byFamily: {} });
  });
});

@@ -491,90 +496,101 @@ async function telemetry(io: CliIo, t: Target, args: string[]): Promise<ExitCode
 export async function runCli(argv: readonly string[], io: CliIo): Promise<ExitCode> {
   try {
     const { values, positionals } = parseArgs({
       args: [...argv],
       strict: false,
       allowPositionals: true,
       options: { url: { type: 'string' }, token: { type: 'string' }, 'env-file': { type: 'string' }, help: { type: 'boolean' } },
     });
     const [command] = positionals;
     if (command === undefined) {
       io.out(USAGE);
       return values.help === true ? 0 : 2;
     }
     const row = COMMANDS.find((c) => c.name === command);
     if (row === undefined) throw new UsageError(`unknown command '${command}'`);
     // Help needs no server: it is printed from the table before a URL or token is asked for.
     if (values.help === true) {
       io.out(commandHelp(row));
       return 0;
     }
     // What remains once the global options are taken out: the command's own arguments.
     const own = stripGlobals(argv).slice(1);
     const t = await target({
       url: typeof values.url === 'string' ? values.url : undefined,
       token: typeof values.token === 'string' ? values.token : undefined,
       envFile: typeof values['env-file'] === 'string' ? values['env-file'] : undefined,
     }, io.env);
     switch (command) {
       case 'create':
         return await create(io, t, own);
       case 'status': {
         const [runId = ''] = one(parseOwn(command, own).positionals, ['<run id>']);
         return report(io, await call(io, t, 'GET', runPath(runId)));
       }
       case 'approve': {
         const { values: flags, positionals: named } = parseOwn(command, own);
         const [runId = '', key = ''] = one(named, ['<run id>', '<station:level>']);
         const draft = str(flags, 'draft');
         const body: ApproveBody = { key: key as ApproveBody['key'], ...(draft === undefined ? {} : { draft }) };
         return report(io, await call(io, t, 'POST', runPath(runId, 'approve'), body));
       }
       case 'reject': {
         const { values: flags, positionals: named } = parseOwn(command, own);
         const [runId = '', key = ''] = one(named, ['<run id>', '<station:level>']);
         const notes = str(flags, 'notes');
         const reason = str(flags, 'reason');
         const body: RejectBody = {
           key: key as RejectBody['key'],
           draft: required(str(flags, 'draft'), '--draft'),
           ...(notes === undefined ? {} : { notes }),
           ...(reason === undefined ? {} : { reason: reason as NonNullable<RejectBody['reason']> }),
         };
         return report(io, await call(io, t, 'POST', runPath(runId, 'reject'), body));
       }
       case 'cancel': {
         const [runId = ''] = one(parseOwn(command, own).positionals, ['<run id>']);
         return report(io, await call(io, t, 'POST', runPath(runId, 'cancel')));
       }
       case 'events': {
         const [runId = ''] = one(parseOwn(command, own).positionals, ['<run id>']);
         return await events(io, t, runId);
       }
       case 'report': {
         const [runId = ''] = one(parseOwn(command, own).positionals, ['<run id>']);
         return report(io, await call(io, t, 'GET', runPath(runId, 'report')));
       }
       case 'outcomes':
         return await outcomes(io, t, own);
+      case 'findings': {
+        const { positionals: named } = parseOwn(command, own);
+        if (named.length > 0) throw new UsageError(`findings takes no positional arguments: ${named.join(' ')}`);
+        return report(io, await call(io, t, 'GET', 'findings'));
+      }
+      case 'dismiss-finding': {
+        const { values: flags, positionals: named } = parseOwn(command, own);
+        const [id = ''] = one(named, ['<finding id>']);
+        const body: DismissBody = { cause: required(str(flags, 'cause'), '--cause') as DismissBody['cause'] };
+        return report(io, await call(io, t, 'POST', `findings/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/dismiss`, body));
+      }
       case 'telemetry':
         return await telemetry(io, t, own);
       case 'readiness': {
         const { values: flags, positionals: named } = parseOwn(command, own);
         if (named.length > 0) throw new UsageError(`readiness takes no positional arguments: ${named.join(' ')}`);
         return await readiness(io, t, str(flags, 'revision'));
       }
       default:
         throw new UsageError(`'${command}' is in the command table but has no handler`);
     }
   } catch (error) {
     if (error instanceof UsageError || (error instanceof TypeError && 'code' in error && String(error.code).startsWith('ERR_PARSE_ARGS'))) {
       io.err(error.message);
       io.err(USAGE);
       return 2;
     }
     io.err(`could not reach the API: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'unknown error'}`);
     return 2;
   }
 }
 
 /** The argument vector without `--url`, `--token`, and their values, in either `--flag value` or `--flag=value` form. */

===== packages/compiler/roles/reviewer.md =====
diff --git a/packages/compiler/roles/reviewer.md b/packages/compiler/roles/reviewer.md
index 89c1bff..e7d428d 100644
--- a/packages/compiler/roles/reviewer.md
+++ b/packages/compiler/roles/reviewer.md
@@ -26,8 +26,27 @@ the code and the evidence.
 
 ## What to say
 
-Your final message is your review. It is recorded beside the evidence for the
-person who approves the change. For each problem, name the file and what is
-wrong with it. If you found nothing wrong, say so and say what you checked. If
-you could not review something, say what and why. Do not soften a finding and
-do not invent one.
+Your final message is your result, and nothing else: one JSON object,
+
+```
+{"findings": [{"category": "...", "path": "...", "line": 1, "note": "..."}]}
+```
+
+One finding per problem. `category` is the question it answers:
+
+- `spec-mismatch`: the change does not do what the spec asks.
+- `beyond-spec`: the change does something the spec does not ask.
+- `untested-behavior`: the acceptance tests do not exercise the behavior the
+  spec describes, or the evidence does not show them failing before the
+  change and passing after it.
+- `check-bypassed`: a check passes without the behavior being there.
+- `uncovered-breakage`: the change breaks something no check covers.
+
+`path` is a file in the working directory, relative to it, and `line` a line
+in that file. `note` says what is wrong there; it is read by the person who
+approves the change, never by the runtime. If you found nothing wrong, return
+`{"findings": []}`. A finding that names no file and line the working
+directory holds is not recorded, and a message that is not this object is
+recorded as unreadable, never as nothing found. Your result decides nothing:
+the change is neither passed nor failed by it. Do not soften a finding and do
+not invent one.

 ## What to say

-there and what an attacker could do with it. If you found nothing, say so and

-not soften a finding and do not invent one.
+Your final message is your result, and nothing else: one JSON object,
+
+```
+{"findings": [{"category": "...", "path": "...", "line": 1, "note": "..."}]}
+```
+
+One finding per problem. `category` is the question it answers:
+
+- `unchecked-input`: input reaches a query, a command, a path, a template, or
+  a deserializer without being checked.
+- `exposed-secret`: a secret is written into the code, a log, an error, or a
+  response.
+- `unenforced-authorization`: an authorization rule is not enforced, or is
+  enforced on one path and not another.
+- `weakened-check`: a check is weakened, skipped, or suppressed so that it
+  passes.
+- `reachable-before-authentication`: an entry point, route, or code path is
+  reachable before authentication.
+- `unnamed-surface`: an administrative, debug, or legacy surface the spec does
+  not name.
+- `unsafe-default`: a default configuration turns a control off.
+- `weakening-combination`: settings that each look safe weaken a control
+  together.
+
+`path` is a file in the working directory, relative to it, and `line` a line
+in that file. `note` says what is wrong there and what an attacker could do
+with it; it is read by a person, never by the runtime. If you found nothing,
+return `{"findings": []}`. A finding that names no file and line the working
+directory holds is not recorded, and a message that is not this object is
+recorded as unreadable, never as nothing found. Your result decides nothing.
+Do not soften a finding and do not invent one.

 /** For P14's table: a drafting run that records each drafting decision, read back for its decider and station. */
 export async function draftingDecision(cause: 'approval-rejected' | 'draft-sent-back' | 'draft-floor-applied' | 'draft-mismatch' | 'draft-invalid'): Promise<void> {
   await withLine(`p14-${cause}-`, async (rig) => {
     const { rejectDraft } = await api();
     const seats: Seats = cause === 'draft-sent-back'
-      ? { critic: (_, attempt) => (attempt === 1 ? JSON.stringify({ findings: ['one'] }) : CLEAN) }
+      ? { critic: (_, attempt) => (attempt === 1 ? JSON.stringify({ findings: [{ path: `.drafts/${rig.runId}/spec.md`, note: 'one' }] }) : CLEAN) }
       : cause === 'draft-invalid'
         ? { spec: (_, id) => helloSpec(id, { [`.drafts/${id}/spec.md`]: '' }) }
         : {};
     const r = await drafting(rig, seats);
     awaiting(await admitAndDrive(r), 'spec:1', `a drafted spec for ${cause}`);
     const vault = (await r.components()).vault;
     const attempt = lastAttempt(await rig.state(), 'spec');
     let found: EnforcementDecision | undefined;
     if (cause === 'approval-rejected') {
       const rejected = await rejectDraft({ runId: rig.runId, key: 'spec:1', draft: attempt.sha256 ?? '', rejectedBy: 'conformance', vault });
       if (!rejected.ok) throw new Error(`P14: the rejection was refused as ${rejected.reason}`);
       const rejections = (await decisionsOf(vault, rig.runId)).filter((d) => d.decision.cause === 'approval-rejected');
       // Bound to the draft rejected, and recorded once.
       const [only] = rejections;
       if (rejections.length !== 1 || only?.decision.cause !== 'approval-rejected' || only.decision.draft.sha256 !== attempt.sha256 || only.decision.draft.attempt !== attempt.attempt) {
         throw new Error(`P14: the rejection of ${String(attempt.sha256)} was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(rejections.map((d) => d.decision))}`);
       }
       found = only;
     } else if (cause === 'draft-mismatch' || cause === 'draft-invalid') {
       found = await refusedApproval(rig, vault, cause === 'draft-mismatch' ? '0'.repeat(64) : attempt.sha256, cause);
     } else {
       const decisions = await decisionsOf(vault, rig.runId);
       found = decisions.find((d) => d.decision.cause === cause);
       if (found === undefined) throw new Error(`P14: no ${cause} decision was recorded; recorded ${JSON.stringify(decisions.map((d) => d.decision.cause))}`);
     }
     const decider = cause === 'approval-rejected' || cause === 'draft-mismatch' || cause === 'draft-invalid' ? 'approval' : 'line';
     if (found.decision.decidedBy !== decider || found.station !== 'spec' || found.runId !== rig.runId) throw new Error(`P14: a ${cause} decision was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(found)}`);
   });
 }
