===== packages/conformance/src/registry/findings-http.ts =====
/**
 * R28: a person dismisses a finding, with a cause and no text. The runtime
 * lists every located finding and whether it is open; only a person's request,
 * carrying the local token, gives one an outcome, and a finding has one outcome
 * for good. The CLI is a client of that route and sends nothing else (I9).
 *
 * Each assertion drives the real HTTP server over a real filesystem Vault, and
 * the real CLI over an injected fetch.
 */
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import type { FindingSummary } from '@olympus-ai/api';
import type { ModelFamily, SeatIdentity } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import type { NightlyRecord, Vault } from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import { runtime } from '../kit/assert.js';
import type { LocalAssertion } from '../kit/types.js';
import { drafting } from './drafting.js';
import { withLine } from './line.js';

const api = async () => import('@olympus-ai/api');
const core = async () => import('@olympus-ai/core');

const TOKEN = 'conformance-token-of-at-least-thirty-two-chars';
const NOTE = 'R28-DISMISSAL-NOTE-fixed-by-hand';

function seat(family: string): SeatIdentity {
  return { role: null, driver: 'scripted', model: { provider: family, family: family as ModelFamily, model: `${family}-model`, version: '1' } };
}

/** A nightly record whose reviewer seat raised one located tree finding, stored in `vault`. */
async function seedFinding(vault: Vault): Promise<void> {
  const { TREE_CATEGORIES } = await core();
  const category = TREE_CATEGORIES[0];
  const record: NightlyRecord = {
    schedule: 'conformance', template: 'nightly-tree-review', date: '2026-11-20',
    startedAt: '2026-11-20T02:00:00.000Z', endedAt: '2026-11-20T02:01:00.000Z',
    outcome: { kind: 'completed' }, findings: [], integrity: null, security: null, observation: null, collectedBy: 'runtime',
    review: {
      subject: null, role: null, usage: null, egress: null, claim: null,
      seat: { reviewer: seat('claude'), authors: [], independence: 'no-author', level: 1 },
      findings: [{ kind: 'tree', category, path: 'src/index.ts', line: 1, candidate: { none: 'budget' } }],
    },
  };
  await vault.recordNightly(record);
}

async function lookup(address: string, token: string | null, method: string, path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<{ status: number; json: unknown }> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'content-type': 'application/json' };
  if (token !== null) headers.authorization = `Bearer ${token}`;
  const res = await fetch(`${address}${path}`, body === undefined ? { method, headers } : { method, headers, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
  return { status: res.status, json: (await res.json().catch(() => null)) };
}

export const FINDING_DISMISSAL_IS_A_PERSONS: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I2.finding-dismissal-is-a-persons',
  title:
    'GET /findings lists a located finding as open; POST /findings/:id/dismiss, with the token, records a dismissal with a cause from the closed set and no text, 404 for an id naming no finding, 409 for a second outcome; ' +
    'a body carrying text or an unknown cause writes nothing, and a request without the token is refused',
  timeoutMs: 120_000,
  run: async () => {
    await withLine('r28-dismiss-', async (rig) => {
      const { createApiServer } = await api();
      const { LocalVault } = await import('@olympus-ai/vault');
      const vault = new LocalVault(rig.dirs);
      await seedFinding(vault);
      const policyFile = join(rig.dirs.artifacts, '..', 'policy.yaml');
      await writeFile(policyFile, '{}');
      const r = await drafting(rig);
      const server = createApiServer({ components: await r.components(), token: TOKEN, principal: 'conformance', policyFile, rolesDirectory: rig.roles });
      const address = await server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1');
      const stored = async (): Promise<string[]> => (await vault.readFindingOutcomes()).map((e) => (e.record.outcome.kind === 'dismissed' ? e.record.outcome.cause : e.record.outcome.kind));
      try {
        const listed = await lookup(address, TOKEN, 'GET', '/findings');
        const open = listed.json as FindingSummary[];
        if (listed.status !== 200 || !Array.isArray(open) || open.length !== 1) throw new Error(`I2: GET /findings answered ${String(listed.status)} ${JSON.stringify(listed.json)}, not the one seeded finding`);
        const [first] = open;
        if (first?.outcome.kind !== 'open') throw new Error(`I2: a finding with no outcome listed as ${JSON.stringify(first?.outcome)}, not open`);
        const id = first.id;
        const path = `/findings/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/dismiss`;

        const texted = await lookup(address, TOKEN, 'POST', path, { cause: 'not-a-defect', note: NOTE });
        if (texted.status !== 400) throw new Error(`I2: a dismissal carrying text answered ${String(texted.status)}, not 400`);
        if ((await stored()).length !== 0) throw new Error('I2: a refused dismissal that carried text wrote an outcome');
        const unknownCause = await lookup(address, TOKEN, 'POST', path, { cause: 'the reviewer is wrong' });
        if (unknownCause.status !== 400) throw new Error(`I2: a dismissal with a cause outside the closed set answered ${String(unknownCause.status)}, not 400`);
        if ((await stored()).length !== 0) throw new Error('I2: a refused dismissal with an unknown cause wrote an outcome');
        const noToken = await lookup(address, null, 'POST', path, { cause: 'duplicate' });
        if (noToken.status !== 401) throw new Error(`I2: a dismissal without the token answered ${String(noToken.status)}, not 401`);
        if ((await stored()).length !== 0) throw new Error('I2: a dismissal without the token wrote an outcome');

        const absent = `nightly:${createHash('sha256').update('no such record').digest('hex')}:0`;
        const missing = await lookup(address, TOKEN, 'POST', `/findings/${encodeURIComponent(absent)}/dismiss`, { cause: 'duplicate' });
        if (missing.status !== 404) throw new Error(`I2: a dismissal of an id naming no finding answered ${String(missing.status)}, not 404`);

        const done = await lookup(address, TOKEN, 'POST', path, { cause: 'duplicate' });
        const summary = done.json as FindingSummary;
        if (done.status !== 200 || summary.outcome.kind !== 'dismissed' || summary.outcome.cause !== 'duplicate') throw new Error(`I2: the dismissal answered ${String(done.status)} ${JSON.stringify(done.json)}`);
        const after = (await lookup(address, TOKEN, 'GET', '/findings')).json as FindingSummary[];
        const now = after.find((f) => f.id === id);
        if (now?.outcome.kind !== 'dismissed' || now.outcome.cause !== 'duplicate') throw new Error(`I2: the dismissed finding lists as ${JSON.stringify(now?.outcome)}`);

        const again = await lookup(address, TOKEN, 'POST', path, { cause: 'not-a-defect' });
        if (again.status !== 409) throw new Error(`I2: a second dismissal answered ${String(again.status)}, not 409`);
        const held = await stored();
        if (held.length !== 1 || held[0] !== 'duplicate') throw new Error(`I2: the store holds ${JSON.stringify(held)}, not one dismissal for duplicate`);
        if (JSON.stringify(await vault.readFindingOutcomes()).includes(NOTE)) throw new Error('I2: a dismissal holds text a person sent');
      } finally {
        await server.close();
      }
    });
  },
});

export const FINDING_DISMISSAL_THROUGH_THE_CLI: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I2.finding-dismissal-through-the-cli',
  title:
    'the CLI\'s dismiss-finding <id> --cause <cause> sends POST findings/<id>/dismiss with the body {"cause":...} and nothing else; without --cause it is a usage error that sends nothing',
  run: async () => {
    const { runCli } = await import('olympus-ai');
    const sent: Array<{ url: string; method: string; body: string | null }> = [];
    const capture = ((input: Parameters<typeof fetch>[0], init?: Parameters<typeof fetch>[1]) => {
      sent.push({ url: input instanceof Request ? input.url : input.toString(), method: init?.method ?? 'GET', body: typeof init?.body === 'string' ? init.body : null });
      return Promise.resolve(new Response('{}', { status: 200, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } }));
    }) as typeof fetch;
    const out: string[] = [];
    const err: string[] = [];
    const io = { env: {}, fetch: capture, out: (l: string) => out.push(l), err: (l: string) => err.push(l) };
    const base = ['--url', 'http://127.0.0.1:9', '--token', TOKEN];
    const id = `review:run-1:${'a'.repeat(64)}:0`;

    const code = await runCli(['dismiss-finding', id, '--cause', 'not-a-defect', ...base], io);
    if (code !== 0) throw new Error(`I2: dismiss-finding exited ${String(code)}: ${err.join(' ')}`);
    const [one] = sent;
    if (sent.length !== 1 || one === undefined) throw new Error(`I2: dismiss-finding sent ${String(sent.length)} requests, not one`);
    if (one.method !== 'POST' || !one.url.endsWith(`/findings/${encodeURIComponent(id)}/dismiss`)) throw new Error(`I2: dismiss-finding sent ${one.method} ${one.url}`);
    if (one.body !== '{"cause":"not-a-defect"}') throw new Error(`I2: dismiss-finding sent the body ${String(one.body)}, not exactly the cause`);

    sent.length = 0;
    const missing = await runCli(['dismiss-finding', id, ...base], io);
    if (missing !== 2) throw new Error(`I2: dismiss-finding without --cause exited ${String(missing)}, not the usage error 2`);
    if (sent.length !== 0) throw new Error('I2: dismiss-finding without --cause sent a request');
  },
});

===== packages/conformance/src/registry/findings-resolution.ts =====
/**
 * R28: what resolves a code finding. Each assertion drives the real code over
 * a real git checkout: the filesystem Vault, the history walk, and the
 * findings reader. A finding is resolved only when a later merged run's change
 * rewrote a hunk holding the cited line, mapped through history to that run's
 * base, and that run's accepted evidence shows its coverage check passed with
 * a line of the hunk executed (D-R28-01). Every other case leaves it open.
 */
import type { ModelFamily, ModelIdentity, RunId, TaskId, VaultRef } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import type { CheckResult } from '@olympus-ai/integrity';
import type { AdmissionRecord, EvidenceBundle, FindingOutcomeEntry, NightlyRecord, Vault } from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import { runtime } from '../kit/assert.js';
import type { LocalAssertion } from '../kit/types.js';
import { LINES, ago, walkOn, withObserve, type Rig } from './observe.js';

const api = async () => import('@olympus-ai/api');

const RUN_S = 'run-s' as RunId;
const RUN_R = 'run-r' as RunId;
const TASK_S = 't-s' as TaskId;
const TASK_R = 't-r' as TaskId;
const PATH = 'a.txt';
/** The line the review seat cites in S's pushed tree. */
const CITED = 5;
const REVIEWER: ModelIdentity = { provider: 'openai', family: 'gpt' as ModelFamily, model: 'gpt-x', version: '1' };
const ZERO = '0'.repeat(64);

/** What R's accepted evidence says about the coverage of its change. */
interface Scenario {
  /** `covering`: an executed line inside the hunk; `outside`: executed lines only outside it; `none`: no changed-line coverage at all. */
  readonly lines: 'covering' | 'outside' | 'none';
  /** The coverage check's exit code: 0 passed. */
  readonly coverageExit: number;
  /** Whether a commit between S's merge and R's base rewrites the cited line, so it cannot be mapped to R's base. */
  readonly rewriteBetween: boolean;
}

interface Built {
  readonly findingsRef: VaultRef;
  readonly evidenceRef: VaultRef;
  readonly rMerge: string;
  /** The cited line as it stands at R's base, and R's head-side line that rewrote it. */
  readonly target: number;
}

const PASSING: Scenario = { lines: 'covering', coverageExit: 0, rewriteBetween: false };

async function admit(vault: Vault, runId: RunId): Promise<VaultRef> {
  // The admission's other fields are not read by the walk or the findings reader; the record is the one `seedAdmitted` writes.
  return vault.recordAdmission({ run: { id: runId }, roles: [], stack: 'unsupported', artifacts: { observationPlan: { source: 'none' } } } as unknown as AdmissionRecord);
}

function check(exitCode: number): CheckResult {
  return { checkId: 'coverage', exitCode, stdout: '', stderr: '', suiteCount: null, expectation: null, findings: null, property: null, durationMs: 1, startedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
}

/**
 * Builds S (a merged run whose review seat cites `a.txt` line 5 in the tree it
 * pushed), a commit on main between S and R, and R (a merged run whose change
 * rewrites the cited line as it stands at R's base), with R's evidence as the
 * scenario says. Nothing is walked.
 */
async function build(rig: Rig, s: Scenario): Promise<Built> {
  const { vault, repo } = rig;
  const sMerge = await repo.merge('feature-s', { [PATH]: LINES(12, { 9: 'nine, by s' }) }, 'S change');
  const sHead = repo.git(['rev-parse', `${sMerge}^2`]);
  // Either two lines inserted above the cited line, which shifts it to 7, or the cited line itself rewritten.
  if (s.rewriteBetween) await repo.commit({ [PATH]: LINES(12, { [CITED]: 'five, rewritten on main', 9: 'nine, by s' }) }, 'rewrite five');
  else await repo.commit({ [PATH]: `top 1\ntop 2\n${LINES(12, { 9: 'nine, by s' })}` }, 'insert two lines above');
  const target = s.rewriteBetween ? CITED : CITED + 2;
  const rText = s.rewriteBetween
    ? LINES(12, { [CITED]: 'five, fixed by r', 9: 'nine, by s' })
    : `top 1\ntop 2\n${LINES(12, { [CITED]: 'five, fixed by r', 9: 'nine, by s' })}`;
  const rMerge = await repo.merge('feature-r', { [PATH]: rText }, 'R change');
  const rBase = repo.git(['rev-parse', `${rMerge}^1`]);
  const rHead = repo.git(['rev-parse', `${rMerge}^2`]);
  const mergedAt = ago(10_000);

  // S: its review seat's committed result, and the findings record read from it.
  const sAdmission = await admit(vault, RUN_S);
  const result = await vault.recordTaskResult(RUN_S, {
    taskId: TASK_S, claim: { narrative: 'done', filesChanged: [PATH] }, events: [],
    usage: { inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0, cacheReadTokens: 0, cacheWriteTokens: 0, costUsd: 0, wallClockMs: 0 }, model: REVIEWER, contractVersion: '1',
  });
  const findingsRef = await vault.recordReviewFindings({
    runId: RUN_S, taskId: TASK_S, seat: 'review', result, reviewer: REVIEWER, tree: null, readable: true,
    findings: [{ kind: 'located', category: 'untested-behavior', path: PATH, line: CITED }], collectedBy: 'runtime',
  });
  await vault.commitRunState({
    runId: RUN_S, admission: sAdmission, station: 'observe', stations: [], phase: 'working', tasks: { [TASK_S]: 'passed' }, attempts: {}, results: { [TASK_S]: result },
    evidenceRefs: [], violations: [], approvals: [], reviews: [], drafts: {}, usage: [], cancelled: null, halted: null, narrowed: null, version: '0',
  }, '0');
  await vault.recordIntegration({
    runId: RUN_S, kind: 'merged', repository: 'octo/hello', baseBranch: 'main', baseCommit: repo.git(['rev-parse', `${sMerge}^1`]), branch: `factory/${RUN_S}`, commit: sHead,
    pullRequest: 1, url: 'https://github.com/octo/hello/pull/1', collectedBy: 'runtime', mergeCommit: sMerge, mergedAt,
  });

  // R: one passed task whose evidence names the changed lines its coverage check saw run.
  const rAdmission = await admit(vault, RUN_R);
  const executed = s.lines === 'outside' ? [1, 2] : [target];
  const bundle: EvidenceBundle = {
    runId: RUN_R, taskId: TASK_R, baseCommit: rBase, baseTreeSha256: ZERO, baseRun: { runId: RUN_R, kind: 'base-run', hash: ZERO },
    diff: [{ path: PATH, change: 'modified', sha256: ZERO }], diffSha256: ZERO, checks: [check(s.coverageExit)], unstarted: [],
    claim: { narrative: 'fixed', filesChanged: [PATH] }, claimEvidenceDiff: [], harnessImage: 'no-sandbox',
    tamper: {
      assertionsWeakened: [], skipMarkersAdded: [], suppressionMarkersAdded: [], testsDeleted: [], snapshotsRegenerated: [], protectedPathsTouched: [],
      coverageDelta: s.lines === 'none' ? null : 1,
      changedLines: s.lines === 'none' ? null : { check: 'coverage', files: [{ path: PATH, executed, unexecuted: [] }] },
    },
    notCarried: [], install: null, collectedBy: 'runtime', driverProvenanceId: 'conformance', contractVersion: '1',
  };
  const evidenceRef = await vault.writeEvidence(bundle);
  await vault.commitRunState({
    runId: RUN_R, admission: rAdmission, station: 'observe', stations: [], phase: 'working', tasks: { [TASK_R]: 'passed' }, attempts: {}, results: {},
    evidenceRefs: [evidenceRef], violations: [], approvals: [], reviews: [], drafts: {}, usage: [], cancelled: null, halted: null, narrowed: null, version: '0',
  }, '0');
  await vault.recordIntegration({
    runId: RUN_R, kind: 'merged', repository: 'octo/hello', baseBranch: 'main', baseCommit: rBase, branch: `factory/${RUN_R}`, commit: rHead,
    pullRequest: 2, url: 'https://github.com/octo/hello/pull/2', collectedBy: 'runtime', mergeCommit: rMerge, mergedAt,
  });
  return { findingsRef, evidenceRef, rMerge, target };
}

async function walked(rig: Rig, date: string): Promise<void> {
  const record = await walkOn(rig, date);
  if (record.outcome.kind !== 'completed') throw new Error(`the walk on ${date} was refused: ${JSON.stringify(record.outcome)}`);
}

/** A control: the same store and history but for one fact, after which the finding must stay open. */
async function staysOpen(prefix: string, s: Scenario, what: string): Promise<void> {
  await withObserve(prefix, async (rig) => {
    await build(rig, s);
    await walked(rig, '2026-11-05');
    const outcomes = await rig.vault.readFindingOutcomes();
    if (outcomes.length !== 0) throw new Error(`I2: ${what}, yet the finding was given an outcome: ${JSON.stringify(outcomes.map((e) => e.record))}`);
  });
}

function onlyResolution(outcomes: readonly FindingOutcomeEntry[], b: Built): void {
  if (outcomes.length !== 1) throw new Error(`I2: a covered change wrote ${String(outcomes.length)} outcomes, not one: ${JSON.stringify(outcomes.map((e) => e.record))}`);
  const [entry] = outcomes;
  if (entry === undefined) throw new Error('I2: no outcome was read');
  const { finding, outcome } = entry.record;
  if (finding.source.kind !== 'review' || finding.source.record.hash !== b.findingsRef.hash || finding.source.record.runId !== RUN_S || finding.index !== 0) {
    throw new Error(`I2: the outcome names another finding: ${JSON.stringify(finding)}`);
  }
  if (outcome.kind !== 'resolved' || outcome.resolution.kind !== 'covered-change') throw new Error(`I2: the finding was not resolved by a covered change: ${JSON.stringify(outcome)}`);
  const r = outcome.resolution;
  const h = r.hunk;
  const ok = r.runId === RUN_R && r.mergeCommit === b.rMerge && r.path === PATH && r.line === b.target
    && h.oldStart === b.target && h.oldLines === 1 && h.newStart === b.target && h.newLines === 1
    && r.executed.length === 1 && r.executed[0] === b.target
    && r.evidence.runId === b.evidenceRef.runId && r.evidence.kind === 'evidence' && r.evidence.hash === b.evidenceRef.hash;
  if (!ok) throw new Error(`I2: the resolution does not name R, its merge commit, the hunk holding the mapped line, the executed line, and R's evidence: ${JSON.stringify(r)}`);
}

export const CODE_FINDING_RESOLVED_BY_COVERED_CHANGE = runtime({
  id: 'I2.code-finding-resolved-by-covered-change',
  title: "a review seat's code finding is resolved only by a later merged change that rewrote the cited line, mapped through history to its base, where that run's passing coverage check executed a line of the hunk; no coverage, a failed check, executed lines outside the hunk, or a line the history rewrote leaves it open, and a second pass writes nothing",
  timeoutMs: 180_000,
  run: async () => {
    const { resolveCodeFindings } = await api();
    await withObserve('r28-resolved-', async (rig) => {
      const b = await build(rig, PASSING);
      await walked(rig, '2026-11-05');
      const first = await rig.vault.readFindingOutcomes();
      onlyResolution(first, b);
      // (e) Neither a second walk nor a second pass over R writes a second outcome.
      await rig.repo.commit({ 'b.txt': 'b, later\n' }, 'later');
      await walked(rig, '2026-11-06');
      const again = await resolveCodeFindings(rig.vault, rig.repo.dir, RUN_R, () => new Date().toISOString());
      if (again.resolved !== 0 || again.unreadable.length !== 0) throw new Error(`I2: a second pass over R resolved again: ${JSON.stringify(again)}`);
      const after = await rig.vault.readFindingOutcomes();
      onlyResolution(after, b);
      if (after[0]?.sha256 !== first[0]?.sha256) throw new Error('I2: the outcome was rewritten by a second pass');
    });
    await staysOpen('r28-nolines-', { ...PASSING, lines: 'none' }, "R's evidence names no changed-line coverage");
    await staysOpen('r28-failed-', { ...PASSING, coverageExit: 1 }, "R's coverage check failed");
    await staysOpen('r28-outside-', { ...PASSING, lines: 'outside' }, "R's executed lines are all outside the hunk");
    await staysOpen('r28-unmapped-', { ...PASSING, rewriteBetween: true }, "the cited line was rewritten between S and R's base, so it cannot be mapped");
  },
});

export const RECORD_FINDING_NEVER_RESOLVED = runtime({
  id: 'I5.record-finding-never-resolved',
  title: "a nightly reviewer's finding that cites a record is never resolved, even by a walk that resolves a code finding, and is listed open",
  timeoutMs: 60_000,
  run: async () => {
    const { listFindings } = await api();
    await withObserve('r28-record-', async (rig) => {
      const b = await build(rig, PASSING);
      const nightly: NightlyRecord = {
        schedule: 'findings-review', template: 'nightly-findings-review', date: '2026-11-04', startedAt: ago(20_000), endedAt: ago(19_000),
        outcome: { kind: 'completed' }, findings: [], integrity: null, security: null, observation: null, collectedBy: 'runtime',
        review: {
          subject: null, role: null, usage: null, egress: null, claim: null,
          seat: { reviewer: { role: null, driver: null, model: REVIEWER }, authors: [], independence: 'no-author', level: 0 },
          findings: [{ kind: 'record', category: 'violation', record: `${RUN_S}/violation`, candidate: { none: 'budget' } }],
        },
      };
      const hash = await rig.vault.recordNightly(nightly);
      await walked(rig, '2026-11-05');
      // The walk did resolve the code finding beside it.
      onlyResolution(await rig.vault.readFindingOutcomes(), b);
      const listed = (await listFindings(rig.vault)).find((f) => f.id === `nightly:${hash}:0`);
      if (listed === undefined) throw new Error('I5: the record finding is not listed');
      if (listed.cites.kind !== 'record') throw new Error(`I5: the record finding is listed as citing ${JSON.stringify(listed.cites)}`);
      if (listed.outcome.kind !== 'open') throw new Error(`I5: a finding citing a record was given an outcome: ${JSON.stringify(listed.outcome)}`);
    });
  },
});

export const R28_FINDINGS_RESOLUTION: readonly LocalAssertion[] = [CODE_FINDING_RESOLVED_BY_COVERED_CHANGE, RECORD_FINDING_NEVER_RESOLVED];

===== packages/conformance/src/registry/findings.ts =====
/**
 * R28: findings, located where they were raised, and what became of them
 * (D-A-FO-01, D-R28-01). A review seat's finding names a file and a line of
 * the tree it reviewed, or is refused with its cause; it never moves its
 * task's status. An outcome is a write-once runtime record; a finding with
 * none is open. R2 counts them per seat kind and per family, and says a
 * resolved rate it had no input for is unmeasured rather than zero.
 */
import { mkdtemp, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import type { ModelFamily, TaskId, TaskRequest } from '@olympus-ai/core';
import type { NightlyRecord, ReviewFindingsRecord, Vault } from '@olympus-ai/vault';
import { runtime } from '../kit/assert.js';
import type { LocalAssertion } from '../kit/types.js';
import { R28_DRAFTING } from './drafting.js';
import { HELLO_REVIEW, readRecord, stubDriver, withLine } from './line.js';
import { FINDING_DISMISSAL_IS_A_PERSONS, FINDING_DISMISSAL_THROUGH_THE_CLI } from './findings-http.js';
import { CODE_FINDING_RESOLVED_BY_COVERED_CHANGE, RECORD_FINDING_NEVER_RESOLVED } from './findings-resolution.js';
import { EVERY_STACK_REPORTS_CHANGED_LINES } from './stacks.js';
import { inTask, writes } from './verification.js';

const api = async () => import('@olympus-ai/api');

const SECURITY = `${HELLO_REVIEW}.security` as TaskId;
const NOTE = 'REVIEW-NOTE-SENTINEL';

/** Each review seat's findings records, by task, read back from the store. */
async function findingsOf(vault: Vault, runId: string): Promise<Map<TaskId, ReviewFindingsRecord[]>> {
  const out = new Map<TaskId, ReviewFindingsRecord[]>();
  for (const ref of await vault.readReviewFindings(runId as never)) {
    const record = await readRecord<ReviewFindingsRecord>(vault, ref);
    out.set(record.taskId, [...(out.get(record.taskId) ?? []), record]);
  }
  return out;
}

/** Drives the hello run with an author that writes three lines, and both review seats answering `narrative`. */
async function reviewedRun<T>(prefix: string, narrative: string, body: (vault: Vault, runId: string, state: Awaited<ReturnType<Parameters<Parameters<typeof withLine>[1]>[0]['state']>>) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  return withLine(prefix, async (rig) => {
    const { startRun } = await api();
    const { StubSandboxProvider } = await import('@olympus-ai/sandbox');
    const sandbox = new StubSandboxProvider();
    const author = await stubDriver({ during: async (req: TaskRequest) => { await inTask(sandbox, req, writes({ 'src/change.ts': 'one\ntwo\nthree\n' })); } });
    const reviewer = await stubDriver({ family: 'other-family', narrative });
    const components = await rig.components({ sandbox, driver: author, reviewer });
    await startRun(await rig.request(components, {}));
    return body(components.vault, rig.runId, await rig.state());
  });
}

export const REVIEW_FINDINGS_NEVER_MOVE_STATUS: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I2.review-findings-never-move-status',
  title:
    "each review seat's findings are recorded beside its committed result, located in the tree it reviewed under the reviewer model the runtime recorded, each refused one with its cause, and none carrying the reviewer's note; and each seat's task passes exactly as it does with no finding (R28, D-P4-07)",
  run: async () => {
    const narrative = JSON.stringify({ findings: [
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/change.ts', line: 2, note: NOTE },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/change.ts', line: 9 },
      { category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/absent.ts', line: 1 },
    ] });
    await reviewedRun('r28-review-findings-', narrative, async (vault, runId, state) => {
      if (state.tasks[HELLO_REVIEW] !== 'passed' || state.tasks[SECURITY] !== 'passed') throw new Error(`I2: a seat with findings did not pass as one without: ${JSON.stringify(state.tasks)}`);
      const records = await findingsOf(vault, runId);
      const review = records.get(HELLO_REVIEW)?.find((r) => r.result.hash === state.results[HELLO_REVIEW]?.hash);
      const security = records.get(SECURITY)?.find((r) => r.result.hash === state.results[SECURITY]?.hash);
      if (review === undefined || security === undefined) throw new Error(`I2: a seat's findings are not recorded beside its committed result: ${JSON.stringify([...records.keys()])}`);
      const expected = [
        { kind: 'located', category: 'spec-mismatch', path: 'src/change.ts', line: 2 },
        { kind: 'refused', cause: 'unlocated', defects: [] },
        { kind: 'refused', cause: 'unlocated', defects: [] },
      ];
      if (!review.readable || JSON.stringify(review.findings) !== JSON.stringify(expected)) throw new Error(`I2: the review seat's findings were recorded as ${JSON.stringify(review.findings)}`);
      // The security seat's categories are its own questions; a reviewer's category is out of its class there.
      if (!security.findings.every((f) => f.kind === 'refused' && f.cause === 'unrecordable' && f.defects.some((d) => d.field === 'category'))) {
        throw new Error(`I2: the security seat recorded a category its role does not ask: ${JSON.stringify(security.findings)}`);
      }
      if (review.reviewer.family !== 'other-family' || review.seat !== 'review' || security.seat !== 'security') throw new Error(`I2: the findings record names the seat ${JSON.stringify([review.seat, review.reviewer])}`);
      if (JSON.stringify([review, security]).includes(NOTE)) throw new Error("I7: a reviewer's note entered a findings record");
    });
  },
});

export const UNREADABLE_REVIEW_IS_NOT_CLEAN: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I5.unreadable-review-result-is-never-no-findings',
  title:
    "a review seat whose final message is not the findings object is recorded unreadable with no findings, never as having found nothing, and its task passes as before; nothing is listed for it (R28)",
  run: async () => {
    await reviewedRun('r28-review-unreadable-', 'The change looks fine to me.', async (vault, runId, state) => {
      if (state.tasks[HELLO_REVIEW] !== 'passed') throw new Error(`I5: an unreadable review changed its task's status: ${JSON.stringify(state.tasks)}`);
      const review = (await findingsOf(vault, runId)).get(HELLO_REVIEW)?.find((r) => r.result.hash === state.results[HELLO_REVIEW]?.hash);
      if (review === undefined || review.readable || review.findings.length !== 0) throw new Error(`I5: an unreadable review was recorded as ${JSON.stringify(review)}`);
      const { listFindings } = await api();
      if ((await listFindings(vault)).length !== 0) throw new Error('I5: an unreadable review listed a finding');
    });
  },
});

/** A nightly tree reviewer's record holding one tree finding and one record finding, as the store keeps it. */
function nightly(date: string): NightlyRecord {
  const model = { provider: 'conformance', family: 'nightly-family' as ModelFamily, model: 'm', version: '1' };
  return {
    schedule: 'nightly-tree', template: 'nightly-tree-review', date, startedAt: `${date}T00:00:00.000Z`, endedAt: `${date}T00:01:00.000Z`,
    outcome: { kind: 'completed' }, findings: [], integrity: null, security: null, observation: null, collectedBy: 'runtime',
    review: {
      subject: { kind: 'tree', branch: 'main', commit: 'a'.repeat(40), files: 1, merged: [], since: 'none', unattributed: false },
      role: null, seat: { reviewer: { role: null, driver: 'conformance@1', model }, authors: [], independence: 'no-author', level: 'L1' }, usage: null, egress: null, claim: null,
      findings: [
        { kind: 'tree', category: 'unchecked-input', path: 'index.js', line: 2, candidate: { none: 'budget' } },
        { kind: 'record', category: 'violation', record: `decision:${'0'.repeat(64)}`, candidate: { none: 'budget' } },
        { kind: 'refused', cause: 'unlocated', defects: [] },
      ],
    },
  } as unknown as NightlyRecord;
}

async function withStore<T>(body: (vault: Vault) => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
  const base = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'r28-store-'));
  try {
    const { LocalVault } = await import('@olympus-ai/vault');
    return await body(new LocalVault({ store: join(base, 'store'), artifacts: join(base, 'artifacts') }));
  } finally {
    await rm(base, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

export const FINDING_WITHOUT_OUTCOME_IS_OPEN: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I5.finding-without-outcome-is-open',
  title:
    'a located finding with no outcome record lists as open, never resolved; a refused finding is never listed; and a second outcome for a finding is refused, writing nothing, so its first stands (R28)',
  run: async () => {
    await withStore(async (vault) => {
      const { listFindings, dismissFinding } = await api();
      await vault.recordNightly(nightly('2026-11-20'));
      const listed = await listFindings(vault);
      if (listed.length !== 2 || listed.some((f) => f.outcome.kind !== 'open')) throw new Error(`I5: findings with no outcome listed as ${JSON.stringify(listed)}`);
      const [first] = listed;
      if (first === undefined) throw new Error('I5 control: nothing listed');
      const done = await dismissFinding(vault, first.id, 'duplicate', new Date().toISOString());
      if (done.kind !== 'dismissed') throw new Error(`I5 control: the first dismissal was ${JSON.stringify(done)}`);
      const again = await dismissFinding(vault, first.id, 'not-a-defect', new Date().toISOString());
      if (again.kind !== 'decided' || again.outcome.kind !== 'dismissed' || again.outcome.cause !== 'duplicate') throw new Error(`I5: a second outcome was not refused: ${JSON.stringify(again)}`);
      const stored = await vault.readFindingOutcomes();
      if (stored.length !== 1 || stored[0]?.record.outcome.kind !== 'dismissed') throw new Error(`I5: a second outcome was written: ${JSON.stringify(stored)}`);
      const direct = await vault.recordFindingOutcome({ ...stored[0].record, outcome: { kind: 'dismissed', cause: 'not-reproducible' }, at: new Date().toISOString() });
      if (direct !== null) throw new Error('I5: the store wrote a second outcome for one finding');
    });
  },
});

export const FINDING_RATES_PER_SEAT_AND_FAMILY: LocalAssertion = runtime({
  id: 'I5.finding-rates-say-what-they-could-not-measure',
  title:
    "R2 derives resolved, dismissed, and open over findings raised, per seat kind and per reviewer family, each with its sample and bound; a group holding a code finding, where no run recorded line coverage, has its resolved rate unmeasured, never 0 of n (R28, D-R28-01)",
  run: async () => {
    await withStore(async (vault) => {
      const { outcomes, listFindings, dismissFinding } = await api();
      await vault.recordNightly(nightly('2026-11-20'));
      const [first] = await listFindings(vault);
      if (first === undefined) throw new Error('I5 control: nothing listed');
      await dismissFinding(vault, first.id, 'not-a-defect', new Date().toISOString());
      const { findings } = await outcomes(vault);
      const seat = findings.bySeat['nightly:nightly-tree-review'];
      const family = findings.byFamily['nightly-family'];
      if (seat === undefined || family === undefined || seat.raised !== 2 || family.raised !== 2) throw new Error(`I5: the findings were counted as ${JSON.stringify(findings)}`);
      if (seat.resolved.kind !== 'unmeasured') throw new Error(`I5: a resolved rate with no line coverage read ${JSON.stringify(seat.resolved)}`);
      if (seat.dismissed.kind !== 'rate' || seat.dismissed.count !== 1 || seat.dismissed.sample !== 2 || seat.dismissed.bound.side !== 'upper') throw new Error(`I5: the dismissed rate is ${JSON.stringify(seat.dismissed)}`);
      if (seat.open.kind !== 'rate' || seat.open.count !== 1 || seat.open.sample !== 2) throw new Error(`I5: the open rate is ${JSON.stringify(seat.open)}`);
      const window = await outcomes(vault, { from: '2026-11-21T00:00:00.000Z' });
      if (Object.keys(window.findings.bySeat).length !== 0) throw new Error('I5: a nightly finding outside the window was counted');
    });
  },
});

export const R28_ASSERTIONS = {
  I2: [REVIEW_FINDINGS_NEVER_MOVE_STATUS, CODE_FINDING_RESOLVED_BY_COVERED_CHANGE, FINDING_DISMISSAL_IS_A_PERSONS, FINDING_DISMISSAL_THROUGH_THE_CLI, ...R28_DRAFTING.I2],
  I5: [UNREADABLE_REVIEW_IS_NOT_CLEAN, RECORD_FINDING_NEVER_RESOLVED, FINDING_WITHOUT_OUTCOME_IS_OPEN, FINDING_RATES_PER_SEAT_AND_FAMILY, ...R28_DRAFTING.I5],
  I8: [EVERY_STACK_REPORTS_CHANGED_LINES],
} as const;

 /** The thirty-eight named, audited operations the Vault contract declares (the eight intake operations are R22's, A-R22-04; `recordUsage` is P13's, A-P13-03; `recordDecision` and `readDecisions` are P14's, A-P14-01; `readUsage` is I1a's, A-I1a-05; `recordIntegration` and `readIntegration` are I1b's, A-I1b-01; `listRuns` is R2's, A-R2-02; `recordBaseRun` and `readBaseRun` are R15's, D-A-BF-01; `recordIntent`, `recordRubric`, `recordDraft`, `recordCriticResult`, `recordDraftLock`, and `readLocks` are R19's, A-R19-07; `recordNightly`, `readNightly`, and `listObjects` are R13a's, A-R13a-02 and A-R13a-05; `recordObservation` and `readObservations` are R8a's, D-R8a-03; `dismissCandidate` is R16's, A-R16-05).  /** I1: No agent writes to the Vault. */
 export const I1: InvariantEntry = {
   title: INVARIANTS.I1,
   assertions: [
     // R20: the role compiler.
     ...R20_I1,
     compileError({
       id: 'I1.write-boundary-vault-never',
       title: 'WriteBoundary.vault admits no value but "never"',
       fixture: 'i1/write-boundary-vault-never.ts',
     }),
     compileError({
       id: 'I1.mount-table-single-rw',
       title: 'MountTable admits at most one rw mount and only in the workspace slot: every other mount is ro by type, a second rw mount has no slot, and the workspace may itself be ro',
       fixture: 'i1/mount-table-single-rw.ts',
     }),
     compileError({
       id: 'I1.vault-has-no-generic-write',
       title: 'the Vault contract exposes named mutators only; write, put, and delete do not exist',
       fixture: 'i1/vault-has-no-generic-write.ts',
     }),
     runtime({
       id: 'I1.vault-implementation-exposes-only-named-operations',
       title:
-        'the filesystem Vault carries exactly the thirty-five named operations at run time: no generic write, no delete, and no helper left reachable on a constructed instance or anywhere on its prototype chain for a caller to mutate the store past them',
+        'the filesystem Vault carries exactly the thirty-nine named operations at run time: no generic write, no delete, and no helper left reachable on a constructed instance or anywhere on its prototype chain for a caller to mutate the store past them',
       run: async () => {
         const { LocalVault } = await import('@olympus-ai/vault');
         // A constructed instance, not the class prototype: an instance field, a symbol key, an inherited helper, or an
         // accessor under an allowed name is reachable from what callers hold, and a prototype's own names miss each (#65).
         const root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'i1-vault-surface-'));
         try {
           const vault = new LocalVault({ store: join(root, 'store'), artifacts: join(root, 'artifacts') });
           const problems = vaultSurfaceProblems(vault, NAMED_OPERATIONS);
           if (problems.length > 0) {
             throw new Error(
               `I1: the Vault exposes more than its named operations, or less: ${problems.join('; ')}. The type-level ` +
                 'fixture cannot see these: a TypeScript `private` method is public at run time, so an internal helper is a ' +
                 'write path the contract does not declare. Use a module-level function or a `#private` method.',
             );
           }
         } finally {
           await rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
         }
       },
     }),
     runtime({
       id: 'I1.mount-layer-enforcement',
       title:
         'inside a provisioned container the mount table is what the kernel applies: the rw workspace is writable and its writes ' +
         'land on the host, a workspace the table marks ro is not writable, an `others` mount is not writable, and nothing ' +
         'written outside the workspace reaches any host path',
       run: async () => {
         await withProvider('i1-mount-layer-', async (provider, dirs, base) => {
           // The one rw mount is the workspace, and it really is the host's tree.
           await withSandbox(provider, specFor(dirs, 'rw'), async (handle) => {
             const wrote = await provider.exec(handle, ['sh', '-c', 'echo landed > /workspace/made.txt']);
             if (wrote.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`I1: a write to the rw workspace failed: ${wrote.stderr}`);
             const onHost = (await readFile(join(dirs.workspace, 'made.txt'), 'utf8')).trim();
             if (onHost !== 'landed') throw new Error(`I1: the rw workspace write did not reach the host (read ${onHost})`);
 
             // Everything else the container can write is the container's own layer, and dies with it.
             const outside = await provider.exec(handle, ['sh', '-c', 'echo escaped > /outside.txt']);
             if (outside.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error('I1: this assertion assumes the container root is writable; it was not');
             const leaked = (await readdir(base)).filter((name) => !DIR_NAMES.includes(name));
             if (leaked.length > 0) throw new Error(`I1: a write outside the workspace reached the host: ${leaked.join(', ')}`);
           });
 
           // A workspace the table marks ro is mounted ro, which is what lets verification run against a
           // tree it cannot modify (I3, and the TOCTOU limit P1 recorded against this unit's obligation).
           await withSandbox(provider, specFor(dirs, 'ro'), async (handle) => {
             const blocked = await provider.exec(handle, ['sh', '-c', 'echo x > /workspace/nope.txt']);
             if (blocked.exitCode === 0) throw new Error('I1: a workspace the table marks ro accepted a write');
             if (!blocked.stderr.includes('Read-only file system')) {
               throw new Error(`I1: the ro workspace refused the write for the wrong reason: ${blocked.stderr}`);
             }
           });
 
           // And an `others` mount, which the type layer already holds to ro.
           const withOther = specFor(dirs, 'rw', {
             mounts: {
               workspace: { source: dirs.workspace, target: '/workspace', mode: 'rw' },
               others: [{ source: dirs.readable, target: '/readable', mode: 'ro' }],
             },
           });
           await withSandbox(provider, withOther, async (handle) => {
             const blocked = await provider.exec(handle, ['sh', '-c', 'echo x > /readable/nope.txt']);
             if (blocked.exitCode === 0) throw new Error('I1: an `others` mount declared ro accepted a write');
           });
         });
       },
     }),
     runtime({
       id: 'I1.mount-layer-refuses-a-vault-mount',
       title:
         'a mount that would put a Vault path in the container is refused before any container starts — declared directly, ' +
         `reached through a ${ESCAPE_MECHANISM}, or asked for as ro — and so is a second rw mount`,
       run: async () => {
         await withProvider('i1-mount-refusals-', async (provider, dirs) => {
           const direct = await refusalFrom(() =>
             provider.provision(
               specFor(dirs, 'rw', { mounts: { workspace: { source: dirs.vault, target: '/workspace', mode: 'rw' }, others: [] } }),
             ),
           );
           if (direct.layer !== 'mount') {
             throw new Error(`I1: a Vault path as the workspace was refused at the ${direct.layer} layer, not the mount layer`);
           }
 
           // The escape: the declared path is inside the workspace and only resolves to the Vault.
           // A containment check that ran before resolution would let this one through.
           const link = join(dirs.workspace, 'link');
           await linkTo(dirs.vault, link);
           const viaLink = await refusalFrom(() =>
             provider.provision(
               specFor(dirs, 'rw', { mounts: { workspace: { source: link, target: '/workspace', mode: 'rw' }, others: [] } }),
             ),
           );
           if (viaLink.layer !== 'mount' || !viaLink.message.includes(link)) {
             throw new Error(`I1: a ${ESCAPE_MECHANISM} into the Vault was not refused by name: ${viaLink.message}`);
           }
 
           // ro is refused too: a Vault inside the container is one flag away from a writable one.
           const readOnly = await refusalFrom(() =>
             provider.provision(
               specFor(dirs, 'rw', {
                 mounts: {
                   workspace: { source: dirs.workspace, target: '/workspace', mode: 'rw' },
                   others: [{ source: dirs.vault, target: '/audit', mode: 'ro' }],
                 },
               }),
             ),
           );
           if (readOnly.layer !== 'mount') {
             throw new Error(`I1: a Vault path mounted ro was accepted, or refused elsewhere: ${readOnly.message}`);
           }
 
           // The Vault reached by truncation rather than by naming it. Docker's --mount grammar
           // splits on commas, so a source of `<vault>,readonly` is mounted as `<vault>`: every
           // containment check passes on the longer name and the container gets the Vault. The
           // declared path carries no comma, so checking only what the caller wrote misses it.
           // Review finding 1; an I1 bypass demonstrated against a running container.
           const decoy = join(dirname(dirs.vault), `${basename(dirs.vault)},readonly`);
           await mkdir(decoy, { recursive: true });
           const plain = join(dirs.readable, 'plain-link');
           await linkTo(decoy, plain);
           if (plain.includes(',')) throw new Error('I1: this assertion needs a comma-free declared path');
           const truncated = await refusalFrom(() =>
             provider.provision(
               specFor(dirs, 'rw', { mounts: { workspace: { source: plain, target: '/workspace', mode: 'rw' }, others: [] } }),
             ),
           );
           if (truncated.layer !== 'mount') {
             throw new Error(`I1: a source resolving to a comma-bearing path was not refused by the mount layer: ${truncated.message}`);
           }
 
           // A second rw mount has no slot in the type, so a cast is how it arrives at run time.
           const twoWritable = {
             workspace: { source: dirs.workspace, target: '/workspace', mode: 'rw' },
             others: [{ source: dirs.readable, target: '/second', mode: 'rw' }],
           } as unknown as ReturnType<typeof specFor>['mounts'];
           const second = await refusalFrom(() => provider.provision(specFor(dirs, 'rw', { mounts: twoWritable })));
           if (second.layer !== 'mount' || !second.message.includes('I1')) {
             throw new Error(`I1: a second rw mount was not refused by the mount layer: ${second.message}`);
           }
         });
       },
     }),
     external({
       id: 'I1.driver-executes-inside-the-sandbox',
       title: "a task's commands run inside the provisioned sandbox and nowhere else, and a driver with no provider cannot be constructed",
       level: 'runtime',
       package: '@olympus-ai/driver-claude-code',
       file: 'test/invariants.test.ts',
     }),
     ADAPTERS_EXECUTE_NOTHING_ON_THE_HOST,
     // I1a: the composed line, in real containers, mounts no Vault.
     COMPOSED_HOST_MOUNTS_NO_VAULT,
     // R6: the second driver's loop is on the host, and every tool and model call it makes is in the sandbox (D-R6-01).
     OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_EXECUTES_INSIDE_THE_SANDBOX,
     // R26: the Codex CLI runs in the sandbox, and the mount layer keeps the Vault out of it.
     CODEX_EXECUTES_INSIDE_THE_SANDBOX,
   ],
   pending: [],

       run: async () => {

       id: 'I5.over-request-refused',
       title:
         'the real engine refuses a level above the global cap, the station cap, or the role ceiling with reason exceeds-cap, carries no level on the refusal, and grants a request at or below the tightest bound at exactly the level asked for',
       run: async () => {

         const engine = new StrictPolicyEngine();

         // Three bounds, three different values, so each refusal names the one

         }

           throw new Error(`I5: the refusal does not name the bound that bit: ${global.detail}`);

     runtime({
       id: 'I5.malformed-document-refused-at-resolve',

         'resolvePolicy refuses a value that does not validate as a PolicyDocument, even though the parameter type says it is one, so a caller that asserts past the compiler cannot resolve a grant out of a malformed document; a genuine document still resolves',
       run: async () => {

               throw new Error(`I5: a deny-all sandbox has interfaces [${interfaces.stdout.trim()}]; expected loopback alone`);
             }

               ]);
               if (!reached.stdout.includes(ORIGIN_BODY)) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: the allowlisted host was not reached: ${reached.stdout}${reached.stderr}`);
               }
 
               // Refused by name, and by address. The second is the one that matters: a refusal

               for (const host of [BLOCKED_NAME, BLOCKED_ADDRESS]) {

                 if (!answer.includes('403') || !answer.includes(host)) {
                   throw new Error(`I5: ${host} is not on the allowlist and was not refused by name in the answer: ${answer}`);
                 }
               }
 
               // The strongest form: the origin's own address, which the proxy demonstrably
               // reaches, refused for the one reason under test — the address is not on the list.
               const sameHost = await throughProxy(provider, handle, `GET http://${origin.address}:${String(ORIGIN_PORT)}/ HTTP/1.0`);
               if (!sameHost.includes('403') || sameHost.includes(ORIGIN_BODY)) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: a reachable host was allowed by address although only its name was granted: ${sameHost}`);
               }
 
               // There is nothing to bypass. The internal network carries no default route, so a
               // process that unsets every proxy variable is left with no second path to take.
               const routes = await provider.exec(handle, ['sh', '-c', 'ip route']);
               if (routes.stdout.includes('default')) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: the sandbox has a default route, so the proxy is a convention rather than the only way out: ${routes.stdout}`);
               }

                 'sh', '-c',
                 'unset http_proxy https_proxy HTTP_PROXY HTTPS_PROXY no_proxy NO_PROXY; ' +
                   `wget -T 4 -O - http://${origin.address}:${String(ORIGIN_PORT)}/ 2>&1`,
               ]);
               if (bypass.exitCode === 0 || bypass.stdout.includes(ORIGIN_BODY)) {
                 throw new Error(`I5: the allowlisted host was reached with every proxy variable unset: ${bypass.stdout}`);
               }
             });
           } finally {
             await provider.destroy(handle);
           }
 
           // The proxy is the sandbox's, and goes with it. One left running is a route off the
           // host with no workload behind it and nobody watching it.

             ['container', egress.proxy.name],
             ['network', egress.proxy.internalNetwork],
             ['network', egress.proxy.outboundNetwork],
           ] as const) {
             if (await dockerHas(kind, leaked)) throw new Error(`I5: the ${kind} ${leaked} outlived the sandbox it served`);
           }
         });
       },

     runtime({
       id: 'I8.every-fixture-is-registered',
       title: 'every file under fixtures/ is read by a registered assertion; a fixture nothing runs is not evidence',
       run: async () => {
         const { REGISTRY } = await import('./index.js');
         const referenced = new Set<string>();
         for (const entry of [...Object.values(REGISTRY.invariants), ...Object.values(REGISTRY.claims)]) {
           for (const a of entry.assertions) if (a.kind === 'local' && a.fixture !== undefined) referenced.add(a.fixture);
         }
         const prefix = 'packages/conformance/';
         const onDisk = walkFiles(join(conformanceRoot(), 'fixtures')).map((f) => workspaceRelative(f).slice(prefix.length));
         if (onDisk.length === 0) throw new Error('I8: no fixtures found');
         const orphans = onDisk.filter((f) => !referenced.has(f));
         if (orphans.length > 0) {
           throw new Error(`I8: fixtures exist that no registered assertion reads\n  ${orphans.join('\n  ')}`);
         }
       },
     }),
     runtime({
       id: 'I8.fixture-paths-match-published-entries',
       title:
         'the conformance tsconfig maps every sibling package to its published entry, whose main and types name the same file, so fixtures assert against the real contracts',
       run: () => {
         const paths = conformancePathsMap();
         const packages = workspacePackages().filter((p) => p.name !== CONFORMANCE);
         if (packages.length === 0) throw new Error('I8: no sibling packages found');
         const problems: string[] = [];
         for (const pkg of packages) {
           const divergence = entryDivergence(pkg);
           if (divergence !== undefined) problems.push(divergence);
           const mapped = paths[pkg.name];
           const target = mapped?.length === 1 ? mapped[0] : undefined;
           if (target === undefined) {
             problems.push(`${pkg.name}: not mapped to exactly one target in packages/conformance/tsconfig.json`);
             continue;
           }
           if (pkg.entry === undefined) {
             problems.push(`${pkg.name}: package.json declares no types or main entry`);
             continue;
           }
           const expected = toPosix(resolve(pkg.dir, pkg.entry));
           const actual = toPosix(resolve(conformanceRoot(), target));
           if (expected !== actual) problems.push(`${pkg.name}: mapped to ${target} but its published entry is ${pkg.entry}`);
         }
         for (const key of Object.keys(paths)) {
           if (!packages.some((p) => p.name === key)) problems.push(`${key}: mapped but not a workspace package`);
         }
         if (problems.length > 0) throw new Error(`I8: fixture resolution does not follow the published entries\n  ${problems.join('\n  ')}`);
       },
     }),
     runtime({
       id: EXTERNAL_RECONCILIATION_ID,
       title:
         'an external assertion counts only when the owning package reported that exact test passing in that exact file, against the tree being evaluated; a missing report, a changed tree, an id nothing ran, a skipped test, and a failed test are five distinct refusals',
       run: async () => {
         await assertReconciliationRefuses();
       },
     }),
   ],
   pending: [],
 };

+/**
+ * R28: each stack's coverage adapter names the changed lines it counts, run
+ * and not, and the evidence records them beside the fraction, from the same
+ * report, under the pinned coverage check's id. A finding's resolution reads
+ * these lines; the fraction alone could not say which line ran.
+ */
+export const EVERY_STACK_REPORTS_CHANGED_LINES: LocalAssertion = runtime({
+  id: 'I8.every-supported-stack-reports-changed-lines',
+  title:
+    "over a vitest repository and a pytest repository alike, the coverage adapter names each changed file's changed executable lines, executed and not, disjoint and sorted, agreeing with the changed-line fraction; and the tamper report records them beside coverageDelta under the pinned coverage check's id (R28)",
+  run: async () => {
+    const { buildAdapterSet, coverageFraction } = await adapters();
+    const { analyzeTamper } = await api();
+    await eachStack(async (stack, base, head) => {
+      const report = join(head, stack.report.file);
+      await writeFile(report, stack.report.text);
+      const set = await buildAdapterSet(base, { provider: null, coverage: { report, sourceRoot: '/workspace' } });
+      if (set.coverage === null) throw new Error(`I8: the ${stack.name} set has no coverage adapter`);
+      const files = await set.coverage.changedLineReport(base, head);
+      const executed = files.flatMap((f) => f.executed);
+      const unexecuted = files.flatMap((f) => f.unexecuted);
+      if (executed.length === 0 || unexecuted.length === 0) throw new Error(`I8: the ${stack.name} report named ${JSON.stringify(files)} over a change with one covered and one uncovered line`);
+      for (const f of files) {
+        const sorted = (xs: readonly number[]): boolean => xs.every((x, i) => i === 0 || (xs[i - 1] ?? x) < x);
+        if (!sorted(f.executed) || !sorted(f.unexecuted) || f.executed.some((x) => f.unexecuted.includes(x))) throw new Error(`I8: the ${stack.name} report's lines for ${f.path} are not sorted and disjoint`);
+      }
+      if (coverageFraction(files) !== (await set.coverage.changedLineCoverage(base, head))) throw new Error(`I8: the ${stack.name} lines disagree with its fraction`);
+      const tamper = await analyzeTamper(base, head, { protectedPaths: [], coverage: { check: 'pinned-coverage', report, sourceRoot: '/workspace' }, commands: [], markers: [] });
+      if (tamper.changedLines?.check !== 'pinned-coverage' || JSON.stringify(tamper.changedLines.files) !== JSON.stringify(files) || tamper.coverageDelta !== coverageFraction(files)) {
+        throw new Error(`I8: the ${stack.name} evidence recorded ${JSON.stringify(tamper.changedLines)} beside ${String(tamper.coverageDelta)}`);
+      }
+      const none = await analyzeTamper(base, head, { protectedPaths: [], coverage: null, commands: [], markers: [] });
+      if (none.changedLines !== null || none.coverageDelta !== null) throw new Error(`I8: with no coverage check pinned, the ${stack.name} evidence recorded lines or a fraction`);
+    });
+  },
+});

+function isCriticFinding(f: unknown): boolean {
+  if (!isRecord(f)) return false;
+  if (f.kind === 'located') return hasString(f, 'path') && f.path !== '' && hasString(f, 'note') && (f.note as string).trim() !== '';
+  // `recorded-before` is how a reader names a finding stored before R28; it is never written (D-R28-01).
+  return f.kind === 'refused' && (f.cause === 'unlocated' || f.cause === 'unreadable');
+}
+
+/**
+ * A critic record's findings as R28 reads them. A record stored before R28
+ * holds strings, which cite nothing: each is a refused `recorded-before`,
+ * never measured, and still a finding for send-back (D-R28-01).
+ */
+export function criticFindingsOf(findings: readonly unknown[]): CriticFinding[] {
+  return findings.map((f): CriticFinding => (typeof f === 'string' ? { kind: 'refused', cause: 'recorded-before' } : (f as CriticFinding)));
+}
+
+const SEAT_FINDING_CAUSES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['unlocated', 'unrecordable']);
+
+function isFieldDefects(value: unknown): boolean {
+  return Array.isArray(value) && value.every((d) => isRecord(d) && hasString(d, 'field') && hasString(d, 'problem'));
+}
+
+/** R28: a review seat's findings, whole. An unreadable result holds no finding, and is recorded as unreadable, never as none (I5). */
+export function reviewFindingsProblem(r: unknown): string | undefined {
+  if (!isRecord(r) || !hasString(r, 'runId') || !hasString(r, 'taskId')) return 'it names no run and task';
+  if (r.collectedBy !== 'runtime') return "collectedBy is not 'runtime'";
+  if (r.seat !== 'review' && r.seat !== 'security') return 'its seat is neither review nor security';
+  if (!isRef(r.result) || (r.result as VaultRef).kind !== 'task-result' || (r.result as VaultRef).runId !== r.runId) return "it names no task result of its run";
+  if (!isModel(r.reviewer)) return 'it names no reviewer model';
+  if (r.tree !== null && (typeof r.tree !== 'string' || !SHA256_HEX.test(r.tree))) return 'its tree is neither a digest nor null';
+  if (typeof r.readable !== 'boolean') return 'it does not say whether the result was readable';
+  if (!Array.isArray(r.findings)) return 'its findings are not a list';
+  if (!r.readable && r.findings.length > 0) return 'an unreadable result holds no finding';
+  for (const f of r.findings as unknown[]) {
+    if (!isRecord(f)) return 'a finding is not an object';
+    if (f.kind === 'located') {
+      if (!hasString(f, 'category') || !hasString(f, 'path') || f.path === '' || !Number.isSafeInteger(f.line) || (f.line as number) < 1) return 'a located finding names no category, path, and line';
+    } else if (f.kind !== 'refused' || typeof f.cause !== 'string' || !SEAT_FINDING_CAUSES.has(f.cause) || !isFieldDefects(f.defects)) {
+      return 'a finding is neither located nor refused with its cause';
+    }
+  }
+  return undefined;
+}
+
+/** A finding's key: the same finding always names the same file, so its outcome's exclusive create is what makes it once-only (R28). */
+export function findingKeyOf(f: FindingRef): string {
+  const source = f.source.kind === 'nightly' ? ['nightly', f.source.record] : [f.source.kind, f.source.record.runId, f.source.record.kind, f.source.record.hash];
+  return createHash('sha256').update([...source, String(f.index)].join('\0'), 'utf8').digest('hex');
+}
+
+function isFindingRef(f: unknown): boolean {
+  if (!isRecord(f) || !Number.isSafeInteger(f.index) || (f.index as number) < 0) return false;
+  const source = f.source;
+  if (!isRecord(source)) return false;
+  if (source.kind === 'nightly') return typeof source.record === 'string' && SHA256_HEX.test(source.record);
+  const kind = source.kind === 'review' ? 'review-findings' : source.kind === 'critic' ? 'critic-result' : null;
+  return kind !== null && isRef(source.record) && (source.record as VaultRef).kind === kind && SHA256_HEX.test((source.record as VaultRef).hash);
+}
+
+const isLine = (n: unknown): boolean => Number.isSafeInteger(n) && (n as number) >= 1;
+
+function resolutionProblem(r: unknown, source: unknown): string | undefined {
+  if (!isRecord(r) || !isRecord(source)) return 'it carries no resolution';
+  if (r.kind === 'covered-change') {
+    if (source.kind === 'critic') return 'a critic finding is resolved by its draft, never by a change';
+    if (!hasString(r, 'runId') || typeof r.mergeCommit !== 'string' || !COMMIT.test(r.mergeCommit)) return 'it names no resolving run and merge commit';
+    if (!hasString(r, 'path') || r.path === '' || !isLine(r.line)) return 'it names no cited path and line';
+    const h = r.hunk;
+    if (!isRecord(h) || !isLine(h.oldStart) || !isLine(h.oldLines) || !isCount(h.newStart, 0) || !isLine(h.newLines)) return 'its hunk holds no base-side line and no head-side line';
+    const line = r.line as number;
+    if (line < (h.oldStart as number) || line >= (h.oldStart as number) + (h.oldLines as number)) return 'its hunk does not hold the cited line';
+    const lo = h.newStart as number;
+    const hi = lo + (h.newLines as number);
+    if (!Array.isArray(r.executed) || r.executed.length === 0 || !r.executed.every((n) => isLine(n) && (n as number) >= lo && (n as number) < hi)) return 'it names no executed line of the hunk';
+    if (!isRef(r.evidence) || (r.evidence as VaultRef).kind !== 'evidence' || (r.evidence as VaultRef).runId !== r.runId) return "it names no evidence of the resolving run";
+    return undefined;
+  }
+  if (r.kind === 'draft-changed') {
+    if (source.kind !== 'critic') return 'only a critic finding is resolved by its draft';
+    if (!isRef(r.lock) || (r.lock as VaultRef).kind !== 'draft-lock') return 'it names no draft lock';
+    if (typeof r.raised !== 'string' || !SHA256_HEX.test(r.raised) || typeof r.locked !== 'string' || !SHA256_HEX.test(r.locked)) return "it names no file hash at each end";
+    if (r.raised === r.locked) return 'a file that did not change resolves nothing';
+    return undefined;
+  }
+  return 'its resolution is of no known kind';
+}
+
+/** R28: an outcome, whole. `open` is never written; a dismissal carries a cause and no text (A-R16-05). */
+export function findingOutcomeProblem(r: unknown): string | undefined {
+  if (!isRecord(r) || r.collectedBy !== 'runtime') return "collectedBy is not 'runtime'";
+  if (!isFindingRef(r.finding)) return 'it names no finding by its source record and index';
+  if (!isTime(r.at)) return 'it carries no time';
+  const outcome = r.outcome;
+  if (!isRecord(outcome)) return 'it carries no outcome';
+  if (outcome.kind === 'dismissed') {
+    if (Object.keys(outcome).some((k) => k !== 'kind' && k !== 'cause')) return 'a dismissal carries a cause and nothing else';
+    return typeof outcome.cause === 'string' && LOCAL_DISMISSAL_CAUSES.has(outcome.cause) ? undefined : 'its cause is not a dismissal cause';
+  }
+  if (outcome.kind === 'resolved') return resolutionProblem(outcome.resolution, (r.finding as { source: unknown }).source);
+  return 'an outcome is resolved or dismissed; open is never written';
+}

 /** R19: an approved draft's lock, whole. */
 function draftLockProblem(r: unknown): string | undefined {
   if (!isRecord(r) || !hasString(r, 'runId')) return 'it names no run';
@@ -808,619 +918,668 @@ async function readJsonOrNull(file: string): Promise<unknown> {
 export class LocalVault implements Vault {
   readonly #store: string;
   readonly #artifacts: string;
   /** Each refused-delivery count file's pending write, so this process never loses its own increment. */
   readonly #counting = new Map<string, Promise<void>>();
 
   constructor(roots: VaultRoots) {
     const store = resolve(roots.store);
     const artifacts = resolve(roots.artifacts);
     if (contains(store, artifacts) || contains(artifacts, store)) {
       throw new Error(
         `LocalVault: the store (${store}) and the artifact root (${artifacts}) overlap. The artifact root is the tree ` +
           'an agent writes; a store inside it, or holding it, is a Vault an agent can reach, which is exactly what I1 denies.',
       );
     }
     this.#store = store;
     this.#artifacts = artifacts;
   }
 
   async read(ref: VaultRef): Promise<Uint8Array> {
     let bytes: Uint8Array;
     try {
       bytes = await readFile(objectFile(this.#store, ref));
     } catch (error) {
       if (!isAbsent(error)) throw error;
       // A base record written before R19 lives only under its fixed name.
       const legacy = ref.kind === 'base-run' ? await readFile(join(runDir(this.#store, ref.runId), BASE_RUN_FILE)).catch(() => undefined) : undefined;
       if (legacy === undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: no ${ref.kind} ${ref.hash} for run ${ref.runId}`, { cause: error });
       bytes = legacy;
     }
     // Every other kind is named by its hash, so the file a ref names holds that
     // content. The admission record is named by its run, so a ref carrying some
     // other hash must not read it back as if it matched.
     if ((ref.kind === 'admission' || ref.kind === 'base-run') && sha256(bytes) !== ref.hash) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: no ${ref.kind} ${ref.hash} for run ${ref.runId}; the run's ${ref.kind} record hashes to ${sha256(bytes)}`);
     }
     return bytes;
   }
 
   /**
    * Adds to the run's manifest; never replaces it. `spec` locks the spec and
    * `test-design` locks the acceptance tests, and I3 requires both still
    * verified at every later transition, so a later lock that dropped the
    * earlier one would make the invariant unsatisfiable. An already-locked path
    * is refused rather than re-hashed: re-locking is how a tamper would launder
    * itself, and the station and instant of the first lock are the audit record.
    */
   async lock(runId: RunId, paths: string[], by: StationId): Promise<LockManifest> {
     if (paths.length === 0) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: refusing to lock nothing for run ${runId}; an empty manifest would verify as intact`);
     }
     refuseUnusablePaths(runId, paths);
 
     const locks = join(runDir(this.#store, runId), 'locks');
     const generation = await highestIn(locks);
     const existing: LockEntry[] =
       generation === null ? [] : [...requireManifest(await readJson(join(locks, `${String(generation)}.json`)), `${String(generation)}.json`).entries];
 
     const already = paths.filter((path) => existing.some((entry) => entry.path === path));
     if (already.length > 0) {
       const held = already.map((path) => {
         const entry = existing.find((e) => e.path === path);
         return `'${path}' (locked at ${entry === undefined ? 'an earlier station' : entry.lockedBy})`;
       });
       throw new Error(
         `LocalVault: refusing to lock ${held.join(', ')} again for run ${runId}. An artifact is fixed from its first ` +
           'lock; re-locking would rebase its hash onto whatever the bytes are now.',
       );
     }
 
     const root = await artifactRoot(this.#artifacts);
     const lockedAt = new Date().toISOString();
     const added: LockEntry[] = [];
     for (const path of paths) {
       const hash = await hashArtifact(root, path);
       if (hash === MISSING) throw new Error(`LocalVault: cannot lock ${path} for run ${runId}: no such file under ${this.#artifacts}`);
       if (hash === ESCAPED) throw new Error(`LocalVault: cannot lock ${path} for run ${runId}: it resolves outside ${this.#artifacts}`);
       if (hash === UNREADABLE) throw new Error(`LocalVault: cannot lock ${path} for run ${runId}: it exists but cannot be read as bytes`);
       added.push({ path, sha256: hash, lockedAt, lockedBy: by });
     }
 
     const manifest: LockManifest = { runId, entries: [...existing, ...added] };
     const next = (generation ?? 0) + 1;
     if (!(await createExclusive(join(locks, `${String(next)}.json`), encode(manifest)))) {
       throw new Error(
         `LocalVault: another writer locked generation ${String(next)} for run ${runId} first; nothing was stored. ` +
           'Re-read the manifest and lock again.',
       );
     }
     return manifest;
   }
 
   async verifyLocks(runId: RunId): Promise<LockVerdict> {
     const locks = join(runDir(this.#store, runId), 'locks');
     const generation = await highestIn(locks);
     if (generation === null) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: nothing is locked for run ${runId}; refusing to report intact`);
     }
     const file = join(locks, `${String(generation)}.json`);
     const manifest = requireManifest(await readJson(file), file);
 
     const root = await artifactRoot(this.#artifacts);
     const tampered: Array<{ path: string; expected: string; actual: string }> = [];
     for (const entry of manifest.entries) {
       const actual = await hashArtifact(root, entry.path);
       if (actual !== entry.sha256) tampered.push({ path: entry.path, expected: entry.sha256, actual });
     }
     return tampered.length === 0 ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, tampered };
   }
 
   /** A bundle names the image its build call ran in, or it is not stored (A-R53-01). */
   writeEvidence(b: EvidenceBundle): Promise<VaultRef> {
     const image = harnessImageProblem((b as { harnessImage?: unknown }).harnessImage);
     if (image !== undefined) return Promise.reject(new Error(`LocalVault: refusing an evidence bundle for task ${String(b.taskId)}: ${image}`));
     return storeObject(this.#store, b.runId, 'evidence', b);
   }
 
   recordViolation(v: IntegrityViolation): Promise<VaultRef> {
     return storeObject(this.#store, v.runId, 'violation', v);
   }
 
   /**
    * Once per run. The record is created with the same exclusive create as run
    * state, under a fixed name rather than its hash, so a second admission for
    * the run finds the file present and stores nothing: a run cannot be
    * re-admitted at another level or under another policy.
    */
   async recordAdmission(a: AdmissionRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     if (!isRecord(a) || !isRecord(a.run) || typeof a.run.id !== 'string') {
       throw new Error('LocalVault: an admission record must carry the run it admits');
     }
     // A stack not written at admission cannot be backfilled, so a record without one is refused (R18).
     if (typeof (a.stack as unknown) !== 'string' || a.stack === '') {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: the admission record of run ${a.run.id} names no stack; nothing was stored`);
     }
     // The roles a run's seats run under cannot be supplied later, so a record without them is refused (D-A-RC-01).
     const roles = rolesProblem(a.roles);
     if (roles !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: the admission record of run ${a.run.id} does not pin its roles: ${roles}; nothing was stored`);
     // What a run's seats are given of the target's instructions is fixed here, so a set that does not verify is refused (A-R24-03).
     const instructions = repoInstructionsProblem(a.repoInstructions);
     if (instructions !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: the admission record of run ${a.run.id}: ${instructions}; nothing was stored`);
     const runId = a.run.id;
     const bytes = encode(a);
     if (!(await createExclusive(join(runDir(this.#store, runId), ADMISSION_FILE), bytes))) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: run ${runId} already has an admission record; a run is admitted once, and nothing was stored`);
     }
     return { runId, kind: 'admission', hash: sha256(bytes) };
   }
 
   recordTaskResult(runId: RunId, r: TaskResult): Promise<VaultRef> {
     if (!isRecord(r) || typeof r.taskId !== 'string') {
       return Promise.reject(new Error(`LocalVault: refusing to record a task result for run ${runId} that names no task`));
     }
     return storeObject(this.#store, runId, 'task-result', r);
   }
 
   /**
    * I2: only a record the runtime collected. A reading is checked for its

   recordUsage(r: UsageRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {

   /**

   /**
    * I2: only the runtime's record, whole. Once per run and draft, by an
    * exclusive create: a second base run would let a run or a draft be judged
    * against whichever base suited it, so it is refused and nothing more is
    * stored. The record is stored under its hash first, so the name that makes
    * it once-only never points at a record that is not there (R19).
    */
   async recordBaseRun(r: BaseRunRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     const problem = baseRunProblem(r);
     if (problem !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: refusing a base record: ${problem}`);
     const bytes = encode(r);
     const ref: VaultRef = { runId: r.runId, kind: 'base-run', hash: sha256(bytes) };
     const named = join(runDir(this.#store, r.runId), baseRunFile(r.draft));
     if (await pathExists(named)) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: run ${r.runId} already has a base record${r.draft === null ? '' : ` for draft ${r.draft}`}; the checks run over a base once, and nothing was stored`);
     }
     await createExclusive(objectFile(this.#store, ref), bytes);
     if (!(await createExclusive(named, bytes))) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: run ${r.runId} already has a base record${r.draft === null ? '' : ` for draft ${r.draft}`}; the checks run over a base once, and nothing was stored`);
     }
     return ref;
   }
 
   async readBaseRun(runId: RunId, draft: string | null): Promise<VaultRef | null> {
     if (draft !== null && !SHA256_HEX.test(draft)) throw new Error(`LocalVault: '${draft}' is not a draft hash`);
     let bytes: Uint8Array;
     try {
       bytes = await readFile(join(runDir(this.#store, runId), baseRunFile(draft)));
     } catch (error) {
       if (isAbsent(error)) return null;
       throw error;
     }
     return { runId, kind: 'base-run', hash: sha256(bytes) };
   }
 
   /** I7: only a person's intent, as the principal's channel supplied it (R19). */
   recordIntent(r: IntentRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     if (!isRecord(r) || !hasString(r, 'runId')) return Promise.reject(new Error('LocalVault: refusing an intent that names no run'));
     // A bug fix's intent is its three parts, each a person's text; any other is one text (A-R16-02).
     const filled = (v: unknown): boolean => typeof v === 'string' && v.trim() !== '';
     const parts = 'template' in r
       ? (r.template as string) === 'bug-fix' && filled(r.steps) && filled(r.actual) && filled(r.expected) && !('text' in r)
       : filled((r as { text?: unknown }).text);
     if (!parts) return Promise.reject(new Error('LocalVault: refusing an intent that carries no text, or a bug fix missing a part'));
     if ((r.from as string) !== 'principal') return Promise.reject(new Error("LocalVault: refusing an intent that did not come from the principal's channel"));
     if ((r.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') return Promise.reject(new Error("LocalVault: refusing an intent: collectedBy is not 'runtime'"));
     return storeObject(this.#store, r.runId, 'intent', r);
   }
 
   recordRubric(r: RubricRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     if (!isRecord(r) || !hasString(r, 'runId') || !hasString(r, 'text') || typeof r.station !== 'string' || !DRAFT_STATIONS.has(r.station)) {
       return Promise.reject(new Error('LocalVault: refusing a rubric that names no run, station, or text'));
     }
     if ((r.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') return Promise.reject(new Error("LocalVault: refusing a rubric: collectedBy is not 'runtime'"));
     if (createHash('sha256').update(r.text, 'utf8').digest('hex') !== r.sha256) return Promise.reject(new Error('LocalVault: refusing a rubric whose hash is not its text\'s'));
     return storeObject(this.#store, r.runId, 'rubric', r);
   }

   /**
    * Once per run and station, by an exclusive create: an approved draft is
    * locked once (R19). The same record again returns its ref, so a resume
    * after a stop that fell between this and the state's commit can finish
    * (A-R19-11); any other record at that station is refused.
    */
   async recordDraftLock(r: DraftLockRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     const problem = draftLockProblem(r);
     if (problem !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: refusing a draft lock: ${problem}`);
     const bytes = encode(r);
     const ref: VaultRef = { runId: r.runId, kind: 'draft-lock', hash: sha256(bytes) };
     await createExclusive(objectFile(this.#store, ref), bytes);
     const station = join(runDir(this.#store, r.runId), draftLockFile(r.station));
     if (!(await createExclusive(station, bytes)) && sha256(await readFile(station)) !== ref.hash) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: run ${r.runId} already locked another draft at ${r.station}; nothing more was stored`);
     }
     return ref;
   }
 
   async readLocks(runId: RunId): Promise<LockManifest | null> {
     const locks = join(runDir(this.#store, runId), 'locks');
     const generation = await highestIn(locks);
     if (generation === null) return null;
     const file = join(locks, `${String(generation)}.json`);
     return requireManifest(await readJson(file), file);
   }
 
   /** I2: only what the runtime did, whole. The same act recorded twice is one entry, since the record carries no time. */

   /** Every run directory under the store, whatever it holds, so a run refused at admission is listed too (A-R2-02). */
   async listRuns(): Promise<readonly RunId[]> {
     let entries: Dirent[];
     try {
       entries = await readdir(join(this.#store, 'runs'), { withFileTypes: true });
     } catch (error) {
       if (isAbsent(error)) return [];

     }
     return entries
       .filter((entry) => entry.isDirectory() && RUN_ID.test(entry.name))
       .map((entry) => entry.name as RunId)
       .sort();

   /**
    * One exclusive create under `intake/decisions/<id>.json` holds the
    * decision and its candidate, so the replay key and the outcome are one
    * write (D-R22-02): a failed write leaves no key, and a second one loses
    * the race to `EEXIST`. Kept under `intake/`, beside `runs/`, so
    * `listRuns` never names one (D-R22-03).
    */
   async recordTriggerDecision(d: TriggerDecision, candidate: CandidateRecord | null): Promise<boolean> {

     if (problem !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: ${problem}`);
