   }
 
 
   async hasTriggerDecision(source: string, key: string): Promise<boolean> {
     return pathExists(join(this.#store, 'intake', 'decisions', `${intakeIdOf(source, key)}.json`));
   }
   async readTriggerDecisions(): Promise<readonly IntakeEntry[]> {
     let names: string[];
     try {
       names = await readdir(join(this.#store, 'intake', 'decisions'));
     } catch (error) {
       if (isAbsent(error)) return [];
       throw error;
     }
     const entries: IntakeEntry[] = [];
     for (const name of names) {
       const id = /^([0-9a-f]{64})\.json$/.exec(name)?.[1];
       if (id === undefined) continue;
       const entry = await this.#intakeEntry(id as CandidateId);
       if (entry !== null) entries.push(entry);
     }
     return entries.sort((a, b) => (a.decision.source === b.decision.source ? a.decision.key.localeCompare(b.decision.key) : a.decision.source.localeCompare(b.decision.source)));
   }
 
   async readCandidate(id: CandidateId): Promise<IntakeEntry | null> {
     if (!/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/.test(id)) return null;
     const entry = await this.#intakeEntry(id);
     return entry?.candidate == null ? null : entry;
   }
 
   async markCandidateAdmitted(id: CandidateId, runId: RunId): Promise<boolean> {
     if ((await this.readCandidate(id)) === null) throw new Error(`LocalVault: no candidate ${id}`);
     return createExclusive(join(this.#store, 'intake', 'admitted', `${id}.json`), encode({ runId: requireRunId(runId) }));
   }
 
   /** The same exclusive mark an admission takes, so a dismissal and an admission cannot both land (D-R16-06). */
   async dismissCandidate(id: CandidateId, cause: DismissalCause, at: string): Promise<boolean> {
     if ((await this.readCandidate(id)) === null) throw new Error(`LocalVault: no candidate ${id}`);
     if (!LOCAL_DISMISSAL_CAUSES.has(cause)) throw new Error(`LocalVault: '${cause}' is not a dismissal cause`);
     if (Number.isNaN(Date.parse(at))) throw new Error(`LocalVault: '${at}' is not a time`);
     return createExclusive(join(this.#store, 'intake', 'admitted', `${id}.json`), encode({ dismissed: { cause, at } }));
   }
 
   async markCandidateExpired(id: CandidateId, at: string): Promise<void> {
     if ((await this.readCandidate(id)) === null) throw new Error(`LocalVault: no candidate ${id}`);
     // A second call loses the exclusive create and keeps the first time.
     await createExclusive(join(this.#store, 'intake', 'expired', `${id}.json`), encode({ at }));
   }
 
   /**
    * One file per bucket and hour, rewritten under a per-file chain in this
    * process. Two processes counting into one store can lose an increment; a
    * count is a rate for the nightly review, not a control, and a lost one
    * understates junk traffic rather than admitting any (D-R22-03).
    */
   async countRefusedDelivery(bucket: string, hour: string, cause: CountedRefusalCause): Promise<void> {
     if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T\d{2}$/.test(hour)) throw new Error(`LocalVault: '${hour}' is not a UTC hour`);
     const file = join(this.#store, 'intake', 'counts', hour, `${sha256(new TextEncoder().encode(bucket))}.json`);
     const previous = this.#counting.get(file) ?? Promise.resolve();
     const next = previous.then(async () => {
       const read = await readJsonOrNull(file);
       const counts: Record<string, number> = isRecord(read) && isRecord(read.counts) ? { ...(read.counts as Record<string, number>) } : {};
       counts[cause] = (counts[cause] ?? 0) + 1;
       await mkdir(dirname(file), { recursive: true });
       await writeFile(file, encode({ bucket, hour, counts }));
     });
     this.#counting.set(file, next.catch(() => undefined));
     await next;
   }
 
   async readRefusedDeliveryCounts(): Promise<readonly RefusedDeliveryCount[]> {
     const root = join(this.#store, 'intake', 'counts');
     let hours: string[];
     try {
       hours = await readdir(root);
     } catch (error) {
       if (isAbsent(error)) return [];
       throw error;
     }
     const out: RefusedDeliveryCount[] = [];
     for (const hour of hours.sort()) {
       const rows: RefusedDeliveryCount[] = [];
       for (const name of await readdir(join(root, hour))) {
         const read = await readJson(join(root, hour, name));
         if (!isRecord(read) || typeof read.bucket !== 'string' || !isRecord(read.counts)) throw new Error(`LocalVault: ${join(root, hour, name)} is not a refused-delivery count`);
         rows.push({ bucket: read.bucket, hour, counts: read.counts });
       }
       out.push(...rows.sort((a, b) => a.bucket.localeCompare(b.bucket)));
     }
     return out;
   }
 
   /** Under `nightly/<sha256>.json`, beside `runs/` and `intake/`, so `listRuns` never names one (A-R13a-02). */
   async recordNightly(r: NightlyRecord): Promise<string> {
     if (!isRecord(r) || (r.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') throw new Error("LocalVault: a nightly record is collected by the runtime");
     const bytes = encode(r);
     const hash = sha256(bytes);
     await createExclusive(join(this.#store, NIGHTLY_DIR, `${hash}.json`), bytes);
     return hash;
   }
 
   async readNightly(): Promise<readonly NightlyEntry[]> {
     const dir = join(this.#store, NIGHTLY_DIR);
     let names: string[];
     try {
       names = await readdir(dir);
     } catch (error) {
       if (isAbsent(error)) return [];
       throw error;
     }
     const out: NightlyEntry[] = [];
     for (const name of names) {
       const hash = CONTENT_NAME.exec(name)?.[1];
       if (hash === undefined) continue;
       const bytes = await readFile(join(dir, name));
       const record = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as unknown;
       if (!isRecord(record) || record.collectedBy !== 'runtime') throw new Error(`LocalVault: ${join(dir, name)} is not a nightly record`);
       // The hash is the name's, not recomputed: a drifted record is the re-hash's to find, not this reader's to hide.
       // A record stored before R13b has no review, and one before R8a no observation; each is read as none.
       out.push({ record: { review: null, observation: null, ...record } as unknown as NightlyRecord, sha256: hash });
     }
     return out.sort((a, b) => a.record.date.localeCompare(b.record.date) || a.record.schedule.localeCompare(b.record.schedule) || a.sha256.localeCompare(b.sha256));
   }

   /** Under `runs/<id>/observations/<sha256>.json`, or `observations/<sha256>.json` beside the runs (D-R8a-03). */
   async recordObservation(r: ObservationRecord): Promise<string> {
     const problem = observationProblem(r);
     if (problem !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: refusing an observation: ${problem}`);
     const dir = r.runId === null ? join(this.#store, OBSERVATIONS_DIR) : join(runDir(this.#store, r.runId), OBSERVATIONS_DIR);
     const bytes = encode(r);
     const hash = sha256(bytes);
     await createExclusive(join(dir, `${hash}.json`), bytes);
     return hash;
   }
 
   async readObservations(runId: RunId | null): Promise<readonly ObservationEntry[]> {
     const dir = runId === null ? join(this.#store, OBSERVATIONS_DIR) : join(runDir(this.#store, runId), OBSERVATIONS_DIR);
     let names: string[];
     try {
       names = await readdir(dir);
     } catch (error) {
       if (isAbsent(error)) return [];
       throw error;
     }
     const out: ObservationEntry[] = [];
     for (const name of names) {
       const hash = CONTENT_NAME.exec(name)?.[1];
       if (hash === undefined) continue;
       const record = await readJson(join(dir, name));
       // A record that is not an observation is a fault, never a record to skip (I5); a drifted hash is the re-hash's to find.
       const problem = observationProblem(record);
       if (problem !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: ${join(dir, name)} is not an observation: ${problem}`);
       if ((record as ObservationRecord).runId !== runId) throw new Error(`LocalVault: ${join(dir, name)} names another run`);
       out.push({ record: record as ObservationRecord, sha256: hash });
     }
     return out.sort((a, b) => a.record.recordedAt.localeCompare(b.record.recordedAt) || a.sha256.localeCompare(b.sha256));
   }
 
   /**
    * Walks the whole store, sorted, and yields every file in it. A file under
    * `runs/<id>/objects/<kind>/` or `nightly/` is named by its hash; every
    * other is named by a run, a number, or an id. An entry that is neither a
    * file nor a directory is not a record this store writes, and stops the
    * walk rather than being skipped (I5).
    */
   async *listObjects(): AsyncIterable<StoredObject> {
     const store = this.#store;
     async function* walk(dir: string, rel: readonly string[]): AsyncGenerator<StoredObject> {
       let entries: Dirent[];
       try {
         entries = await readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
       } catch (error) {
         if (isAbsent(error) && rel.length === 0) return;
         throw error;
       }
       entries.sort((a, b) => (a.name < b.name ? -1 : a.name > b.name ? 1 : 0));
       for (const entry of entries) {
         const parts = [...rel, entry.name];
         const full = join(dir, entry.name);
         if (entry.isDirectory()) {
           yield* walk(full, parts);
           continue;
         }
         if (!entry.isFile()) throw new Error(`LocalVault: ${parts.join('/')} in the store is neither a file nor a directory`);
         yield { path: parts.join('/'), runId: runOf(parts), named: contentNamed(parts) ? 'content' : 'name', bytes: await readFile(full) };
       }
     }
     yield* walk(store, []);
   }
 
   async #intakeEntry(id: CandidateId): Promise<IntakeEntry | null> {
     const file = join(this.#store, 'intake', 'decisions', `${id}.json`);
     let bytes: Uint8Array;
     try {
       bytes = await readFile(file);
     } catch (error) {
       if (isAbsent(error)) return null;
       throw error;
     }
     const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
     if (!isRecord(parsed) || !isRecord(parsed.decision) || intakeIdOf(String(parsed.decision.source), String(parsed.decision.key)) !== id) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: ${file} is not the trigger decision its name says`);
     }
     const admitted = await readJsonOrNull(join(this.#store, 'intake', 'admitted', `${id}.json`));
     const expired = await readJsonOrNull(join(this.#store, 'intake', 'expired', `${id}.json`));
     const stored = (parsed.candidate ?? null) as (Omit<CandidateRecord, 'schema'> & { schema?: CandidateRecord['schema'] }) | null;
     return {
       decision: parsed.decision as unknown as TriggerDecision,
       // A candidate stored before R13a names no schema: its fields were checked against its kind's (A-R13a-07).
       candidate: stored === null ? null : { ...stored, schema: stored.schema ?? stored.kind },
       sha256: sha256(bytes),
       admittedAs: isRecord(admitted) && typeof admitted.runId === 'string' ? (admitted.runId as RunId) : null,
       expiredAt: isRecord(expired) && typeof expired.at === 'string' ? expired.at : null,
       // The spend mark holds a run or a dismissal, never both (D-R16-06).
       dismissed: isRecord(admitted) && isRecord(admitted.dismissed) && typeof admitted.dismissed.cause === 'string' && typeof admitted.dismissed.at === 'string'
         ? { cause: admitted.dismissed.cause as DismissalCause, at: admitted.dismissed.at }
         : null,
     };
   }

     let names: string[];

       names = await readdir(dir);
     } catch (error) {

     }
     return names

       .filter((hash): hash is string => hash !== undefined)
       .sort()
       .map((hash) => ({ runId, kind, hash }));
   }
 
   async readRunState(runId: RunId): Promise<RunState> {
     const states = join(runDir(this.#store, runId), 'state');
     const version = await highestIn(states);
     if (version === null) throw Object.assign(new Error(`LocalVault: no run state for ${runId}`), { code: 'no-run-state' as const, runId }) satisfies NoRunState;
     const file = join(states, `${String(version)}.json`);
     return requireRunState(await readJson(file), file);
   }
 
   /**
    * Compare-and-swap, arbitrated by the filesystem. The next version's file is
    * created with `wx`, so of any number of writers holding the same
    * `ifVersion` exactly one creates it and the rest are refused with `EEXIST`.
    * There is deliberately no read-then-compare here: that shape loses an
    * update whenever two writers interleave between the read and the write, and
    * it passes a sequential test while doing so.
    *
    * `ifVersion` is authoritative and `s.version` is ignored; the contract left
    * the choice to this unit (D-S1, S1 spec section 2). The precondition below
    * is an extra refusal, not the concurrency control: it rejects a version
    * that never existed, so a caller cannot jump the run forward and leave a
    * hole in its history.
    */
   async commitRunState(s: RunState, ifVersion: string): Promise<RunState> {
     if (!VERSION.test(ifVersion)) {
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: '${ifVersion}' is not a run state version`);
     }
     const states = join(runDir(this.#store, s.runId), 'state');
     const current = Number(ifVersion);
     if (!Number.isSafeInteger(current)) throw new Error(`LocalVault: run state version ${ifVersion} is out of range`);
 
     const held = await highestIn(states);
     if (current === 0 ? held !== null : held === null || held < current) {
       const at = held === null ? 'no committed state' : `version ${String(held)}`;
       throw new Error(`LocalVault: run ${s.runId} has ${at}, so version ${ifVersion} was never current; nothing stored`);
     }
 
     // The history it replaces, read before anything is written: a commit may extend it and never shorten or rewrite it (codex-5).
     const previous = current === 0 ? [] : requireRunState(await readJson(join(states, `${ifVersion}.json`)), `${ifVersion}.json`).stations;
     const problem = stationsProblem(previous, (s as { stations?: unknown }).stations);
     if (problem !== undefined) throw new Error(`LocalVault: refusing run ${s.runId}'s state: ${problem}; nothing stored`);
 
     const next = String(current + 1);
     const stored: RunState = { ...s, version: next };
     if (!(await createExclusive(join(states, `${next}.json`), encode(stored)))) {
       throw new Error(
         `LocalVault: run ${s.runId} has already moved past version ${ifVersion}; another writer committed ${next} first. ` +
           'Nothing was stored. Re-read the state and commit again.',
       );
     }
     return stored;
   }
 }

  */
 
 /** A path the reader can list: the two root files, a rule, or a skill's `SKILL.md`. */

 export class StubVault implements Vault {
   /**

   readonly unsafe: { readonly component: 'StubVault'; readonly cannotEnforce: readonly string[] } = {
     component: 'StubVault',
     cannotEnforce: [
       'persistence: the store is process memory, and everything in it is lost with the process',
       "I1 at the mount layer: nothing prevents a process on the host from reaching this memory; the guarantee is the sandbox's, and the sandbox is also a stub",
     ],

   private readonly locks = new Map<RunId, LockManifest>();

   /** `root` is what locked paths resolve against (S1 finding 4d; P1 decides where the base belongs). */
   constructor(root: string) {
     this.root = root;
   }
 
   read(ref: VaultRef): Promise<Uint8Array> {
     const bytes = this.blobs.get(refKey(ref));
     if (bytes === undefined) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: no ${ref.kind} ${ref.hash} for run ${ref.runId}`));
     return Promise.resolve(new Uint8Array(bytes));
   }
 
   /**
    * Adds to the run's manifest and refuses an already-locked path, as the
    * local Vault does (D-P1-05). The skeleton replaced the manifest instead,
    * which went unnoticed while its line locked once; a line that locks at
    * `spec`, `test-design`, and `plan` would drop the spec from verification at
    * the second lock (D-P4-05).
    */
   async lock(runId: RunId, paths: string[], by: StationId): Promise<LockManifest> {
     if (paths.length === 0) {
       throw new Error(`StubVault: refusing to lock nothing for run ${runId}; an empty manifest would verify as intact`);
     }
     const existing = this.locks.get(runId)?.entries ?? [];
     const already = paths.filter((path) => existing.some((entry) => entry.path === path));
     if (already.length > 0) {
       throw new Error(`StubVault: refusing to lock ${already.join(', ')} again for run ${runId}; an artifact is fixed from its first lock`);
     }
     const lockedAt = new Date().toISOString();
     const entries: LockEntry[] = [...existing];
     for (const path of paths) {
       const hash = await this.hashFile(path);
       if (hash === undefined) throw new Error(`StubVault: cannot lock ${path} for run ${runId}: no such file under ${this.root}`);
       entries.push({ path, sha256: hash, lockedAt, lockedBy: by });
     }
     const manifest: LockManifest = { runId, entries };
     this.locks.set(runId, manifest);
     this.runs.add(runId);
     return structuredClone(manifest);
   }
 
   async verifyLocks(runId: RunId): Promise<LockVerdict> {
     const manifest = this.locks.get(runId);
     if (manifest === undefined) throw new Error(`StubVault: nothing is locked for run ${runId}; refusing to report intact`);
     const tampered: Array<{ path: string; expected: string; actual: string }> = [];
     for (const entry of manifest.entries) {
       const actual = (await this.hashFile(entry.path)) ?? MISSING;
       if (actual !== entry.sha256) tampered.push({ path: entry.path, expected: entry.sha256, actual });
     }
     return tampered.length === 0 ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, tampered };
   }
 
   writeEvidence(b: EvidenceBundle): Promise<VaultRef> {
     // As the filesystem Vault refuses it: a bundle names the image its build call ran in (A-R53-01).
     const image = harnessImageProblem((b as { harnessImage?: unknown }).harnessImage);
     if (image !== undefined) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: refusing an evidence bundle: ${image}`));
     return Promise.resolve(this.store(b.runId, 'evidence', b));
   }
 
   recordViolation(v: IntegrityViolation): Promise<VaultRef> {
     return Promise.resolve(this.store(v.runId, 'violation', v));
   }
 
   recordAdmission(a: AdmissionRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     const runId = a.run.id;
     if (typeof (a.stack as unknown) !== 'string' || a.stack === '') {
       return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: the admission record of run ${runId} names no stack; nothing was stored`));
     }
     // The roles a run's seats run under cannot be supplied later (D-A-RC-01).
     if (!Array.isArray(a.roles)) {
       return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: the admission record of run ${runId} does not pin its roles; nothing was stored`));
     }
     const instructions = repoInstructionsProblem(a.repoInstructions);
     if (instructions !== undefined) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: the admission record of run ${runId}: ${instructions}; nothing was stored`));
     if (this.admitted.has(runId)) {
       return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: run ${runId} already has an admission record; nothing was stored`));
     }
     this.admitted.add(runId);
     return Promise.resolve(this.store(runId, 'admission', a));
   }
 
   recordTaskResult(runId: RunId, r: TaskResult): Promise<VaultRef> {
     return Promise.resolve(this.store(runId, 'task-result', r));
   }
 
   recordUsage(r: UsageRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     if ((r.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') return Promise.reject(new Error("StubVault: a usage record is collected by the runtime, or it is not recorded"));
     // As the filesystem Vault refuses it: timing is a start, and an end or null, never null, {}, or a list (codex-7).
     const timing: unknown = r.timing;
     if (timing === null || typeof timing !== 'object' || Array.isArray(timing) || typeof (timing as { startedAt?: unknown }).startedAt !== 'string' || !('endedAt' in timing)) {
       return Promise.reject(new Error('StubVault: a usage record carries the times the runtime stamped on the call (A-R12-01)'));
     }
     // As the filesystem Vault refuses it: the line never writes a call with no driver (A-R9-01).
     if (typeof r.driver !== 'string' || r.driver === '') return Promise.reject(new Error('StubVault: a usage record names the driver the call was made through (A-R9-01)'));
     // As the filesystem Vault refuses it: a call names the image it ran in, never `not-recorded` (A-R53-01).
     const image = harnessImageProblem((r as { harnessImage?: unknown }).harnessImage);
     if (image !== undefined) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: refusing a usage record: ${image}`));
     const ref = this.store(r.runId, 'usage', r);
     const refs = this.usage.get(r.runId) ?? [];
     if (!refs.some((u) => u.hash === ref.hash)) refs.push(ref);
     this.usage.set(r.runId, refs);
     return Promise.resolve(ref);
   }
 
   readUsage(runId: RunId): Promise<readonly VaultRef[]> {
     return Promise.resolve([...(this.usage.get(runId) ?? [])]);
   }
 
   recordDecision(d: EnforcementDecision): Promise<VaultRef> {
     if ((d.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') return Promise.reject(new Error('StubVault: an enforcement decision is collected by the runtime, or it is not recorded'));
     const ref = this.store(d.runId, 'decision', d);
     const refs = this.decisions.get(d.runId) ?? [];

   recordDraftLock(r: DraftLockRecord): Promise<VaultRef> {
     if ((r.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') return Promise.reject(new Error('StubVault: a draft lock is collected by the runtime, or it is not recorded'));
     const key = `${r.runId}\0${r.station}`;
     // The same record again is the lock already taken, as a resume asks for it (A-R19-11).
     const record = JSON.stringify(r);
     const held = this.draftLocks.get(key);
     if (held !== undefined && held !== record) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: run ${r.runId} already locked another draft at ${r.station}; nothing was stored`));
     this.draftLocks.set(key, record);
     return Promise.resolve(this.store(r.runId, 'draft-lock', r));
   }
 
   readLocks(runId: RunId): Promise<LockManifest | null> {
     const manifest = this.locks.get(runId);
     return Promise.resolve(manifest === undefined ? null : structuredClone(manifest));
   }
 
 
   recordTriggerDecision(d: TriggerDecision, candidate: CandidateRecord | null): Promise<boolean> {
     const problem = intakeProblem(d, candidate);
     if (problem !== undefined) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: ${problem}`));
     const id = intakeId(d.source, d.key);
     if (this.intake.has(id)) return Promise.resolve(false);
     const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify({ decision: d, candidate }));
     this.intake.set(id, { decision: structuredClone(d), candidate: structuredClone(candidate), sha256: sha256(bytes), admittedAs: null, expiredAt: null, dismissed: null });
     return Promise.resolve(true);
   }
 
 
   hasTriggerDecision(source: string, key: string): Promise<boolean> {
     return Promise.resolve(this.intake.has(intakeId(source, key)));
   }
   readTriggerDecisions(): Promise<readonly IntakeEntry[]> {
     return Promise.resolve(

         .map((entry) => structuredClone(entry))
         .sort((a, b) => (a.decision.source === b.decision.source ? a.decision.key.localeCompare(b.decision.key) : a.decision.source.localeCompare(b.decision.source))),
     );
   }
 
   readCandidate(id: CandidateId): Promise<IntakeEntry | null> {
     const entry = this.intake.get(id);
     return Promise.resolve(entry?.candidate == null ? null : structuredClone(entry));
   }
 
   markCandidateAdmitted(id: CandidateId, runId: RunId): Promise<boolean> {
     const entry = this.intake.get(id);
     if (entry?.candidate == null) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: no candidate ${id}`));
     if (entry.admittedAs !== null || entry.dismissed !== null) return Promise.resolve(false);
     this.intake.set(id, { ...entry, admittedAs: runId });
     return Promise.resolve(true);
   }
 
   dismissCandidate(id: CandidateId, cause: DismissalCause, at: string): Promise<boolean> {
     const entry = this.intake.get(id);
     if (entry?.candidate == null) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: no candidate ${id}`));
     if (!DISMISSAL_CAUSES.includes(cause)) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: '${cause}' is not a dismissal cause`));
     if (entry.admittedAs !== null || entry.dismissed !== null) return Promise.resolve(false);
     this.intake.set(id, { ...entry, dismissed: { cause, at } });
     return Promise.resolve(true);
   }
 
   markCandidateExpired(id: CandidateId, at: string): Promise<void> {
     const entry = this.intake.get(id);
     if (entry?.candidate == null) return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: no candidate ${id}`));
     if (entry.expiredAt === null) this.intake.set(id, { ...entry, expiredAt: at });
     return Promise.resolve();
   }
 
   countRefusedDelivery(bucket: string, hour: string, cause: CountedRefusalCause): Promise<void> {
     const key = `${hour}\0${bucket}`;
     const counts = this.refusedCounts.get(key) ?? {};
     this.refusedCounts.set(key, { ...counts, [cause]: (counts[cause] ?? 0) + 1 });
     return Promise.resolve();
   }
 
   recordNightly(r: NightlyRecord): Promise<string> {
     if ((r.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') return Promise.reject(new Error('StubVault: a nightly record is collected by the runtime'));
     const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(r));
     const hash = sha256(bytes);
     if (!this.nightly.has(hash)) this.nightly.set(hash, bytes);
     return Promise.resolve(hash);
   }
 
   recordObservation(r: ObservationRecord): Promise<string> {
     if ((r.collectedBy as string) !== 'runtime') return Promise.reject(new Error('StubVault: an observation is collected by the runtime'));

     const hash = sha256(bytes);
     if (!this.observations.has(hash)) this.observations.set(hash, bytes);
     return Promise.resolve(hash);
   }
 
+  recordFindingOutcome(r: FindingOutcomeRecord): Promise<string | null> {

   readObservations(runId: RunId | null): Promise<readonly ObservationEntry[]> {
     const out = [...this.observations.entries()]
       .map(([hash, bytes]) => ({ record: JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as ObservationRecord, sha256: hash }))
       .filter((e) => e.record.runId === runId);
     return Promise.resolve(out.sort((a, b) => a.record.recordedAt.localeCompare(b.record.recordedAt) || a.sha256.localeCompare(b.sha256)));
   }
 
   readNightly(): Promise<readonly NightlyEntry[]> {
     const out = [...this.nightly.entries()].map(([hash, bytes]) => ({ record: { review: null, observation: null, ...(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as object) } as NightlyRecord, sha256: hash }));
     return Promise.resolve(out.sort((a, b) => a.record.date.localeCompare(b.record.date) || a.record.schedule.localeCompare(b.record.schedule) || a.sha256.localeCompare(b.sha256)));
   }
 
   /** The content-addressed objects and the nightly records. The stub keeps run state, locks, and intake as values, not bytes, so it yields none of them: it is `unsafe`. */
   async *listObjects(): AsyncIterable<StoredObject> {
     await Promise.resolve();
     for (const [key, bytes] of [...this.blobs.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
       const [runId = '', kind = '', hash = ''] = key.split('/');
       yield { path: `runs/${runId}/objects/${kind}/${hash}.json`, runId: runId as RunId, named: 'content', bytes };
     }
     for (const [hash, bytes] of [...this.nightly.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
       yield { path: `nightly/${hash}.json`, runId: null, named: 'content', bytes };
     }
     for (const [hash, bytes] of [...this.observations.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))) {
       const runId = (JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes)) as ObservationRecord).runId;
       yield { path: runId === null ? `observations/${hash}.json` : `runs/${runId}/observations/${hash}.json`, runId, named: 'content', bytes };
     }
   }
 
   readRefusedDeliveryCounts(): Promise<readonly RefusedDeliveryCount[]> {
     return Promise.resolve(
       [...this.refusedCounts.entries()].sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b)).map(([key, counts]) => {
         const [hour = '', bucket = ''] = key.split('\0');
         return { bucket, hour, counts: { ...counts } };
       }),
     );
   }
   listRuns(): Promise<readonly RunId[]> {
     return Promise.resolve([...this.runs].sort());
   }
 
   readRunState(runId: RunId): Promise<RunState> {
     const state = this.states.get(runId);
     if (state === undefined) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`StubVault: no run state for ${runId}`), { code: 'no-run-state' as const, runId }) satisfies NoRunState);
     // As the filesystem Vault reads a state committed before R19: no drafts, and grants that name none;
     // and before R12: no station entries (A-R12-02).
     const read = structuredClone(state) as Omit<RunState, 'drafts' | 'approvals' | 'stations' | 'narrowed'> & { drafts?: RunState['drafts']; stations?: RunState['stations']; narrowed?: RunState['narrowed']; approvals: ReadonlyArray<Omit<RunState['approvals'][number], 'draft'> & { draft?: RunState['approvals'][number]['draft'] }> };
     // Only an absent history is a legacy one; a present one that is not a list is refused, as the filesystem Vault refuses it (codex-7).
     if (read.stations !== undefined && !Array.isArray(read.stations)) {
       return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: run ${runId} holds station entries that are not a list`));
     }
     return Promise.resolve({ ...read, reviews: read.reviews.map((seat) => seatBeforeR9(seat) as RunState['reviews'][number]), drafts: read.drafts ?? {}, stations: read.stations ?? [], narrowed: read.narrowed ?? null, approvals: read.approvals.map((g) => ({ ...g, draft: g.draft ?? null })) });
   }
 
   /**
    * `ifVersion` is authoritative and `s.version` is ignored; the contract
    * leaves which of the two wins to P1 (docs/decisions.md, D-S1).
    */
   commitRunState(s: RunState, ifVersion: string): Promise<RunState> {
     const current = this.states.get(s.runId)?.version ?? '0';
     if (ifVersion !== current) {
       return Promise.reject(new Error(`StubVault: run ${s.runId} is at version ${current}, not ${ifVersion}; nothing stored`));
     }
     const stored = structuredClone({ ...s, version: String(Number(current) + 1) });
     this.states.set(s.runId, stored);
     this.runs.add(s.runId);
     return Promise.resolve(structuredClone(stored));
   }
 
   private store(
     runId: RunId,
-    kind: 'evidence' | 'violation' | 'admission' | 'task-result' | 'usage' | 'decision' | 'integration' | 'base-run' | 'intent' | 'rubric' | 'draft' | 'critic-result' | 'draft-lock',
+    kind: 'evidence' | 'violation' | 'admission' | 'task-result' | 'usage' | 'decision' | 'integration' | 'base-run' | 'intent' | 'rubric' | 'draft' | 'critic-result' | 'draft-lock' | 'review-findings',
     record: EvidenceBundle | IntegrityViolation | AdmissionRecord | TaskResult | UsageRecord | EnforcementDecision | IntegrationRecord | BaseRunRecord
-      | IntentRecord | RubricRecord | DraftRecord | CriticRecord | DraftLockRecord,
+      | IntentRecord | RubricRecord | DraftRecord | CriticRecord | DraftLockRecord | ReviewFindingsRecord,
   ): VaultRef {
     const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(record));
     const ref: VaultRef = { runId, kind, hash: sha256(bytes) };
     this.blobs.set(refKey(ref), bytes);
     this.runs.add(runId);
     return ref;
   }
 
   /** SHA-256 of the file's bytes under root, or undefined when there is no such file. */
   private async hashFile(path: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
     try {
       return sha256(await readFile(resolve(this.root, path)));
     } catch (error) {
       if (isMissingFile(error)) return undefined;
       throw error;
     }
   }
 }

+      readonly executed: readonly number[];
+      readonly evidence: VaultRef;
+    }
+  | { readonly kind: 'draft-changed'; readonly lock: VaultRef; readonly raised: string; readonly locked: string };
+
+/**
+ * What became of a finding (R28). `open` is never recorded: a finding with no
+ * outcome record is open, so an unknown outcome never reads as resolved. A
+ * dismissal is a person's, with a cause and no text (A-R16-05).
+ */
+export type FindingOutcome =
+  | { readonly kind: 'resolved'; readonly resolution: FindingResolution }
+  | { readonly kind: 'dismissed'; readonly cause: DismissalCause };
+
+/** A finding's one outcome, write-once and content-addressed (R28). `at` is the runtime's clock. */
+export interface FindingOutcomeRecord {
+  readonly finding: FindingRef;
+  readonly outcome: FindingOutcome;
+  readonly at: string;
+  readonly collectedBy: 'runtime';
+}
+
+/** An outcome as stored, beside the SHA-256 of its bytes, which is its name. */
+export interface FindingOutcomeEntry {
+  readonly record: FindingOutcomeRecord;
+  readonly sha256: string;
+}
